# gameplay: paragon_3

Stellaris Cygnus v4.5.2 (9776). Extracted 2026-10-12. 1307 entries. Original raw strings retained.

## skrand_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Askrand_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然过去的重担压在他的肩上，斯克兰德从未停止展望未来，而是带着热情和好奇心探索未知的世界。他庄重地宣誓履行自己的承诺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:3`

### English

Although the past weighs on his shoulders, Skrand never loses sight of the future, approaching the unknown with passion and determination. With a solemn vow of service, he undertakes his duty.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:4`

### English

## paragon_2.2105.desc_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_2.2105.desc_01&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经毫无疑问地证明了星系处处充满生命。除了占主导地位的物种和国家，还有许多其他的星球。杰出个体可能会不时涌现，动摇着星系霸权的基础。\n\n如果我们能驾驭他们的雄心壮志，这些著名领袖可能会愿意加入我们的事业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

We have proven beyond any doubt that the galaxy is filled with life. Apart from the dominant species and nations, there are many others who traverse the stars. From time to time, exceptional individuals may arise to shake the foundations of galactic power.\n\nIf we can harness their ambition, these renowned leaders may be willing to join our cause.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:19`

## paragon_2.2105.desc_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_2.2105.desc_02&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经毫无疑问地证明了星系处处充满生命。除了占主导地位的物种和国家，还有许多其他的星球。杰出个体可能会不时涌现，动摇着星系霸权的基础。\n\n尽管这些外星人的处事方式相当怪异甚至令人厌恶，但只要驾驭得了这些§H著名领袖§!的天赋，就可能会有助于我们的事业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:16`

### English

We have proven beyond any doubt that the galaxy is filled with life. Apart from the dominant species and nations, there are many others who traverse the stars. From time to time, exceptional individuals may arise to shake the foundations of galactic power.\n\nAlthough their alien ways may be strange or even repellent, harnessing the talents of such §Hrenowned leaders§! may prove a boon to our cause.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:20`

## sm_recruitment_office_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_recruitment_office_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

语言学家、猎头和探索者，他们通力协作，在所有已知的星系内踏破铁鞋寻找才华卓著者。著名领袖可以在这里招募来为我们的事业服务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:21`

### English

Linguists, headhunters, explorers: they work together to scour the known stars in search of talent. Here, leaders of renown can be recruited to serve our cause.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:25`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_recruitment_office_tooltip&lang=en)

## egalitarian1

### 中文

超然均衡教团的坚忍斗士。通过义体和生物科技进行升级，[Root.GetFirstName]誓要踏碎星系所有的暴政。

### English

## egalitarian2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aegalitarian2&lang=en)

### 中文

一位充满创造力和幽默感的科学家，还有一位友好的小机器人助手，“戈尔基”。[Root.GetHeSheCap]想要分享[Root.GetHisHer]的知识，来改善[Root.GetHisHer]身边人们的生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

A creative and playful scientist. Assisted by Gorky, a small friendly robot, [Root.GetHeSheCap] seeks to share [Root.GetHisHer] knowledge to improve the lives of those around [Root.GetHisHer].

## materialist1

### 中文

一位以合成人之身实现不朽的舰队司令。[Root.GetHisHerCap]的思维直接链接着[Root.GetHisHer]麾下的舰船。

## paragon.40100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_xenophile1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

$NAME_xenophile1$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:73`

## paragon.40100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H面部识别扫描确认该个体为李萨拓·辛格，是一位通缉海盗。他狡黠地笑着，礼貌地点了点头。§!\n\n你好，我是李萨拓船长。我是想来谈合作的。\n\n我指挥着三艘船和一群被遗弃的乌合之众。到目前为止，我们是为……为自己效劳。换句话说，时代变了，我们已经准备好加入一个更大的事业。如果你们赦免我们所谓的罪行并在你们境内帮我们一个安全的避难所，我们将为你们效劳。\n\n你怎么看？要签个合同吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

§HFacial recognition scans identify the individual as Lysator Syng, a wanted pirate. Smiling slyly, he offers a polite nod.§!\n\nGreetings, I am Captain Lysator. I come to you with an offer.\n\nI command three ships and a rag-tag crew of outcasts. Until now, we have worked... independently. That said, times are changing, and we are ready to join a larger enterprise. If you grant us full pardons for our so-called crimes and a safe haven within your borders, we will work for you.\n\nWhat do you say? Should we draw up a contract?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:74`

## paragon.40100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要一个海盗有何用？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

What need do we have for a pirate?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:75`

## paragon.40100.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

好吧，说到这个，我更愿意自称为一名私掠者。有些东西你在学校是学不到的。我的专长在于把握时机：什么时候出击，什么时候撤退，在敌人面前摆什么诱饵等等。哪里薄弱我们就袭击哪里，最后在别人意识到发生了什么之前就带着战利品逃走。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:68`

### English

Well, as to that, I prefer to think of myself as a privateer. Some things you just can't learn in an academy. My skills lie in finding opportunities: when to strike, when to retreat, what bait to dangle in front of a foe. We hit hard and where it hurts, and make off with the loot before anyone realizes what happened.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:76`

## paragon.40100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你们为什么要加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

Why do you wish to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:77`

## paragon.40100.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我的船员已经过腻了奔波的日子，想要寻找一处安全的港湾。你的帝国看起足够开放，能允许一群被驱逐的人加入其中。海盗生涯让我们也付出了很多代价，但我们还没打算安定下来，而你这样厉害的赞助人肯定能减轻我们的负担。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

My crew is tired of living a life on the run. We seek a safe harbor. Your empire seems open enough to allow a collection of outcasts into your ranks. Freebooting has cost us much; we're not exactly looking to settle down, but a patron of your calibre could take a certain load off.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:78`

## paragon.40100.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们凭什么相信你们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

Why should we trust you?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:79`

## paragon.40100.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

不凭什么，就凭我一介宵小之辈的话。如果这还不够，那就再见吧。我就是来提一笔买卖罢了，没指望别的什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

No reason, other than the word of a scoundrel. If that's not enough, fare thee well. I propose a deal, nothing more.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:80`

## paragon.40100.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.d&lang=en)

### 中文

说说你的船员们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

Tell us about your crew.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:81`

## paragon.40100.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

逃跑的奴隶、赏金猎人、贪财的雇佣兵……还有一位怪厨子。我们来自星系的五湖四海，在自由和冒险的召唤下走到一起来了。在我去的日子里我们几乎成了一家人。只要听从命令并且不吃掉其他的船员，谁想来加入我都欢迎。我很荣幸能统领这一帮人才。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

Escaped slaves, bounty hunters, soldiers of fortune... Even the odd chef. From all corners of the galaxy they answered the call of liberty and adventure. Over the years we've grown into a family of sorts. I welcome anyone, as long as they follow my orders and don't try to eat any of the others. I consider it an honor to lead them.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:82`

## paragon.40100.hire_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40100.hire_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_xenophile1$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。\n3艘海盗船被添加到我们的帝国中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

§Y$NAME_xenophile1$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.\n3 pirate ships are added to our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:83`

## paragon.40200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_xenophile2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

$NAME_xenophile2$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:85`

## paragon.40200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H女人带来了惊人的礼物：一块妥善保管的彗星碎片，一块缇扬奇骨雕，和一只装在罐子里的微小软体动物。§!\n\n你好，我叫诺娜，是科基尔家族的永恒受祝者。我以这些礼物来表达我的尊敬之情。我探索了许多星球，反复学习了许多课程。我愿在你的祝福下学习你们的文化，作为回报，我会向你分享数百个文明的智慧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

§HThe woman bears fascinating gifts: a preserved shard from a comet, a Tiyanki bone carving, and a tiny molluscoid in a jar.§!\n\nGreetings, I am Nona, the Eternally Blessed of the House Colzyrion. I offer you these gifts as a token of esteem. I have explored many worlds, and relearned countless lessons. With your blessing, I would like to study your culture, and in return, share the wisdom of a hundred civilizations.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:86`

## paragon.40200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你能为我们做什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

What can you do for us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:87`

## paragon.40200.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我的特长在于外星学和外交。知识、财富和谈话本身就是我能给予的。毕竟人们除此之外还有什么它求吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

My expertise lies in both xenology and diplomacy. Knowledge, credits, and conversation are what I offer. What more could one ask for?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:88`

## paragon.40200.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么要加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

Why do you want to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:89`

## paragon.40200.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我觉得你们的坦率和诚实很吸引人。你和我一样看到了合作中蕴含的可能性。能和这样开放的灵魂生活在一起是一种荣幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

You have an openness and honesty that I find appealing. Like me, you see the possibilities in collaboration. It will be a privilege to live among such open souls.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:90`

## paragon.40200.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你的文化特长是怎么来的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

How did you come by your cultural expertise?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:91`

## paragon.40200.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

理解一个外星文化的最好方式就是拥抱它。我初沐爱河是在一颗外星球上，他们的文化深深吸引了我。尽管我们已经不在一起了，但那段感情教会了我宽容和尊重的意义。从那以后，我尝试着在我所访问的地方尽可能多地学习，不再受各种偏见的拖累。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

The best way to understand a foreign culture is to embrace it. I discovered love for the first time on an alien world. I was fascinated by my partner and their culture, and even though we're no longer together, that relationship taught the meaning of tolerance and respect. Since then, I have tried to assimilate as much as I can in the places I visit, free from the burden of judgment.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:92`

## paragon.40200.hire_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40200.hire_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_xenophile2$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

§Y$NAME_xenophile2$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:93`

## paragon.40300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_militarist1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

$NAME_militarist1$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:95`

## paragon.40300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H男爵身材魁梧。他身上的一切都散发着严格的军旅气息：靴子一尘不染，制服熨得整整齐齐，他的眼神机警而富于计谋。但在严格的外表之外，还有别的东西：他的脸上写满了哀伤。§!\n\n我可以作为一个舰队司令和战术家为您服务。\n\n接受我的效忠，我就会粉碎那些可能伤害到你的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

§HThe Baron is of imposing stature. Everything about him radiates rigid military discipline. His boots are spotless, his uniform neatly pressed, and his eyes are watchful and calculating. But beyond the strict facade, there is something else: his face is a map of grief.§!\n\nI come to offer my services as an admiral and tactician.\n\nAccept my pledge, and I will crush those who would do you harm.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:96`

## paragon.40300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你说你是一位舰队司令，你的舰队呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

If you are an admiral, where is your fleet?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:97`

## paragon.40300.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

羞愧之至，它不复存在了。\n\n我们当时围绕着一颗红矮星进行着紧急维修，而我们的敌人在这时候暗算了我们。他们从四面八方包围而来。\n\n我们以一敌十，在那血色般的恒星光芒中，我仿佛见到了我的舰队和船员们的末日。他们作为施舍饶了我一命。时至今日我还无法洗刷那场失败的耻辱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

To my shame, it is lost.\n\nWe were orbiting a red star, making vital repairs, when our enemies ambushed us. They came from all directions.\n\nOutnumbered ten to one, I watched as my ships and their crews met their end around that hellish sun. As a parting 'gift', I was allowed to live. Even now, I carry the shame of that defeat.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:98`

## paragon.40300.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么要加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Why do you wish to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:99`

## paragon.40300.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你明白，和平是一件以战争作为价码的奢侈品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

You understand that peace is a luxury bought by war.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:100`

## paragon.40300.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你不应该去侍奉你的亲族吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

Should you not serve your noble House?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:101`

## paragon.40300.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40300.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

夏里登家族和我一同衰败了。我战败后，复仇的渴望蒙蔽了我的双眼。相信我，我的仇敌得到了应有下场。尽管我已经荣誉尽失，但我的技艺将常伴我身。为此我愿以生命起誓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:92`

### English

House Jariden fell with me. After my defeat, I was consumed by a thirst for vengeance. Believe me, my enemies paid for what they did. My honor may have been taken, but not my skill in battle. On this I pledge my life.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:102`

## paragon.40400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H一队奴隶抬着一乘巨大的轿子，上面坐着一只可畏惧的爬行类。所有关于军阀的刻板印象都可以在她这里找到：大片骇人的伤疤，张扬跋扈的武器，作为杀戮战利品的骨架等等。§!\n\n我叫基雅·祖蒙，这就是我开出的条件！\n\n§H她从坐席上起身，随从徐徐跪服在她的身侧。§!\n\n我将率领你的军队，击溃你的敌人。他们的星球将化为焦土，他们的财宝将为你所得。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

§HA litter of enormous proportions is carried by a procession of slaves. Atop it sits a formidable reptilian. All the hallmarks of a warlord are there: extensive scarring, brazen weaponry, the skeletal remnants of past kills.§!\n\nI am Gia'Zumon, and these are my terms!\n\n§HAs she removes herself from the throne, the members of her entourage prostrate themselves before her.§!\n\nI will lead your armies. I will break your enemies. Their worlds shall burn and their riches will be yours.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:106`

## paragon.40400.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40400.a&lang=en)

### 中文

等会儿。你怎么会想加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Hold on. Why do you want to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:107`

## paragon.40400.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40400.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我洗劫过无数的星球。这活儿倒是轻松，只是我已经腻了！那些个废物在官场、艺术和无聊的废话中浪费那么多时间。\n\n§H她发出野性的嘶声，露出她锯齿状的牙齿。§!\n\n但你的帝国很强大，你的敌人也是块美味的硬骨头！让我来嚼碎它们吧。我渴望像样的战斗！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

I have sacked many worlds. And it was easy. And it was boring! The weak waste their time on bureaucracy, art, and senseless blather.\n\n§HShe lets out a feral hiss and bares her jagged teeth.§!\n\nYour empire is mighty and your foes are worthy! Let me destroy them. Give me a proper fight!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:108`

## paragon.40400.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40400.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你的军队呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Where is your army?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:109`

## paragon.40400.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40400.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

呵！那可真是值得铭记的一天！两年前，我们入侵了一个遥远的星球。我们消灭了一切胆敢防抗的人，夺取了一切值钱的东西，把有价值的人收作奴隶。然后援军来了。\n\n§H她摸着腰带上的一颗头骨，露出利齿狡黠地笑着。§!\n\n那是一场血腥的屠杀，一曲残暴的赞歌。最后他们打败了我们，但我的战士们死得其所。我来，为的不是荣华富贵，我想要战斗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

Ha! Now, that was a day to remember! Two years ago, we invaded a remote world. We annihilated all who opposed us, seizing everything of value and taking the worthy as slaves. Then the reinforcements arrived.\n\n§HShe touches one of the trophies hanging from her belt. She bares her teeth, grinning.§!\n\nSuch magnificent carnage. An ode to brutality. In the end they crushed us, but my warriors died well. I come to you now not for riches, or glory. I come seeking a challenge.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:110`

## paragon.40400.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40400.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你看起来有点……上头了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

You seem a little... Overzealous.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:111`

## paragon.40400.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40400.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

战争是一种运动，是一种必需品，是一种工具。但是战斗……啊，它能说道的太多太多了。这是检测一个人极限最纯粹的方式。谁能不为这样的前途感到踌躇满志呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

War is a sport, a necessity, a tool. But the fight... Ah, now that reveals much. There is no purer way to test one's limits. Who would not be invigorated at the prospect?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:112`

## paragon.40500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位外星人被包裹在一个咯咯作响的生命维持装置中。浓稠的液体从管子和通风口流过，他的话语变成了一种潮湿的汩汩声。§!\n\n你们的工厂太差劲了吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

§HThe alien is encased in a gurgling life-support unit. Thick fluid sloshes through the tubes and vents, causing his speech to emerge in a wet gurgle.§!\n\nYour industrial plants are a disgrace.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:116`

## paragon.40500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你什么意思。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Explain yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:117`

## paragon.40500.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40500.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我的特长能够帮上你。让我告诉你什么叫效率。\n\n§H外星人的触手湿哒哒地在头盔玻璃上扭动着。§!\n\n强者壮大，弱者灭亡。星系从不缺少工人，我会给你带过来一些。他们要么工作，要么去死。利润则会滚滚而来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

You could benefit from my expertise. Let me show you the meaning of efficiency.\n\n§HThe alien's tentacles twitch wetly against the helmet glass.§!\n\nReward the strong. Cull the weak. The galaxy has no shortage of workers. I will bring them to you. They will work, or they will die. And the profits will flow.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:118`

## paragon.40500.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40500.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么选我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

Why us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:119`

## paragon.40500.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40500.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你们足够纯粹，不和外人搞在一起。\n\n§H随着可怕的喷溅声，液体充入了头盔。这个外星人挥舞着触手，表现出某种欣快感。§!\n\n因为我们有一样的价值观。因为我也一样独善其身。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

You do not mingle with outsiders.\n\n§HWith a hideous sloshing sound, the helmet is flooded with liquid. Tentacles waving, the alien projects a certain euphoria.§!\n\nBecause I share your values. Because I keep myself apart.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:120`

## paragon.40500.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40500.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是你穿着隔离服的原因？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

Is that why you wear an environmental suit?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:121`

## paragon.40500.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40500.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

正是。\n\n§H一阵狂暴的吸气声。§!\n\n在这里面，没有人能碰到我。在这里面我才感受得到洁净。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Just so.\n\n§HA gurling intake of breath.§!\n\nIn here, nothing can touch me. In here, I am clean.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:122`

## paragon.40600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H我们的传感器显示该机器人的外壳已经有数百年历史。它的语音缺乏音调，但话语却意外地洪亮。§!\n\n数据显示你的政府和附属国之间不协调。缺乏纪律。不合格的教化。建议操作：向系统中添加亚图里昂。删除不必要的功能。强调秩序。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

§HOur scans reveal that the outer shell of the robot is hundreds of years old. Its voice is monotone, but its words are oddly resonant.§!\n\nData indicates misalignment between your government and its subjects. Lack of discipline. Substandard indoctrination. Recommended action: addition of Aturion to the system. Eliminate unnecessary functions. Prioritize order.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:126`

## paragon.40600.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40600.a&lang=en)

你为什么而来？

### English

Why are you here?

## paragon.40600.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40600.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

每个社会都是一个方程。请让亚图里昂来帮你解它。方程里不需要不确定项。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Every society is an equation. Allow Aturion to solve yours. There is no need for uncertainty.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:128`

## paragon.40600.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40600.b&lang=en)

## paragon.40600.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40600.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的文明接近理想状态。可以在最短的时间内达到完美的平衡。据亚图里昂计算，成功的可能性为91.1%。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

Your civilization approaches ideal conditions. Perfect balance can be attained in the shortest period of time. Aturion has calculated the likelihood of success at 91.1%. 

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:130`

## paragon.40600.d.response

### 中文

§H从机器人胸口的一个端口投射出几十个全息图。它开始了单调乏味的解释，包括统计分析、经济学、心理分析、基因操纵……其中一个全息图是进度条。它表示演示将再持续127.8个标准日。§!

It indicates that the presentation will last another 127.8 standard days.§!

$NAME_egalitarian1$

### English

## paragon.40700.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40700.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位武士装备着先进的武器和装甲。扫描显示其体内有高度的生化改造和义体升级。§!\n\n基恩将军听您吩咐。我是超然均衡教团的一位特使。我被派遣来帮助所有需要消除暴政的人。接纳我的誓言，我的武力将为你所用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

§HThe warrior is armed with advanced weaponry and armor. Scans reveal extensive biological modifications and cybernetic enhancements.§!\n\nGeneral Jynn, at your service. I am an envoy of the Numinous Equilibrium. I have been sent to defend those in need and help bring about the end of tyranny. Accept my oath, and I shall put my arms at your service.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:138`

## paragon.40700.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40700.a&lang=en)

### 中文

聊聊你自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

Tell us more about yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:139`

## paragon.40700.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40700.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我是一名为理想而战的士兵。我们教团致力于消除压迫、解放奴隶。我们愿为此抛头颅，洒热血。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

I am a soldier in service to an ideal. My order works to end oppression and free the enslaved. For this we spill our blood.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:140`

## paragon.40700.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40700.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么要加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:127`

### English

Why do you wish to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:141`

## paragon.40700.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40700.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你们是一个崇尚平等的文明，所有的个体都能凭借自己的努力而成就伟大。这还不够吸引我吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:128`

### English

Yours is a civilization that celebrates equality, where any individual may rise to greatness on their own merit. Is this not reason enough?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:142`

## paragon.40700.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40700.c&lang=en)

### 中文

再多说说你的教团。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:129`

### English

Tell us more about your order.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:143`

## paragon.40700.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40700.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

超然均衡教的创始人伊斯查顿宣扬平等的教义。自由是美丽而脆弱的。权力不应属于某一只手——它是一种必须共同承担的负担。我的教团成员发誓要维护这些价值观。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

The founder of the Numinous Equilibrium, Yschaton, preached a doctrine of equality. Freedom is as fragile as it is beautiful. Power was not meant for a single hand - it is a burden that must be shared. Members of my order vow to uphold these values.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:144`

## paragon.40800.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位科学家全神贯注地看着手中的输入板，手指对着按键上下纷飞。她哼着欢快的旋律，似乎对工作心无旁骛。一个小型机器人同伴漂浮在她旁边的空中，它发出叽叽喳喳的声音，磕了一下她的肩膀。她倏然抬起头。§!\n\n啊，对不起。你好！我是佐西拉。很抱歉打扰你们，那个，我之前的实验室相当于是，爆炸了。\n\n§H小机器人又叽叽喳喳了起来。§!\n\n啊所以，那个，我是说，我想要找一个地方能让我继续作曲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

§HThe scientist is focused on a tablet in her hand. As her fingers dance over the keys, she hums a cheerful melody. She seems lost in her work and oblivious to the world. A small robot companion floats in the air next to her; it chirps and bumps her shoulder. She looks up.§!\n\nOh, sorry. Hello, I'm Zosira. Sorry to bother you, but my last lab, well, it sort of blew up.\n\n§HThe small robot chirps again.§!\n\nAh, yes, what I am trying to say is that I'm looking for a place to compose.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:148`

## paragon.40800.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.a&lang=en)

### 中文

找地方……作曲？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

A place to... compose?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:149`

## paragon.40800.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

是啊，宇宙就像音乐一样。环绕着的气态巨行星的振动，新生恒星的咆哮，盘旋原子的甜美歌谣……\n\n§H她像是在指挥一支看不见的宇宙交响乐队一样挥舞着双手。§!\n\n哒~哒~啦~噔~，加上合适的节奏，然后：当当！于是我们就创造了魔法！啊不是，科学。差不多一回事，真的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

The universe is music. The vibrations from the orbiting gas giants, the roar of a newborn star, the sweet song of swirling atoms...\n\n§HShe gestures with her hands as if she were conducting an invisible cosmic orchestra.§!\n\nDa da da dum, then add a fitting rhythm and: tadaa! We have created magic! Or, well, science. The same thing, really.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:150`

## paragon.40800.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么要加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Why do you seek to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:151`

## paragon.40800.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的想法看起来相当合意。我希望自己的发现能造福每个人，而不只是权贵子弟。这支宇宙的交响乐里可包含了奇迹和魔法！每个人都应该听到！\n\n§H小机器人继续叽叽喳喳。§!\n\n就是这样戈尔基！你说得一点儿也没错。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

Your head seems to be in the right place. I want my discoveries to benefit everyone, not just the rich and powerful. The symphony of the universe, the wonders, the magic! Everyone deserves to hear it!\n\n§HThe small robot chirps.§!\n\nThat's right Gorky! Very well said.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:152`

## paragon.40800.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这个小机器人是谁？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Who is that little robot?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:153`

## paragon.40800.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

欧，这是戈尔基！他是不是超可爱的？他是我自己造的，我做什么他都帮得上忙。\n\n§H小机器人开心地叽叽喳喳。§!\n\n好呀，好呀，我会告诉他们的。戈尔基为我安排会见、吃饭、饮水和睡眠的时间。你知道，我要是为一件事着迷的时候，就……咻——地会忘记时间。\n\n§H小机器人继续叽叽喳喳。§!\n\n哈哈对！就是就是！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

Oh, this is Gorky! Isn't he adorable? I built him myself, and he helps me with everything.\n\n§HThe robot chirps happily.§!\n\nOkay, okay, I'll tell them. Gorky makes sure that I make my meetings, eat, drink and sleep. You know, when I get passionate about something, it's just... swoosh! I lose all track of time.\n\n§HGorky chirps again.§!\n\nHa ha, yes! Exactly!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:154`

## paragon.40800.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你说你的实验室炸了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

You say your lab blew up?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:155`

## paragon.40800.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40800.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

哦，哎呀，怎么说呢？！你瞧，我的家乡在关于谁能当科学家这件事可是相当苛刻。所以，我从老旧工业区里搜刮破烂，悄悄组建了自己的实验室。我本以为离子化力场可以让易爆微粒稳定下来，但是……\n\n§H小机器人一边叽叽喳喳一边发出滴滴声。§!\n\n对吧？那些微粒的重低音可真是太劲爆了。所以我什么时候可以开始？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

Oh, wow, where to start?! You see, my homeworld is very restrictive when it comes to who is allowed to be a scientist. So, I put together the lab in secret using whatever I could scavenge from the old industrial zones. Now, I thought the ionization field would keep the volatile motes stable, but...\n\n§HThe small robot chirps and beeps.§!\n\nRight? The bass from those motes was truly booming. So when do I start?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:156`

## paragon.40900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H在一架巨大的移动吊臂上悬着一副类人生物的头和躯干。这一定曾经是一个有机生物，但现在大部分已经由金属、电缆和塑料构成。毫无预兆下，那副躯干振动了一下活动了起来，两只合成眼发出光芒，发出嘎吱作响的嗓音。§!\n\n我是松达尔。我在寻找能效力的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

§HSuspended on a massive mobility crane is the head and torso of a humanoid. Once this must have been an organic being, but most of what remains is metal, wires and plastic. Without warning, the body jolts and comes to life, two synthetic eyes begin to glow, and a crackling voice emerges.§!\n\nI am Xondar. I seek a command.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:160`

## paragon.40900.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你……你出过什么事吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

What... happened to you?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:161`

## paragon.40900.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我曾经由血肉组成。像其他同类一样，我会受到疾病和年龄的侵蚀。血肉苦弱，因此我放弃了它。我把自己的意识被数字化并上载，成功地从囚禁我的躯体中刑满释放了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

I was once flesh and blood. Like the rest of my people, I was vulnerable to age and disease. My flesh was weak, so I shed it. My consciousness was digitized and uploaded. I have broken free of the body that held me prisoner.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:162`

## paragon.40900.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么要加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Why do you wish to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:163`

## paragon.40900.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

迷信、宗教信仰……都是毫无根据的谎言，最后不过是蛊惑人心化为屠戮的热血。那是无数战争和无尽苦难的根源。你和你的人看穿了这些无稽之谈。看得见，尝得到，摸得着的才叫真实。就像要达到完美必须抛弃肉体一样，幼稚的幻想也必须抛弃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

Superstition, religion... Baseless lies, useful only to inflame the heart to murderous passion. The cause of countless wars and endless suffering. You and your people see through such nonsense. What is real can be seen, tasted, touched. Just as the flesh must be discarded to reach perfection, childish fantasies must be cast aside.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:164`

## paragon.40900.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你是想指挥一支舰队吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

You seek to command a fleet?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:165`

## paragon.40900.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你所见到的只是我的一小部分。虚空才是我的归宿，而我只是我舰队的一员。我的意识正扎根在舰船的系统中，传感器就是我的耳目，船壳就是我的肌肤。只要有我的执掌，你的舰队将达到无可比拟的强大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

The form you see before you is but one part of me. The void is my home, and I am one with my ships. My consciousness is ingrained in their very systems; the sensors are my eyes and ears, the hull is my skin. With me at their head, I can direct your fleets to an unprecedented level of excellence.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:166`

## paragon.40900.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.d&lang=en)

### 中文

说说你怎么变成这样的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

Tell us about your transformation?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:167`

## paragon.40900.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40900.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

还有什么可介绍的吗？我的身体生病了。我获得了一个保存意识的机会，然后我就抓住了它。许多其他人也常识这么做，但没有一个活到了现在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:152`

### English

What is left to say? My body fell ill. I was offered a chance to save my mind, and I leapt at it. Many others tried the same. None survive today.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:168`

## paragon.41000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H粗短结实的机械装置被挤压在一具外骨骼中。工具、零零散散的零件和油腻腻的手都能看出他是个修补匠，他的眼睛里闪烁着好奇和热情。§!\n\n早啊老板！我叫博林，诺嘉之子。很高兴认识你！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

§HThe short and sturdy mechanic is squeezed into an exoskeleton. Tools, scattered parts and greasy hands bear the telltale marks of a tinkerer, and his eyes glint with curiosity and enthusiasm.§!\n\nMornin' boss! I'm Borin, son of Noga. Pleased to make your acquaintance!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:172`

## paragon.41000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那么你能为我们做什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

So, what can you do for us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:173`

## paragon.41000.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我是一个有三十年经验的打捞者。这简历就已经够牛逼了吧。你要是有什么东西要建造或者修理，\n\n§H他用大拇指戳了戳自己的胸口。§!\n\n我都能搞定。我可不是吹牛，我真的什么东西都能修。而且不只有我一个，我还有一支可以拆解、切割、焊接、收集、修理和组装任何东西的订制机器团队。只要你想的话，给我一队人，我就能服务你整支海军。当然，人手越多越好哇！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Been a salvager for thirty years. I figure that'll do for a resume. You need things built? Something repaired?\n\n§HHe points his thumb at his chest.§!\n\nI can do it. Not to boast or anything, but I've fixed everything there is to be fixed. And not just me - I've got a team of custom bots to dismantle, cut, weld, gather, repair and reassemble whatever you need. Gimme a crew and I can service your whole navy if you want. The bigger the better as far as I'm concerned.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:174`

## paragon.41000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么为我们工作？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

Why work for us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:175`

## paragon.41000.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我就是满世界跑，为自己谋个出路。你看起来是一个会重视劳动的老板、一位靠谱的领导。你能看清这个世界的本来面目，利用我们所拥有的东西来建设更美好的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

I'm just trying to make my way in the galaxy. You seem like an employer that values hard work and a steady hand. Someone who sees the world for what it is, taking the things we have and using them to build a better future.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:176`

## paragon.41000.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.c&lang=en)

### 中文

那你为什么从之前的单位离职了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Why did you leave your previous job?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:177`

## paragon.41000.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你问真的吗？我腻了。从我记事起，我就梦想在虚空中遨游。我想逼一下自己看看能走多远。所以我就收拾好工具，然后就是现在这样咯。现在，还有别的问题吗？要不我们开建吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

Honestly? I got bored. Ever since I can remember I've dreamed of cruising the void. I want to push myself, see how far I can go. So I packed my tools and here I am. Now, unless you have more questions, what do you say we get building?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:178`

## paragon.41000.hire_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41000.hire_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_materialist2$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

§Y$NAME_materialist2$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:179`

## paragon.41100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_pacifist1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

$NAME_pacifist1$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:181`

## paragon.41100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位外星人身着华丽和繁复的长袍。尽管年事已高，但他的双眼仍旧警觉，透出敏锐的智慧。§!\n\n向您致敬。我想作为一个和平卫士为您服务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

§HThe alien is adorned in rich, intricate robes. Despite his obvious age, his eyes are alert and betray a keen intellect.§!\n\nSalutations. I come to offer my services as a keeper of peace.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:182`

## paragon.41100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

聊聊你自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

Tell us more about yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:183`

## paragon.41100.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我是我族人中少数被选中的大法官之一。这是一个非常光荣的职位。我学习了许多星球的法律，通过长期的经验，我还学会了如何平衡正义的天平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

I am one of the select few anointed as a Judge among my people. This is a position of great honor. I have memorized the laws of many worlds, and through long experience I have learned how to balance the scales of justice.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:184`

## paragon.41100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么想与我们共事？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

Why do you wish to work with us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:185`

## paragon.41100.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我老了。我在办公室里忙碌了大半生。尽管对我这样的人来说为年轻人让位是再自然不过的，但我还不满足。犯罪是无国界的，我还有很多能做的事。这事众所周知，星系中有很多人来找过我，想让我为他们工作。但为那些用错权力的人工作有什么意义呢？我相信我们是一路人，能为你的人民服务是我的无上荣幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

I am old. I have served my time in office. Although it is the custom of my kind to give way to the young, I am not finished. Crime knows no border, and I still have much to offer. This is known, and many in the galaxy have approached me with offers of employment. But what sense is there in working for those who misuse their authority? I believe that our values align on this, and it would be my privilege to serve your people.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:186`

## paragon.41100.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.c&lang=en)

### 中文

是什么让你选择了法律？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

What led you to the law?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:187`

## paragon.41100.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41100.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙就是一位无情的法官。\n\n§H这位外星老人的咽喉深处发出了可怕暗哑的声音。§!\n\n作为有情感的生物，我们有责任做得更好。只有理性才能诞生出公平。\n\n§H他的目光停留在不远处。在那一瞬间他仿佛忘记了自己的存在。§!\n\n法律的力量必须谨慎使用，不能让它成为不公正的根源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

The universe is a merciless judge.\n\n§HFrom deep in the old alien's throat emerges an eerie clicking sound.§!\n\nAs sentient beings, it is up to us to do better. It is through reason alone that fairness is born.\n\n§HHis gaze rests in the middle distance. For a brief moment, he seems to forget himself.§!\n\nThe power of the law must be used with caution, lest it become its own injustice.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:188`

## paragon.41100.hire_custom_tooltip

§Y$NAME_pacifist1$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

### English

§Y$NAME_pacifist1$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

$NAME_pacifist2$

### English

$NAME_pacifist2$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:191`

## paragon.41200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位女士的服饰和珠宝让人对其生活的奢华窥见一斑。她轻轻一鞠躬后开口说道，§!\n\n我愿意作为一位总督和一位外交官而效劳。让我成为你的盟友，我能让你在星系的任何一个角落都找到强大的友邦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

§HThe Sister's clothing and jewelry offer a glimpse into her opulent lifestyle. She bows before speaking.§!\n\nI offer my services as a governor, and as a diplomat. Make me your ally and I will ensure you have powerful friends, in every corner of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:192`

## paragon.41200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

和我们聊聊你自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

Tell us more about yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:193`

## paragon.41200.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我在庭荠磁舵星姐妹会长大。和其她姐妹一样完成完成学业后，我被委任到了这个外派岗位上。多年来，我一直为别人出谋划策。对此斗胆一言，我还取得了一些小小的成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

I was raised in the Sisterhood of the Alyssum Magnetar. When my training was complete, like all my Sisters, I was assigned to an offworld position. For many years I provided counsel, and if I may be so bold as to claim it, I enjoyed some modest success.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:194`

## paragon.41200.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么要加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

Why do you wish to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:195`

## paragon.41200.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我相信和平是唯一能给我们星系带来稳定未来的东西。外交的艺术如在刀尖上舞蹈。我相信你一定会重视这方面的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

I believe that peace is the only thing that can create a stable future for our galaxy. The art of diplomacy is a subtle thing. I believe you can respect this.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:196`

## paragon.41200.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你还在姐妹会效力吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

Do you still serve the Sisterhood?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:197`

## paragon.41200.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我为一切生命效力。来吧，我们边喝杯热饮边继续这个话题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

I serve all life. Now come, let's say we continue this conversation over a warm drink?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:198`

## paragon.41200.hire_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41200.hire_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

§Y$NAME_pacifist2$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

### English

§Y$NAME_pacifist2$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

$NAME_authoritarian1$

### English

$NAME_authoritarian1$

## paragon.41300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H扫描显示，这个女人的斗篷里隐藏着许多令人震惊的武器。我们找不到任何关于她存在的官方记录，但面部识别算法显示她至少参与了三起备受关注的暗杀。§!\n\n请允许我省去那些繁文缛节。你是个聪明人，知道宇宙中危机四伏，其中的许多甚至目不可及。只要出个好价钱，我就能让你一窥究竟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

§HScans show that the woman's cloak conceals a truly shocking array of weaponry. We cannot find any official record of her existence, yet facial-recognition algorithms place her in the vicinity of at least three high-profile assassinations.§!\n\nAllow me to dispense with the formalities. You are no fool. You understand that the universe is full of dangers, most of them invisible. For the right price, I can reveal them.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:202`

## paragon.41300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你是谁？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Who are you?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:203`

## paragon.41300.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我是谁这一点都不重要。我想你会更感兴趣我有什么能耐。我是一件利器，只要给我足够的时间，就没有任何人敢反抗你。毕竟，死人是不会背叛你的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

Who I am is of no importance. Better ask what I can do for you. I am a useful tool. Give me time, and none will dare rebel. After all, there can't be a betrayal if the traitors are already dead.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:204`

## paragon.41300.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么协助我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

Why help us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:205`

## paragon.41300.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

单打独斗确实有好处，缺点就是不太稳定。在什么都要亲力亲为一段时间后，谁都会感到疲惫。实话说，我已经分了太多的心了。您王座后的阴影正是我最佳的藏身之所。更何况，在这我还可以让我以不同的，更微妙的手段来做事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

Working as an independent has its perks, but stability isn't one of them. After a while, one tires of playing all sides at once. To be honest, I was starting to attract too much attention. The shadow of your throne is as good a spot as any for me to lay low. Plus it lets me try my hand at a different, more subtle game.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:206`

## paragon.41300.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们凭什么信任你？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

How can we trust you?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:207`

## paragon.41300.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

相信？你不可能也大可不必相信我。我只卖给出价最高的人。只要你出价够高，那就没必要担心。此外，要真有人要你相信他，这反倒才相当可疑，甚至可以说是危险。对这种人我会立即派一队人去监听他家，跟踪他，密切关注他遇到的每一个人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

You can't and shouldn't. I sell to the highest bidder. Just make sure that's you and there's no need to worry. Besides, if someone says that you should trust them, I would say that such a person is suspicious. Perhaps even treacherous. I would immediately send a team to bug their home, tail them and keep tabs on whoever they meet.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:208`

## paragon.41300.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你的武装对一个间谍而言也太重型了点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

For a spy, you come heavily armed.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:209`

## paragon.41300.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

在我这一行，一点意外都能让人死无葬身之地。我更喜欢准备充分一点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

In my line of work, surprises tend to be fatal. I like to be ready.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:210`

## paragon.41300.hire_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41300.hire_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

§Y$NAME_authoritarian1$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

### English

§Y$NAME_authoritarian1$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:213`

## paragon.41400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位外星人的皮肤闪烁着粘稠的分泌物。他煞费苦心地深鞠了一躬。但当他一开口，声调却像孩子一样出奇地尖细。§!\n\n这位大人，欧！至高无上的统治者。我，看守人喀·拉，只是您卑微的仆人。您的每句话都是命令。让我管理一颗你的星球，我会给您带来荣耀和财富。啊，那么多的财富！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

§HThe alien's skin glistens with viscous secretions. He bows elaborately from the waist. When he speaks, his voice is surprisingly high pitched, almost childlike.§!\n\nMy lord, Your Honor, Oh! Supreme ruler - I, Q'la-Minder, am but your humble servant. Your word shall be my command. Let me take charge of one of your planets, and I will bring you glory and wealth. Oh, so much wealth!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:214`

## paragon.41400.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你具体能做些什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

What exactly can you do for us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:215`

## paragon.41400.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们都明白一些道理。比如平民是懒惰的。他们总是要求更多的权利，更高的工资，更多的自由……如果不加以控制，他们就会把本该属于您的东西塞进肚子里！但如果让我负责的话……\n\n§H他慢慢地攥紧拳头。§!\n\n给我一颗行星，我会把这些思想残渣从他们的脑子里榨出去。然后他们将对您感恩戴德，伟大的王。欧！他们真会如此！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

We both know how things are. The commoner is lazy. Always demanding more rights, higher wages, more freedom... Left unchecked, they would stuff their bellies with what rightly belongs to you! But if I were placed in charge...\n\n§HSlowly, he clenches a fist.§!\n\nJust give me a planet, and I will squeeze these silly notions from their heads. They will show gratitude, Glorious One. Oh! They will indeed!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:216`

## paragon.41400.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么想加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

Why do you seek to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:217`

## paragon.41400.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

谁会放弃为这样伟大的您效劳的机会呢？\n\n§H他期待地咯咯笑了起来。§!\n\n有些人生为王者，其他人就应当臣服。这就是万物之理。您可以信任我。我会忠诚地为您效劳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

Who would not leap at the chance to serve such majesty?\n\n§HHe giggles with anticipation.§!\n\nSome are born to rule. The rest must be led. That is the order of things. You can trust me. I will serve you faithfully.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:218`

## paragon.41400.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你还挺能捧我的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

You grovel well.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:219`

## paragon.41400.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

哦，圣洁的王！这只我的真实感想！请让我继续说下去好吗？和您相比，我不过是一坨烂泥、一只在泥土里蠕动的幼虫！赞美您是我的荣幸和特权。仅仅是您的存在就使我满心欢喜。请允许我以无限的忠诚为您服务!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

Oh, most Exalted One! This is but a taste! Shall I go on? Compared to you, I am but slime. I am but a larva wriggling in the dirt! Praising you is my honor and my privilege. Your mere Presence floods me with joy. Let me serve you with boundless devotion!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:220`

## paragon.41400.hire_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41400.hire_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_authoritarian2$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

§Y$NAME_authoritarian2$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:221`

## paragon.41500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_spiritualist1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

$NAME_spiritualist1$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:223`

## paragon.41500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位外星人一动不动。§!\n\n命运指引着我的每一步。我穿越了无边的黑暗来到你的面前。我是神谕者尤拉斯塔尔。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

§HThe alien is motionless.§!\n\nFate guides my steps. I have crossed the blind reaches to stand before you. I am Ulastar, the Oracle.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:224`

## paragon.41500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你能说说我们的命运吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

What can you say about our destiny?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:225`

## paragon.41500.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H他仿佛用那双盲眼凝视着。§!\n\n在一条路上，白色的火焰在虚空中燃烧。另一条则覆满飞溅的余烬。你们将面对恐怖，那将是你们的毁灭。除非？命运之线织出最微弱的希望。厄运或许可以避免。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

§HHe stares with blind eyes.§!\n\nOn one path, a white flame burns against the void. Along the other lies a sputtering ember. You will face Terror, and it shall be your undoing. Unless? The threads of fate weave the thinnest hope. Doom may yet be averted.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:226`

## paragon.41500.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么想加入我们？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Why do you seek to join us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:227`

## paragon.41500.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H他从一个隐藏的袋子里扔出一团旋转的尘埃。当他说话时，就通过这样的方式做出宣告：§!\n\n我受到召唤而来。你们必会或必将遇见我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

§HFrom a hidden pouch he flings a swirling cloud of dust. When he speaks, he does so as a proclamation:§!\n\nI came because I was called. You have sought me out. Or perhaps you will.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:228`

## paragon.41500.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你具体能做些什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

What exactly will you do for us?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:229`

## paragon.41500.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H从长袍的内袋中，$NAME_spiritualist1$翻出了一套雕刻的骨头。将它们抛到地上后，他似乎在通过某种超越视觉的感官在观察这些骨头的摆放。§!\n\n我会与信徒分享我之所见。我会在你的民族之船即将坠入风暴时将它扶正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

§HFrom the folds of his robes $NAME_spiritualist1$ produces a set of carved bones. Scattering them across the floor, he appears to study the pattern with senses beyond sight.§!\n\nI will share my visions with the faithful. I will right the ship of your nation as it prepares to plunge into the storm.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:230`

## paragon.41500.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你的能力是怎么来的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

How did you come by your gifts?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:231`

## paragon.41500.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41500.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

许多年前，我在一个行星矿坑里辛苦工作。虽然我的肉眼能看见，但我的心之眼却被物质世界蒙蔽了。后来，我觉醒的那一天到来了。\n\n他们说这是意外，但我知道这是天意。突然的爆炸声炸裂了我的视觉神经。在随后的黑暗中，我看到了时间之河。直到今天，它还在我眼前汩汩流淌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

Many years ago, I toiled in the pits of a planetary mine. Though my physical body could see, my soul was blinded by the material world. Then came the day of my awakening.\n\nThey called it an accident, but I know it was providence; a sudden explosion fried my optical nerves. In the darkness that followed, I perceived the river of time. To this day, it flows before my eyes.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:232`

## paragon.41500.hire_custom_tooltip

### 中文

§Y$NAME_spiritualist1$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

§Y$NAME_spiritualist1$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

## paragon.41600.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_spiritualist2$

### English

$NAME_spiritualist2$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:235`

## paragon.41600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位男子形如枯骨，饱经风霜：他精美的服饰几乎褪色殆尽，他的皮肤如羊皮纸般干燥粗糙，他的嗓音近乎低语。§!\n\n吾名为纳苏兹·德墨忒尔。吾身为纤尘与迷思所化。感谢您接纳我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

§HThe man is thin to the point of starvation. There is a sense of decrepitude about him: his fine clothes are faded, his skin as dry as parchment, and his voice rises barely above a whisper.§!\n\nMy name is Nasuz Demetor. I am a collector of dust and myth. Thank you for receiving me.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:236`

## paragon.41600.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.a&lang=en)

### 中文

纤尘与迷思是什么东西？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Dust and myth?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:237`

## paragon.41600.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H他微笑了起来，皮肤为之绷紧。§!\n\n星系非常古老。在你们之前，已有无数的文明兴起又衰败。纤尘是他们的遗产，迷思是我们的传承。我尝试发掘过去的真相，让人可以铭记，可以学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

§HHis smile strains the surface of his skin.§!\n\nThis galaxy is old. Long before yours came to be, countless other civilizations rose and fell. Dust is their legacy, and myth our inheritance. I seek to uncover the truth of the past, that it may be preserved. That it may be learned from.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:238`

## paragon.41600.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是你来的原因吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

And this is why you've come?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:239`

## paragon.41600.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

这不止是为了我自己的好处，我保证。历史之赋予远超我们的想象，我将与你分享。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

It is not only for my own benefit, I assure you. No, the riches of the past are beyond counting. I would share them with you.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:240`

## paragon.41600.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你通过考古得到了什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

What have you gained from your excavations?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:241`

## paragon.41600.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

文物、智慧、景观……但不止于此。过往的一切皆有回声。是不是听起来有点自相矛盾？明明我们在与遗忘抗争，却往往忘记去倾听。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

Artifacts, wisdom, splendor... But not only this. All things have their echo in the past. Paradoxical is it not? How in our fight against oblivion we often forget to listen.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:242`

## paragon.41600.hire_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41600.hire_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_spiritualist2$§!作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

§Y$NAME_spiritualist2$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:243`

## paragon.40110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

兹泰格·迈尔的宝藏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

The Treasure of Zteg-Mayr

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:248`

## paragon.40110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!知道很多关于可怕的海盗之王兹泰格·迈尔的故事。几个世纪前，他一路劫掠星系，攫取财富，稀有资源和价值连城的古代文物。现在，多亏了一套古老的星图，[Root.GetFirstName]相信[Root.GetHeShe]已经发现了哲泰格-迈尔藏匿他大量战利品的地方。[Root.GetFirstName]拒绝详细说明究竟是如何获得这些海图的，但可以相信它们的真实性，并希望我们派一艘工程船去挖掘宝藏。如果那儿还有宝藏的话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! knows many stories about the dreaded Pirate Lord Zteg-Mayr. Centuries ago, he pillaged his way across the galaxy, seizing riches, rare resources and ancient artifacts of untold value. Now, thanks to a set of old star charts [Root.GetFirstName] believes [Root.GetHeShe] has discovered where Zteg-Mayr hid his bountiful booty. [Root.GetFirstName] refused to elaborate on how he acquired the charts, but believes them to be real, and wants us to send a construction ship to excavate the treasure. If there's anything left.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:249`

## paragon.40110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

也许可以去看看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

Might be worth looking into.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:250`

[event_target:renowned_xenophile1.GetName]发现的兹泰格·迈尔的宝藏。

The pirate treasure of Zteg-Mayr discovered by [event_target:renowned_xenophile1.GetName].

## paragon.40111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40111.name&lang=en)

海盗宝藏

Pirate Treasure

## paragon.40111.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40111.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了大海盗兹泰格·迈尔藏匿他的宝藏的地下掩体。这些从许多星球偷来的宝物，现在将造福于我们的民族。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

The underground vault containing the treasure of Pirate Lord Zteg-Mayr has been breached. This treasure trove of artifacts, stolen from a number of different worlds, will now benefit our nation.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:254`

## paragon.40111.a

## paragon.40120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

安全港

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Safe Haven

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:257`

## paragon.40120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们欢迎§Y[Root.GetName]§!的传言已经传遍了星系。现在各种被放逐者、恶棍，还有海盗都把我们的帝国看作一个向所有人开放的地方，在这里所以人都可以有新的开始。[Root.GetFirstName]正在利用这一点，重新树立名望，并邀请他以前的熟人加入我们。许多人响应号召，来寻找更好的新生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

Word of the welcome we extended to §Y[Root.GetName]§! has spread across the galaxy. Outcasts, scoundrels, and pirates of all stripes now view our nation as open to all, a place where anyone can start over. [Root.GetFirstName] is playing on this and enjoying his newfound fame, and has invited his former acquaintances to join us. Many have answered the call, looking for a new, better life.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:258`

## paragon.40120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

They are welcome.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:259`

### English

Grateful Outcasts

## pop_outcast_happy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apop_outcast_happy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些曾经被放逐的人面对重启人生的机会展现出极大的感激，很快就接受了我们的方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

Showing great appreciation for the opportunity of a fresh start, these former outcasts have quickly taken to our ways.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:262`

## pop_outcast_unhappy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apop_outcast_unhappy&lang=en)

### English

Ungrateful Outcasts

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:263`

## pop_outcast_unhappy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apop_outcast_unhappy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些曾经被放逐的人在我们的社会中很难找到一席之地，感觉到自己并不被欢迎，就倾向于向社会表达出他们的不满。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

Being slow to find their place in our society, these former outcasts feel unwelcome and tend to express their unrest.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:264`
