## pop_outcast_crime_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apop_outcast_crime_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些曾经被放逐的人尝试滥用我们的信任欺骗了我们的善良。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

Seeking to exploit our trust, these former outcasts have preyed on our kindness.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:266`

## paragon.40210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪贡品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

Lost Tribute

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:269`

## paragon.40210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!听说了一支失踪的船队携带了大量财宝的传言。据说有一位外星使者和他的随从被派去给某个军阀带去贡品。在最近的一次任务中，[Root.GetHeShe]准确地定位了船队最后的位置。我们只需要派一艘船去取回它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has heard rumors about the vast riches carried by a lost convoy. It seems that an alien emissary and his retinue were sent to bring tribute to a certain warlord. During [Root.GetHisHer] latest assignment [Root.GetHeShe] was able to pinpoint the convoy's last location. We only need to send a ship to retrieve it.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:270`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40210.a&lang=en)

别介意我们的所作所为。

### English

Don't mind if we do.

## paragon.40220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

布洛葛“来袭”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

A Blorg Comes Calling

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:278`

## paragon.40220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位§Y[Root.GetName]§!曾经的伙伴，布洛葛行政官§Y$NONA_BLORG_EX_HUSBAND_NAME$§!希望加入我们的帝国。这位行政官遇上了麻烦，正在寻找重新开始的机会。[Root.GetFirstName]声称这位$NONA_BLORG_EX_HUSBAND_NAME$对管理矿产星球的经验相当丰富，而那位和善的行政官也带来了一箱珍贵的矿物作为见面礼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]'s§! ex-partner, the Blorg official §Y$NONA_BLORG_EX_HUSBAND_NAME$§!, wishes to join our empire. The official has fallen on hard times and is looking for a fresh start. [Root.GetFirstName] claims $NONA_BLORG_EX_HUSBAND_NAME$ has vast experience in the management of mining worlds, and that the affable official has brought a cache of precious minerals as a token of goodwill.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:279`

## paragon.40220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他应该还是能有点用的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

We can probably find some use for him.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:280`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:281`

## NONA_BLORG_EX_HUSBAND_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ANONA_BLORG_EX_HUSBAND_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一位靠自我奋斗塑造而成的勤奋的布洛葛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

A hardworking Blorg, shaped by countless personal struggles.

## paragon.40310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

挣脱[Root.GetHisHerCap]的枷锁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

Freed of [Root.GetHisHerCap] Burdens

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:287`

## paragon.40310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

终于，§Y[Root.GetName]§!终于从[Root.GetHeShe]家族衰败的情绪低落中恢复出来。在摆脱了负罪和悲痛的枷锁之后，在服务我们的人民中终于找到了[Root.GetHisHer]新的人生意义。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

At long last, §Y[Root.GetName]§! has recovered from the emotional shock of the fall of [Root.GetHisHer] once-proud noble House. Unburdened by the guilt and melancholy, [Root.GetHeShe] has found a new purpose in the service of our people.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:288`

## paragon.40310.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40310.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

Very good.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:289`

## paragon.40320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

坦率的扩张者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Outspoken Expansionist

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:291`

## paragon.40320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一场激情似火的演讲中，§Y[Root.GetName]§!宣称帝国必须将重心放在扩张上。[Root.GetHeSheCap]提交了两个关于给舰队调配更多资源以完成目标的计划。这两者确实对我们的舰队都很有价值，从长远看来也能让我们都收益。然而同时事实两个计划是不可能的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

In a fiery speech, §Y[Root.GetName]§! declared that the nation's focus must be on expansion. [Root.GetHeSheCap] submitted two plans on how the fleet could use additional resources to further such a goal. Both options are valid and could benefit us greatly in the long run. However, implementing both plans at once is impossible

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:292`

## paragon.40320.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40320.a&lang=en)

### 中文

舰队强大的是真的强大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

A strong fleet will show them our might.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:293`

## paragon.40320.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40320.b&lang=en)

### 中文

后勤补给才是胜利的保障。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Superior logistics will lead us to victory.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:294`

## vyctor_aggressive_expansion_plan

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avyctor_aggressive_expansion_plan&lang=en)

### 中文

积极扩张计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Aggressive Expansion Plan

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:295`

## vyctor_aggressive_expansion_plan_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avyctor_aggressive_expansion_plan_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

赢得战斗需要策略，但自身的强大则是成功的源泉。当需要使用强力手段时，当然是越强力越好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Battles are won with strategy, but strength remains the foundation of success. When applying force, it is best to do so resolutely.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:296`

## vyctor_improved_fleet_logistics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avyctor_improved_fleet_logistics&lang=en)

### 中文

改进舰队后勤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Improved Fleet Logistics

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:297`

## vyctor_improved_fleet_logistics_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avyctor_improved_fleet_logistics_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

提升效率的作用绝对不容小视：优化船坞的产出，我们的扩张将会因此变得更加迅捷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Efficiency should not be underestimated: By optimizing the output of our shipyards, our expansion will be that much swifter.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:298`

## paragon.40410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

久经沙场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Drawn by Bloodshed

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:301`

## paragon.40410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!赢得过许多战争。[Root.GetHeSheCap]曾经同另一位伟大的勇士，$GIA_ZUMON_GREAT_WARRIOR_NAME$，一起并肩作战。此人正在寻求新的可以侍奉的主人。他是一位果敢的舰队司令，[Root.GetFirstName]对其大加褒奖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has won many battles. [Root.GetHeSheCap] once fought alongside another great warrior, $GIA_ZUMON_GREAT_WARRIOR_NAME$, who is looking for a new master to serve. He is a ruthless admiral, and [Root.GetFirstName] speaks highly of him.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:302`

## paragon.40410.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40410.a&lang=en)

### 中文

来吧，荣耀在等待着他。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

Glory awaits him if he joins us.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:303`

## paragon.40410.a.custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40410.a.custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

一位叫§Y$GIA_ZUMON_GREAT_WARRIOR_NAME$§!的奇恩舰队司令作为一名§H领袖§!加入了我们的帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

The Tzynn Admiral §Y$GIA_ZUMON_GREAT_WARRIOR_NAME$§! joins our empire as a §HLeader§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:304`

## paragon.40410.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40410.b&lang=en)

### 中文

关于战争他能教授我们一些什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

What can he teach us about war?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:305`

## GIA_ZUMON_GREAT_WARRIOR_NAME

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGIA_ZUMON_GREAT_WARRIOR_NAME&lang=en)

### 中文

哈斯克·古特拉

## paragon.40420.name

## paragon.40420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!坐立不安，渴望战斗。[Root.GetHeSheCap]受够了那些荼毒[coliseum_planet.GetName]的懦夫。[Root.GetHeSheCap]提议建造一座大型竞技场，让荣誉猎手和罪犯身处同一地方。这样一个计划能够为我们的战士提供宝贵的实训机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! is restless and craves a fight. [Root.GetHeSheCap] has had enough of the weaklings that plague [coliseum_planet.GetName]. [Root.GetHeSheCap] has proposed an arena where glory-hunters may be pitted against criminals. Such a plan is sure to provide our warriors with valuable training.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:312`

### English

### 中文

尽管我们的战士很珍惜这次发泄压力的机会，但高昂的嗜血在民众中滋生了焦虑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

Though our warriors appreciate the chance to blow off steam, the kindled bloodthirst has led to some anxiety among the populace.

## paragon.40510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

优化提案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Optimization Proposal

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:320`

## paragon.40510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!希望能自动化[planet_governed.GetName]上的物流方案。提出的计划称会将工厂、精炼厂和处理设施通过一个复杂的管道和传送带网络连接起来。该计划在理论上看起来不错，但对生活在附近的人口而言存在风险。工业泄漏、辐射和高频振动之类危险是真实存在的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! wishes to automate the shipping methods on [planet_governed.GetName]. The proposed plan would link the factories, refineries, and processing facilities together through a complex network of pipelines and conveyor belts. Although the project looks good in theory, there are risks for the population living nearby; between industrial spillages, radiation concerns and high-frequency vibration, the danger is real.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:321`

### English

## factorator_industrial_network_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afactorator_industrial_network_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_xenophobe1$§!设计的复杂货运管道，超级高效，但要付出居民的健康为代价。

### English

## paragon.40520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有意义的实验？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Promising Experiments?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:327`

## paragon.40520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!提交了一项研究计划，旨在评估未来的工业发展。该研究将会惠及经济，并促进[Root.GetHisHer]的个人发展。[Root.GetHeSheCap]准备了一系列非常具有意义、但可能会导致受试目标死亡的实验。[Root.GetHeSheCap]向我们保证这些实验只是需要一些小小的牺牲来成就上善之道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has presented research plans to evaluate future industrial developments. This could benefit the economy as well as [Root.GetHisHer] personal projects. [Root.GetHeSheCap] has prepared a number of experiments that look very promising but would likely kill most of the test subjects. [Root.GetHeSheCap] assures us that a small sacrifice is necessary for the greater good.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:328`

## aturion_population_reform_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaturion_population_reform_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了集体的利益，人民本应准备好献出生命和肢体。与之相比，这些小小的改变根本不值一提。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

For the good of the whole, our people should be prepared to give both life and limb. By comparison, these small changes are a kindness.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:342`

## paragon.40620.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40620.name&lang=en)

罪犯改造计划

### English

Criminal Reformation Program

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:344`

## paragon.40620.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40620.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!开发了一项复杂的改过计划，把社会上那些问题分子改造成勤勉的公民。风险评估估计，17.3%的罪犯将不可能改过自新，这应该被认为是‘可接受的损失’。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has developed a complex rehabilitation program to reform the more troublesome elements in our society into diligent citizens. Risk assessment estimates that 17.3% of the criminal population will be impossible to rehabilitate and should be considered 'acceptable losses.'

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:345`

## aturion_criminal_reformation_programs_desc

这些项目依靠的是教育和改进，而不是强制的预防措施，这有助于让民众更幸福。

## paragon.40720.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40720.name&lang=en)

### 中文

超然支持

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Numinous Support

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:363`

## paragon.40720.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40720.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!向[Root.GetHisHer]的教团“超然均衡”请求支援，来帮助我们帝国继续对抗暴政和压迫者。为我们运送宝贵合金的货船已经从他们的秘密总部抵达。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has requested support from [Root.GetHisHer] order, the Numinous Equilibrium, to aid our empire in the ongoing struggle against tyranny and oppressors. Freighter ships carrying valuable alloys to sustain us have arrived from their secret headquarters.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:364`

### English

## paragon.40810.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40810.name&lang=en)

### 中文

科学就是魔法

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

Science is Magic

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:368`

## paragon.40810.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40810.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!和[Root.GetHisHer]的机器人伙伴戈尔基花费大部分业余时间进行私人实验。其中一些现在得到了有趣的结果，[Root.GetHeShe]与我们帝国的科学家们分享了[Root.GetHisHer]的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! and [Root.GetHisHer] robot companion Gorky have spent much of their spare time conducting private experiments. Some of them have now yielded interesting results, and [Root.GetHeShe] has shared [Root.GetHisHer] findings with our empire's scientists.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:369`

## paragon.40810.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40810.a&lang=en)

好消息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

Great news.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:370`

## paragon.40820.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40820.name&lang=en)

### 中文

收养外星宠物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

Adopted Alien Pet

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:372`

## paragon.40820.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40820.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!收养了一只外星生物来陪伴[Root.GetHisHer]的机器人助手戈尔基。它是一只五彩斑斓的未开智软体动物，表现出了不俗的智力，能对口哨指令作出反应。收养这只生物似乎改变了[Root.GetHisHer]的性格，[Root.GetHimHer]变得更加有责任心，更加安定了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has adopted an alien creature to accompany [Root.GetHisHer] robot assistant Gorky. It's a colorful mollusk who, though pre-sapient, exhibits an impressive intellect and responds to whistled commands. Taking responsibility for the creature appears to have altered [Root.GetHisHer] personality, making [Root.GetHimHer] far more responsible and balanced.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:373`

## paragon.40820.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40820.a&lang=en)

它不咬人吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

Does it bite?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:374`

## paragon.40910.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40910.name&lang=en)

### English

[Root.GetName] 2.0

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:377`

## paragon.40910.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40910.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!声称已经在多方面“改进”了[Root.GetHisHer]的身体。在没有获得允许的情况下，[Root.GetHeShe]为了庆祝[Root.GetHisHer]自己达到了完美的阶段而向整支舰队发送信息，并就此发表了一份宣言称，文明的出路唯有合成飞升：所有有机生命都脆弱不堪，无法逃离死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! claims to have 'improved' [Root.GetHisHer] physical form in a number of ways. Without permission, [Root.GetHeShe] has sent messages across the fleet celebrating [Root.GetHisHer] perfected state and has released a manifesto claiming that the only viable future for a civilization is synthetic ascension: all organic life is frail and doomed to die.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:378`

### English

[Root.GetHeSheCap] will show us the way.

### English

## paragon.40920.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40920.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高价合金

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Valuable Alloys

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:382`

## paragon.40920.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.40920.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!从国境内所有的舰船传感器上收集数据。使用算法计算重力扭曲后，[Root.GetFirstName]发现了我们的宇域中有一个天体的表面下隐藏着什么东西。如果数据准确无误，那么那颗荒芜的岩石下应该满是稀有合金。[Root.GetFirstName]将此视为一个拓展并升级舰队的好机会，提议派遣一艘工程船去挖取那些合金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has collected sensor data from all ships within the our borders. Using algorithms that calculate gravimetric distortions [Root.GetFirstName] has discovered something hidden beneath the surface of one of the galactic objects in our space. If the data is correct, the barren rock should be filled with rare alloys. [Root.GetFirstName] sees this as an opportunity to expand and upgrade the fleet, and proposes dispatching a construction ship to extract the alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:383`

### English

## paragon.41010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!在[Root.GetHisHer]的房间里闭门数月撰写自传，其中不仅有[Root.GetHisHer]自己的毕生经历，还有[Root.GetHeShe]所了解的工程学研究、建设和稀有材料的知识。只要拜读[Root.GetHisHer]的大作，我们的科学家就一定能有所发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has spent months in [Root.GetHisHer] cabin writing a self-biography that includes not only [Root.GetHisHer] life story and travels, but also everything [Root.GetHeShe] knows about engineering research, construction, and rare materials. When reviewing his work, our scientists made some interesting discoveries.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:393`

### English

## paragon.41020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

向$BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$问好

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Say Hello to $BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:396`

## paragon.41020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!建造了一个机器伙伴，叫做$BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$。这台机器人具有高级学习能力，并且拥有人格矩阵。它帮助[Root.GetName]进行[Root.GetHisHer]的研究和维修工作。然而，与它的创造者一样，$BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$也是好奇心满满，渴望着离开家去冒险。[Root.GetName]问我们，$BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$能不能在我们社会中拥有自主的一席之地？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! built a robot companion named $BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$. The robot is capable of advanced learning, and has a personality matrix. It is assisting [Root.GetName] with [Root.GetHisHer] research and repair jobs. However, like its creator, $BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$ is curious and longs to leave home to embark on its own adventures. [Root.GetName] wonders if $BORIN_ROBOT_COMPANION_NAME$ could play an autonomous role within our society.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:397`

### English

### English

## borin_administrator_beryllia_desc

### 中文

打捞者§Y$NAME_materialist2$§!的机器人伙伴，现在任职于办公室中，可以积极利用她的高级学习能力，精简帝国相关流程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

Salvager §Y$NAME_materialist2$'s§! robot companion has taken to office work, helping to refine our procedures by putting her self-learning capabilities to good use.

## paragon.41110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

罪恶显形

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

Criminals Exposed

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:407`

## paragon.41110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!长期在调查一处境内隐秘发展的犯罪网络。[Root.GetHeSheCap]将许多犯罪头目和海盗与腐败政客和军官勾结的证据整理到了一起。[Root.GetName]采用了新系统来加快此类案件的司法程序，目的是在罪犯有机会离开我们的司法管辖区之前抓住他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has been investigating a criminal network that has been growing quietly within our borders. [Root.GetHeSheCap] has put together evidence that connects various crime lords and pirates with corrupt politicians and military officers. [Root.GetName] has implemented new systems to expedite judicial proceedings in cases like this, with the intention of catching all criminals before they have a chance to leave our jurisdiction.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:408`

## paragon.41110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这会让所有人都受益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

This will benefit us all.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:409`

## judge_improved_crime_fighting_laws

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajudge_improved_crime_fighting_laws&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:410`

## judge_improved_crime_fighting_laws_desc

一系列用于对抗大规模犯罪阴谋的新法规

### English

A series of new legislation to fight large scale criminal conspiracies.

## paragon.41120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

论犯罪之本源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

On the Nature of Crime

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:413`

## paragon.41120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!花费了[Root.GetHisHer]的大半生学习犯罪的本源和暴力侵犯的动机。[Root.GetHeSheCap]向专家寻求建议，研究其他现存和已故帝国的文化，并使用先进算法来设想一个乌托邦社会可能的样子。[Root.GetHisHerCap]的毕生心血凝聚成了一篇宏大的社会学论文，将会对未来的几代人起到指导作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has spent [Root.GetHisHer] life studying the nature of crime and the motivation of violent offenders. [Root.GetHeSheCap] has sought advice from experts, studied the cultures of other empires, both living and dead, and used advanced algorithms to envision what a utopian society might look like. [Root.GetHisHerCap] life's work has culminated in a vast sociological essay that will serve as a guide for future generations.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:414`

## paragon.41120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

对所有人是件大好事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

A great benefit to us all.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:415`

## paragon.41210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

和平与繁荣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Peace and Prosperity

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:418`

## paragon.41210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!因举办奢华宴会而享有盛誉。这些宴会以庭荠磁舵星成员的精彩表演、奇异的食物和饮料以及哲学和伦理讨论而闻名。在这里，人们建立有意，促成交易，解开心结。[Root.GetHisHerCap]将这么多千差万别的人凝聚起来的能力是我们繁荣发展的坚实基础。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has garnered a wide reputation for hosting lavish feasts. The gatherings are known for spectacular performances by members of the Alyssum Magnetar, exotic food and drink, as well as philosophical and ethical discussions. Here friendships are formed, business deals are struck and old grievances are forgiven. [Root.GetHisHerCap] ability to unite such disparate individuals lays a solid foundation for our future prosperity.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:419`

## paragon.41210.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41210.a&lang=en)

### 中文

团结就是力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

We are stronger together.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:420`

团结的人民

### English

Population United

## vas_population_united_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Avas_population_united_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_pacifist2$§!非常受人欢迎，她的能力和声望将大家团聚在一起，团结一致的社会变得更加稳定了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

Owing to §Y$NAME_pacifist2$'s§! popularity with the people and her skill at bringing them together, society as a whole has grown more stable.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:422`

## paragon.41220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们需要的时候

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

In Our Time of Need

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:424`

## paragon.41220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y§![Root.GetName]简直是一位劝诱方面的艺术家。[Root.GetHeSheCap]利用[Root.GetHisHer]的人脉和政治影响力从整个星系收集重要信息。[Root.GetFirstName]把[Root.GetHisHer]所有的知识都保存在一件传家宝里：一块浅绿色的水晶，[Root.GetHeShe]偶尔会在[Root.GetHisHer]的奢华宴会上佩戴。当在特定的光谱下观察时，它的秘密就会以一个壮丽光晕的形式投射出来。现在[Root.GetHeShe]认为是时候利用这些知识造福社会了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! is a true artiste when it comes to charm and persuasion. [Root.GetHeSheCap] has used [Root.GetHisHer] contacts and political clout to gather vital information from all over the galaxy. [Root.GetFirstName] has kept all of [Root.GetHisHer] knowledge in a family heirloom: a pale green crystal that [Root.GetHeShe] occasionally wears at [Root.GetHisHer] lavish feasts. When held up to a specific spectrum of light, its secrets are projected in a magnificent halo. Now, [Root.GetHeShe] believes the time has come to use this knowledge for the good of society.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:425`

## paragon.41220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会好好使用的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

It will be put to good use.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:426`

## paragon.41310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

情报库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Gathered Intel

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:429`

## paragon.41310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!呈递了一个数据库，其中包含了多年来从[Root.GetHisHer]的延伸线人处收集来的敏感信息。这一巨大的数据库包括军事部署计划、秘密研究、意识读取数据在内的等等信息。这些东西当然极具价值，但使用这些信息可能会加剧内部和外部冲突。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! presents a database containing sensitive information gathered over the years from [Root.GetHisHer] extensive contacts. The massive cache includes everything from schematics over military installations, secret research, and extracts from mind probing. What is offered is valuable, but using the information may escalate internal as well as external conflicts.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:430`

使用数据。

## paragon.41320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41320.name&lang=en)

扩展线人网络

### English

Expanded Contact Network

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:436`

## paragon.41320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!在整个星系中继续铺展开[Root.GetHisHer]的线人网络。有些人是自愿告密的，而另一些人则是被勒索或洗脑的。但不管怎么样，[Root.GetHeShe]收集到的信息都会非常有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! has grown [Root.GetHisHer] network of contacts throughout the galaxy. Some are willing informants, while others are blackmailed or brainwashed. In any case, the information [Root.GetHeShe] gathers will be extremely beneficial.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:437`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41320.a&lang=en)

### English

## paragon.41410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奢华宫殿

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Vanity Palace

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:442`

## paragon.41410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]，§!那位奢靡无度的行政官，在[planet_governed.GetName]为[Root.GetHimselfHerself]建造了一座豪华的宫殿。这座宫殿既彰显着[Root.GetHisHer]的权利和财富，也让[Root.GetHisHer]的浮夸和腐朽显露无疑。为了庆祝宫殿落成，[Root.GetHeShe]将举办一场盛大的宴会，所有的显赫人物都受邀出席。[Root.GetHeShe]还会罕见地一示慷慨，与百姓分享一些[Root.GetHisHer]的财富。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§!, the decadent official, has built an extravagant palace for [Root.GetHimselfHerself] on [planet_governed.GetName]. This palace is as much a symbol of [Root.GetHisHer] power and wealth as of [Root.GetHisHer] vanity and corruption. To celebrate its completion, [Root.GetHeShe] will hold a grand feast, to which everyone of importance has been invited. And in a rare show of generosity [Root.GetHeShe] has also shared some of [Root.GetHisHer] riches with the population.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:443`

## paragon.41410.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41410.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还能指望他什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

What else could be expected?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:444`

这座宏伟的建筑既怪诞又迷人。

### English

This monumental building is as grotesque as it is fascinating.

## paragon.41420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41420.name&lang=en)

### 中文

贪赃者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

The Embezzler

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:448`

## paragon.41420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!，那位奢靡无度的行政官，其贪污腐败行为暴露了。这一现象已经持续了挺长的时间，[Root.GetHeShe]不仅将[Root.GetHisHer]的财产存入银行，还藏匿于地下金库和星系各处的深空贮藏中。在面对质问时，[Root.GetHeShe]涕泗横流，拿[Root.GetHimselfHerself]的头抢地，信誓旦旦地保证这些财产的根本目的是为统治者准备的献礼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§!, the decadent official, has been caught embezzling. This has been going on for quite some time, and [Root.GetHeShe] has stashed [Root.GetHisHer] wealth not just in bank accounts but also in underground vaults and deep space depots all around the galaxy. In face of the charges, [Root.GetHeShe] threw [Root.GetHimselfHerself] to the ground, swearing that these items were intended as gifts for the ruler.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:449`

## paragon.41420.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41420.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这条蛀虫！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

That worm!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:450`

## paragon.41510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

伟大预言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

The Great Prophecy

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:453`

## paragon.41510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多年来，神秘人§Y[Root.GetName]§!解读了许多揭示更大命运的征兆和预示。现在，[Root.GetSheHe]宣称预见到了整个星系将会统一在一面旗帜下的画面——而那正是我们的旗帜。真正的辉煌还在后头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

For many years, §Y[Root.GetName]§! the Mystic has interpreted signs and portents that reveal a greater destiny. Now, [Root.GetSheHe] claims a vision of the galaxy united under a single banner - our own. The real splendor is still to come.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:454`

## paragon.41510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41510.a&lang=en)

### 中文

伟大的荣耀等待着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

Great glory awaits.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:455`

## ulastar_prophesied_greater_destiny

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aulastar_prophesied_greater_destiny&lang=en)

### 中文

预言伟大命运

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Prophesied Greater Destiny

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:456`

## ulastar_prophesied_greater_destiny_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aulastar_prophesied_greater_destiny_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_spiritualist1$§!的关于我们光辉未来的预言激励着人们重燃起热情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

§Y$NAME_spiritualist1$'s§! proclamations about our future glory spur the populace to renewed fervor and purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:457`

## paragon.41520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

追随召唤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

Heeding the Call

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:459`

## paragon.41520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多年来，§Y[Root.GetName]§!一直通过强大的灵能梦向虚空呼喊。现在，几艘载有许多物种成员的民用飞船出现在[planet_governed.GetName]轨道上。他们愿意为我们服务，以期和他们的先知一起生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

For years, §Y[Root.GetName]§! has been calling out into the void via powerful psionic dreams. Now, several civilian ships carrying members of many species have appeared in orbit around [planet_governed.GetName]. In exchange for a chance to live with their prophet, they are willing to serve us.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:460`

## paragon.41520.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41520.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们将会加入我们的族群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

They will be a part of our flock.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:461`

## paragon.41520.a.custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41520.a.custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

Adds §Y300§! Spiritualist, Psionic Pops to [event_target:planet_governed.GetName].

## paragon.41610.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41610.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古遗珍

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

Relics of an Ancient Past

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:466`

## paragon.41610.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41610.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

档案员§Y[Root.GetName]§!发现了一处远古遗物的储藏地。[Root.GetHeSheCap]从古老的传说和漫游海盗交易的传感器日志中拼凑出他们的行踪信息。这些神秘物体的起源尚不清楚，但通过吸收低频声音，它们会偶然产生轻微的时间扭曲。这项技术应该是有益的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! the Archivist has discovered a cache of ancient relics. [Root.GetHeSheCap] pieced together information of their whereabouts from old myths and sensor logs traded from roaming pirates. The origin of the mysterious objects remains unclear, but by absorbing low frequency sound, they occasionally produce slight temporal distortions. The technology should prove beneficial.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:467`

## paragon.41610.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41610.a&lang=en)

### 中文

确实是有价值的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

A valuable find indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:468`

## paragon.41620.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41620.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚空之语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Voices in the Void

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:470`

## paragon.41620.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41620.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

档案员§Y[Root.GetName]§!截获了一条奇怪的信息。这些信息在星际间回荡了数千年，受到严重的静电噪音影响。它包含了来自外星文明的问候，以及他们家园的精确位置。这个物种已经灭绝了很长一段时间，但在它们的母星上可能仍然有重要的遗迹和远古的知识有待发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! the Archivist has intercepted a strange message. Distorted by static, the message has reverberated between the stars for millennia. It contains a greeting from an alien civilization, as well as the precise location of their home world. The species has been extinct for quite some time, but there may still be important remnants and ancient knowledge to uncover on their home planet.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:471`

## paragon.41620.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.41620.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谁知道会不会发现什么好东西？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

Who knows what treasures we might find?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:472`

## integrated_command_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aintegrated_command_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的综合指挥流程使我们能够创新在战斗中使用攻击舰船的方式。我们也将摆脱烦人的海军部的争吵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

Our integrated command processes have allowed us to innovate the way we use strike ships in combat. We will also be rid of the quarrels of the pesky admiralty.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:478`

### English

## crisis_studied_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis_studied_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多亏斯克兰德的洞见，我们能制定出与天灾战斗的有效策略。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Thanks to Skrand's insights we were able to devise effective ways to wage our war against the Crisis.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:480`

装甲分层

### English

Armor Layering

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:482`

## talon_armor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atalon_armor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对利爪号表层装甲进行调研为我们提供了船体优化的思路。这可以在不增加整舰重量的情况下提升舰船的装甲防护能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

Investigation into the Talon's cladding has provided us with insights into hull optimization. This has allowed us to improve the armor of our ships without adding more weight.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:483`

## talon_hull

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atalon_hull&lang=en)

### 中文

船体强化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Hull Hardening

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:484`

## talon_hull_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atalon_hull_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过研究利爪号的船体，我们对于舰船结构的理解有了相当程度的进展，并且很快就会应用到加固舰船的实际操作之中去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

By studying the Talon's hull, we have improved our understanding of ship frames, processes that should be quickly applicable in fortifying our own ships' exteriors.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:485`

## skrand_story

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Askrand_story&lang=en)

### 中文

受历史感召

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

United In History

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:486`

## skrand_story_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Askrand_story_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我国人民被斯克兰德的陈述所感动，认为他是军事美德的典范，是提醒人们保持警惕的重要人物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

Moved by Skrand's testimony, our people regard him as a paragon of military virtue and a crucial reminder of the importance of vigilance.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:487`

## paragon.3000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地恢复了[gateway_system.GetName]星系中远古星门的供能！尽管年代久远，这座装置还是形成了一条完全可以供通行的稳定亚空间通路。\n\n我们还检测到了异常的、类似于进出星门时的能量读数，但没有任何船只过境的迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

We have successfully restored power to the ancient Gateway in the [gateway_system.GetName] system! Despite its age, the device has formed a stable subspace conduit that is fully traversable.\n\nWe're also detecting unusual energy readings, similar to those that occur when entering or exiting a gate, but without any evidence of a ship in transit.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:491`

## paragon.3000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

监视该状况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

Monitor the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:492`

## paragon.3001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抵达

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Arrival

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:494`

## paragon.3001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘巨大的舰船从星门中出现，它的体积之巨大我们前所未见。军队已处于高度戒备状态。\n\n那艘未知舰船对我们的呼叫没有应答，但鉴于它似乎完全处于瘫痪状态，我们暂时不会与它交战。我们仍在进行调查并试图开启通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

A hulking ship has emerged from the gate, its sheer scale unlike anything we have seen before. Military forces have been put on high alert.\n\nThe unknown vessel is not responding to our hails, but given the fact that it appears completely disabled, we will not engage it for now. Investigation and attempts to open comms continue.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:495`

## paragon.3001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3001.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是够大的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

It's huge!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:496`

## paragon.3001.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3001.b&lang=en)

### 中文

执行扫描流程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

Engage scanning procedures.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:497`

## paragon.3002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

Insight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:499`

## paragon.3002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对这艘巨大舰船的调查并没有得到很多信息。然而，我们已经能够破译星门第一次打开时收到的一些数据。看来，这艘舰船已经被困在星门里一段时间了（可能久得难以想象）。\n\n为了了解更多，我们需要登上它。由于这是一艘军舰，建议派遣特种部队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

Our investigation into the hulking ship hasn't yielded many answers. However, we have been able to decipher some of the data we received when the gate first opened. It appears that the ship has been stuck inside the gate for an unknown (and possibly incredible) length of time.\n\nTo find out more, we will need to board the ship. As it is a military vessel, sending a special ops unit is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:500`

## paragon.3002.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3002.a&lang=en)

### 中文

准备陆军运输船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

Prepare troop transport.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:501`

## PARAGON_GATEWAY_DUDE_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_GATEWAY_DUDE_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

登船行动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

Boarding Operation

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:502`

## PARAGON_GATEWAY_DUDE_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_GATEWAY_DUDE_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须登上这艘外星舰船。微弱的能量读数表明其中可能有一个正常运转的生命维持系统。有可能有人或什么东西潜伏在船上。我们的士兵很想知道答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

We must board the alien vessel. Faint energy readings could indicate a functioning life-support system. There is a chance that someone, or something, may be lurking on the ship. Our soldiers are eager to find out.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:503`

## paragon.3003.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3003.name&lang=en)

### 中文

剑拔弩张的欢迎会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

Uneasy Welcome

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:505`

## paragon.3003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在登上舰船后不久，我们就遭遇了一队异形的外星人。由于我们的突然出现，许多外星人受到了惊吓，纷纷拔出武器。谢天谢地，交火得以避免！\n\n在要求双方冷静下来之后，外星人指挥官解释道这艘船需要进行紧急维修。一旦所有系统正常运转，他和他的船员打算返回自己的母星，斯卡拉维德。\n\n这位指挥官全身着军队盛装礼服，其形象十分令人深刻。这艘舰船的船员在提及他时都满怀敬意，其个人魅力甚至影响到了我方人员。他已经营造了双边建立正式外交关系的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

Soon after boarding the vessel, we encountered a group of uniformed aliens. Startled by our sudden appearance, many were quick to draw weapons. Thankfully, a firefight was avoided.\n\nCalling for calm, the alien commander explained that the ship is in need of emergency repairs. Once all systems are operational, he and his crew intend to return to their home planet, Skravird.\n\nDressed in full military regalia, he certainly cuts an impressive figure. The ship's crew speak of him with reverence, and the force of his personality is palpable even to our people. He has already raised the possibility of establishing formal diplomatic relations.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:506`

## paragon.3003.desc_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3003.desc_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

在舰船的内部，我们发现了一支外星人团队，他们正在尝试修复舰船的功能。我们的突然出现让他们感觉受到了威胁，许多外星人很快拔出了武器。尽管避免了冲突，这位外星人指挥官显然还是把我们当做了敌人。\n\n他身着全套军服，器宇不凡。即便在我们看来，他的人格魅力也是显而易见的。然而，目前似乎很难进行进一步的沟通。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

Inside the vessel, we discovered a group of aliens working to restore the ship's functionality. Viewing our sudden appearance as a threat, many were quick to draw weapons. Although violence was avoided, the alien commander clearly views us as adversaries.\n\nDressed in full military regalia, he cuts an impressive figure. The force of his personality is palpable even to us. However, at this point further dialogue seems unlikely.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:507`

## paragon.3003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3003.a&lang=en)

### 中文

帮助指挥官修复他的舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

Help this commander repair his ship.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:508`

## paragon.3003.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3003.b&lang=en)

### 中文

无视他。转而扣押这艘舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

Ignore him. Seize the ship instead.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:509`

修复外星舰船。

### English

Fix the Alien Vessel

## PARAGON_GATEWAY_DUDE_REPAIR_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_GATEWAY_DUDE_REPAIR_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

这艘舰船需要进行全面维护。我们自愿支援维修工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

The alien vessel requires extensive maintenance. We have agreed to assist with repairs.

## paragon.3004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被遗忘的舰队司令

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Forgotten Admiral

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:513`

## paragon.3004.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3004.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工程团队已经对外星人船员提供了援助。这艘船的番号大致可以翻译作“利爪一号”，外星指挥官自称斯克兰德·夏普比克，是一位享有盛名的海军上将。\n\n通过对比利爪一号和我们自己的数据，我们确认斯克兰德和他的船员被困在星门里长达几千年。对他们来说，这是一次短暂的旅行，但对星系的其他地方来说，几千年已经过去了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

Our engineering team has ably assisted the alien crew. The vessel's designation translates roughly to 'First Talon', and their commander has identified himself as Skrand Sharpbeak, an admiral of renown.\n\nBy comparing data from the First Talon's databanks and our own, we have confirmed that Skrand and his crew were trapped within the gate for several millennia. For them, it was a short trip - but for the rest of the galaxy, thousands of years passed by.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:514`

## paragon.3004.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3004.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.3004.intro$\n\n得益于我们对斯卡拉维德的发掘，至少我们已经能向夏普比克司令指出他故乡星球的所在了，但他曾经爱过的所有人和事都早已离去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

$paragon.3004.intro$\n\nThanks to our excavation on Skravird, at least we can point Admiral Sharpbeak towards his home planet, but everything and everyone he has have ever loved is long since gone.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:515`

## paragon.3004.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3004.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.3004.intro$\n\n他们无家可归，他们曾经爱过的所有人和事都早已离去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

$paragon.3004.intro$\n\nThey have no home to return to. Everything and everyone they have have ever loved is long since gone.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:516`

## paragon.3004.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3004.a&lang=en)

### 中文

悲惨的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

A sad fate.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:517`

## paragon.3005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

斯克兰德·夏普比克

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

Skrand Sharpbeak

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:519`

## paragon.3005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H利爪一号的舰队司令斯克兰德·夏普比克向我们打了个招呼。他显得精疲力竭。§!\n\n斯卡拉维德陷落了。我的家园不复存在了。数十亿的生命……逝去了。\n\n利爪一号就这么变成了最后一艘利爪号，而我也变成了一个无依无靠的士兵。感谢你们的帮助，我代表我的船员宣誓效忠。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

§HAdmiral Skrand Sharpbeak of the First Talon hails us. He appears exhausted.§!\n\nSkravird has fallen. My home is no more. Billions of lives... gone.\n\nThe First Talon has become The Last Talon, and I am a soldier without a cause. For your help, and on behalf of my crew, I pledge our allegiance.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:520`

## paragon.3005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谢谢。但我们也有值得尊敬的指挥官。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Our thanks. But we have worthy commanders of our own.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:521`

## paragon.3005.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H斯克兰德眨眨眼咳嗽了起来。§!\n\n我当然知道。但你要知道我的服务不是轻易提供的。作为善意的象征，我会指示我的工程师保持利爪号的运转，并教会你的工程师如何维护它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

§HSkrand blinks and coughs.§!\n\nNo doubt. But know that my service is not offered lightly. As a token of goodwill, I will instruct my engineers to keep the Talon operable and teach your engineers how to maintain it.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:522`

## paragon.3005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.b&lang=en)

### 中文

那你有什么能耐阻止我们据为己有吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

What is to prevent us from simply taking it ourselves?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:523`

## paragon.3005.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H夏普比克后退了几步，它的喙发出一连串非常不愉快的吱吱声。§!\n\n这艘船是我已故家乡的荣耀，是工人们用双爪乃至生命建造出来的！这是我们的故土的最后一片残羽，是我们怀念自己幼巢的最后依靠。\n\n§H斯克兰德调整了身姿，冷静下来。§!\n\n简而言之，我会用爪子和喙战斗，至死方休。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

§HSharpbeak recoils, his beak issuing a series of highly unpleasant squawks.§!\n\nThis ship is the pride of my lost homeland, built with the talons of workers who gave their lives to the project! It's the last piece of our home - all that's left to remind us of our fledgling nests.\n\n§HRegaining his posture, Skrand calms his clacking.§!\n\nSuffice to say, I would fight beak and claw to stop you.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:524`

## paragon.3005.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.c&lang=en)

### 中文

雇佣领袖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Hire Leader

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:525`

## paragon.3005.c_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.c_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§L很好。我们接受你的效忠。为荣耀而战吧，司令。§!\n$SEPARATOR$\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

§LVery well. We accept your pledge. Serve with honor, Admiral.§!\n$SEPARATOR$\n

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:526`

## paragon.3005.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H夏普比克舒展了一下羽毛。§!\n\n我会的。利爪终号会以新的身份再度翱翔天际。你让我们找到了继续下去的意义。谢谢你。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

§HSharpbeak spreads his plummage.§!\n\nI shall. The Last Talon will fly again, albeit under new colors. You have restored our purpose. I thank you.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:527`

## paragon.3005.d_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.d_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§L语言总是苍白无力。再会吧，司令。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

§LWords are hollow. Farewell, Admiral.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:528`

## paragon.3005.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.d&lang=en)

解散

### English

Dismiss

## paragon.3005.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3005.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H夏普比克的目光如炬。§!\n\n那也好。利爪终号将会独自翱翔。再见吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

§HSharpbeak's gaze is piercing.§!\n\nVery well. The Last Talon will fly alone. Farewell.

### 中文

胜利！

### English

Victory!

## paragon.3006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的士兵轻易地战胜了外星舰船的船员。我们摧毁了他们的防御，清理掉了舰桥上的敌军士兵。这艘船是我们的了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

Our troops easily overpowered the vessel's crew. We destroyed their defenses and cleared the bridge of enemy soldiers. The ship is ours!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:534`

## paragon.3006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要研究它的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

We shall learn its secrets.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:535`

## paragon.3007.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3007.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大屠杀！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

Massacre!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:536`

## paragon.3007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我方登录外星舰船的部队遭到了残虐！敌人令人难以置信地凶悍。结束战斗后，我们重启了舰船的战术系统。系统显示，超光速引擎仍然无法启动，但舰船本身正在主动争夺我们的控制权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

Our troops aboard the alien vessel were decimated! The ferocity of the defenders was incredible. After the battle, power was returned to the ship's tactical systems. According to our scans, the hyperdrive is still inoperable, but the vessel itself is actively contesting our space.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:537`

## paragon.3007.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3007.a&lang=en)

### 中文

派出舰队！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

Send a fleet!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:538`

## paragon.3902.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3902.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Hulking Ship

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:540`

## paragon.3902.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3902.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在常规调查中，我们发现这艘舰船一直在激活的星门旁警戒，但是对于我们的呼叫没有任何应答，而且其似乎在处于高功率运转状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

During our standard survey, we found a hulking ship standing guard near an activated gate. The vessel is not responding to our summons and appears to be at functioning at critical power levels.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:541`

## paragon.3902.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3902.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开展进一步的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

Try to learn more.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:542`

## tech_strike_craft_skrand_desc

从斯克兰德和他的人那儿学到的设计经验，这些先进的舰载机可以运用灵活适应的战术进行长时间作战。

## paragon.3010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

坏羽病

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

Feather Rot

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:557`

## paragon.3010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着一种新型瘟疫在舰船上散播开来，利爪一号上的船员们开始陷入恐慌。根据初步的分析判断，对于我们的族群而言，这是一种相对无害的病毒。然而，对于船上的鸟类船员来说情况就不一样了，病毒首先附着在羽毛上，导致羽毛根部感染腐烂，不出几天，感染就会蔓延到血液中。\n\n如果任由这样发展下去，最终会达到不可挽回的地步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

Panic grips the crew of the First Talon as a novel disease is sweeping across the ship. According to initial analysis, it's a strain of a relatively harmless virus, common to our people. However, to the avian crew of the Talon, its effects are both serious and disturbing. First manifesting in the plumage, the virus causes feathers to rot at the root. Within days, the rot spreads to the blood.\n\nOver time, it is likely to prove fatal.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:558`

## paragon.3010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究解药！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Work on the cure!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:559`

## paragon.3011.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3011.name&lang=en)

### 中文

英雄之死

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Death of a Hero

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:560`

## paragon.3011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们用尽全力，但斯克兰德还是倒在了坏羽病的侵袭之下。他的同伴们正准备为此举行一场传统的葬礼仪式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Despite our best efforts, Skrand has succumbed to Feather Rot. His crew are preparing a traditional funeral service.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:561`

## paragon.3012.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3012.name&lang=en)

### 中文

疾病蔓延

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Sickness Spreads

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:562`

## paragon.3012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们在利爪一号上采取了严格的隔离措施，但根据斯克兰德刚刚的报告，估计船上约有60%的船员已被感染。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

Despite our best efforts to quarantine the First Talon, Admiral Skrand has contracted a severe case of Feather Rot. We estimate that 60% of his crew is already sick.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:563`

## paragon.3013.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3013.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新症状

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

New Symptoms

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:564`

## paragon.3013.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3013.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

到目前为止，坏羽病的新症状几乎在一夜之间就涌现出来了，这超出了利爪一号自身医务室的处理极限，船上的走廊里挤满了病患还有奄奄一息的船员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

Hitherto unseen symptoms of the Feather Rot epidemic have appeared almost overnight. The First Talon's sickbay is overflowing, and the ship's corridors are full of the sick and dying.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:565`

## paragon.3014.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3014.name&lang=en)

### 中文

状况恶化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

Worsening State

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:566`

## paragon.3014.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3014.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

舰船已经汇报了坏羽病导致的第一例死亡病例。那些鸟类船员目前仍然无法抵御病毒的感染，除非我们能找到治疗方法，不然的话据推算最终死亡率可达95%。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

The first fatalities of the Feather Rot epidemic have been reported. The avians remain unable to fight off the infection on their own, and unless we find a way a cure, we estimate a mortality rate of 95%. 

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:567`

## paragon.3015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

解药找到了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

Cure Found!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:568`

## paragon.3015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在数月的努力后，我们成功治愈了利爪一号上染病的船员们，舰船运转逐步重回正轨。毫无疑问斯克兰德将非常感谢我们在这件事上的帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

After months of work, we were able to administer a successful treatment to the crew of the First Talon. Efforts are underway to up production. Admiral Skrand will no doubt be very grateful for our help in this matter.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:569`

## paragon.3020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

表示感激

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Show of Gratitude

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:571`

## paragon.3020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人员一直在努力促进与斯克兰德和他的船员的关系。这种跨文化交流的努力已经取得初步成效：刚刚斯克兰德同意提供他们高效舰载机的设计。这可能会大大提升我们对太空作战的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

Our personnel have worked hard to foster relations with Skrand and his crewmates. This effort in cross-cultural exchange is already bearing fruit: Skrand has just agreed to hand over the designs for their highly efficient strike craft. This could lead to a great leap in our understanding of space combat.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:572`

## paragon.3020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对该礼物表达感激

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

We are thankful for this gift.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:573`

## paragon.3021.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3021.name&lang=en)

### 中文

已研究外星舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Alien Vessel Studied

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:574`

## paragon.3021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

历时数月的研究、编写和实验终于提供了一些启发。外星飞船的计算机库包含了舰载机编队的适应性战术。现在这项技术由我们来运用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

Months of studying, cataloging, and experimenting finally provided some insight. The alien vessel's computer banks contain adaptive tactics in strike craft formation. Now, this technology is ours to implement.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:575`

## paragon.3021.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3021.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以利用这些。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

We can use these.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:576`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3030.c&lang=en)

让他们解决问题。

### English

Make them work it out.
