## paragon.3031.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3031.name&lang=en)

### 中文

整合结构

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Integrated Structures

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:579`

## paragon.3031.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3031.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们的基层军官和指挥官正在与斯克兰德密切合作，但他们也不是完全言听计从。双方所习惯的指挥结构不同，带来了一些不必要的紧张局面。因此我们亟需一场联合军事演练。\n\n该演练可能会降低我们指挥舰队的能力，但不能准备好军队的话可能会导致极其严重的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

Our officers and commanders are working closely with Skrand, but they are not always in accord with him. Their preferred command structures differ, causing unnecessary tension. This has precipitated a need for joint military training.\n\nThe work may limit our ability to command our fleets, but failing to properly prepare the troops could have dire consequences.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:580`

## paragon.3031.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3031.a&lang=en)

### 中文

准备指挥训练课目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Prepare an admiralty training program.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:581`

## paragon.3031.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3031.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这种军事演习有什么意义么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Such war games are immaterial.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:582`

## paragon.3032.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3032.name&lang=en)

### 中文

训练完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Training Completed

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:583`

## paragon.3032.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3032.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

双方都取得了显著进展，我们认为联合演训项目取得了巨大成功。我们从斯克兰德那里学到了很多关于近距离太空作战的知识，同时他和他的军官们也熟悉了我们的后勤系统。一种新的乐观主义和同志情谊已经在军队中扎根下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

With exceptional progress on both sides, we consider the joint training program a great success. We learned much from Skrand about close-quarters space combat, while bringing him and his officers up to speed on our logistics practices. A new sense of optimism and camaraderie has taken root.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:584`

## paragon.3033.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3033.name&lang=en)

### 中文

军事洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Military Insight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:585`

## paragon.3033.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3033.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

主要问题在于我们使用舰载机的战术截然不同。斯克兰德部署了多个大型攻击机小组来打破敌人的指挥链。他们在母舰的明确目标下联合行动。\n\n我们的方法则更灵活，让他们承担一些态势感知型的任务，并去执行高危险高回报的行动，但我们缺乏斯克兰德和他的军官所拥有的大局观。目前，我们还在努力寻找可以一起建立的共同基础。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

The main problem lies in how differently we employ strike-craft tactics. Skrand deploys multiple large strike-craft teams to break the enemy's chain of command. They operate united by clear objectives from their mother ship.\n\nOur approach is more flexible with roles dictated by situational awareness and the use of potentially high-risk, high-reward actions, but we lack the cohesion seen in Skrand and his officers. For now, we still strive to find a common ground to build on.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:586`

## paragon.3034.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3034.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被遗忘的战术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

Forgotten Tactics

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:587`

## paragon.3034.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3034.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

模拟战斗表明，斯克兰德的集中火力战术在最初阶段非常奏效，但很快会被反制战术所克制。即便如此，他战果也还是比起我们的的表现好得多。\n\n同时实施这两种战术会导致混乱。幸运的是，从这些失败中产生了可能想法，这将缓解潜在的问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

Simulation battles have shown Skrand's concentrated tactics are very effective at first, but quickly fail against counter strategies. Even so, the results were superior to those we could expect ourselves.\n\nImplementing parts of both tactics in parallel resulted in chaos. Fortunately, from these failures emerged promising ideas that should alleviate the underlying problems.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:588`

## paragon.admiral_fight.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.admiral_fight.name&lang=en)

### 中文

指挥问题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

Command Issue

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:589`

## paragon.admiral_fight.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.admiral_fight.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

显然，斯克兰德陷入了与我们自己的一位指挥官[leader_to_fire_skrand.GetName]的肢体冲突。问题与舰队司令的个人荣誉感有关。简而言之，斯克兰德觉得自己受到了侮辱，他表示，除非我们解除[leader_to_fire_skrand.GetName]的职务，否则他将立即离开我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

Apparently Skrand got into a physical altercation with [leader_to_fire_skrand.GetName], one of our own commanders. The issue has to do with the Admiral's deep sense of personal honor. In short, Skrand feels insulted, and has stated he will leave our service immediately unless we relieve [leader_to_fire_skrand.GetName] of their duties.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:590`

## paragon.3040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

潜在升级

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

Potential Upgrades

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:598`

## paragon.3040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对利爪终号的联合研究得到了对这艘钢铁巨兽内部运作方式的独到见解。我们相信我们的研究团队跟利爪号的船员们通力合作将有可能进一步改进舰船的外部结构，让它面对攻击和船体破坏具有更强的抵抗力。\n\n我们已经获得了斯克兰德的允许，但项目还需要更多资金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

Our joint research on the Last Talon has given us unique insights into the inner workings of this behemoth. We believe that our engineering team, in cooperation with those aboard the Talon, have an opportunity to further improve the outer layers of the ship, making it even more resistant to attacks and potential hull breaches.\n\nWe have already received permission from Skrand, but the project will require further funds.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:599`

### 中文

利爪2.0

### English

Talon 2.0

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:602`

## paragon.3041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们没能完成预定目标，但还是成功地改进了利爪号的装甲系统。我们的工程师还发现了可以用于改进我们自己舰船的灵感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Although we didn't achieve the desired results, we managed to improve the Talon's armor systems. Our engineers also found valuable insights that can be applied to improve our own ships.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:603`

## paragon.3042.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3042.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缓慢进展

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Slow Progress

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:604`

## paragon.3042.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3042.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对改进利爪号的结构强度而不牺牲速度或机动性进行了诸多努力，但进展相当缓慢。斯克兰德的工程师虽然帮了我们很多忙，但是他们不愿意我们在没有密切监督的情况下实施我们的想法。尽管如此，工作仍在继续推进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

We have made several different attempts to increase the Talon's structural integrity without compromising speed or maneuverability, but progress has been slow. The assistance of Skrands' engineers has been a great boon to us, but they are reluctant to implement our ideas without close supervision. However, work continues to move forward.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:605`

## paragon.3043.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3043.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不匹配材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Incompatible Materials

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:606`

## paragon.3043.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3043.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利爪号的部分材料和我们的同类产品不同。这些合金的韧性更差，导致船体在安装额外的装甲板时意外断裂。驻船工程师对我们的监督是正确的，幸好意外发生时他们就在附近。\n\n我们需要使用一些原来用于升级的材料来维修船体，这将会导致整个升级工作的延迟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

Some of the Talon's materials differ from our equivalents. Its alloy is much less flexible, which caused the hull to break unexpectedly during the installation of additional armor plates. The ship's engineers were right to oversee our work, and we were lucky they were nearby.\n\nWe had to use some of the materials allocated for improvements to repair the ship, which has in turn delayed the whole operation.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:607`

## paragon.3043.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3043.a&lang=en)

### 中文

调拨更多资金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

Allocate more funding.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:608`

## paragon.3043.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3043.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这是徒劳的，放弃项目吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

It's futile, scrap the project.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:609`

## paragon.3050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最终飞航

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

Final Flight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:611`

## paragon.3050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H闪烁的屏幕上浮现出斯克兰德的指挥界面。这位舰队司令的羽翼蓬立，双眼发出锐利的目光。§!\n\n这些……混账！他们只知道杀戮、破坏！\n\n§H他愤怒地咬紧了喙，继续说。§!\n\n我请求……不，我下令发起战争！我们不能让他们毁掉一切，让我们再次一无所有！我、我相信我们一起可以防止我人民所遭遇的悲剧再度重演。我已经准备好与你们分享我们所知道的一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

§HThe screen flickers as Skrand's command deck appears. The admiral's feathers are ruffled, his sharp eyes flashing.§!\n\nThese... outsiders! They came here only to kill, to destroy!\n\n§HClacking his beak furiously, he continues.§!\n\nI request-- No, I DEMAND that we declare war! We can't let them destroy everything, not again! I... Together I believe we can prevent a repeat of what befell my people. I am ready to teach you everything we know.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:612`

## paragon.3050.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你之前曾和这些威胁交过手吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

Have you come into contact with this threat before?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:613`

## paragon.3050.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

不，并没有。但我以前见过这种情况……这种模式再熟悉不过了。威胁是一样的。如果我们不尽快采取行动，我们将遭受到与我的留守同胞们同样的结局。\n\n§H他全身的羽毛支棱了起来。§!\n\n我不会再让任何人遭遇这种不幸了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

No, not exactly. But I've seen this play out before... The pattern is all too familiar. The threat is the same. If we don't act soon, we will meet the same end that once befell my people.\n\n§HHe ruffles his feathers.§!\n\nI would not wish such a fate on anyone.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:614`

## paragon.3050.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你愿意分享什么信息？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

What info can you share?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:615`

## paragon.3050.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将这种敌人称为“威胁”。他们从不回复任何通讯请求，更不用说外交，任何挡住他们去路的人后果都是死。\n\n§H他发出了一声尖利的嘶鸣。§!\n\n他们不知从何而来突然出现，烧杀抢掠！我们几乎没有还手能力，但我们知道了与这样的敌人作战意味着什么。常规战争是不够的。一不做，二不休。我们必须消灭他们，一个不留，否则就见不到明天了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

We called our enemy the Menace. They never responded to comms requests, let alone diplomacy, and everyone who got in their way wound up dead.\n\n§HHe emits a high-pitched scream.§!\n\nThey appeared out of nowhere, killing, mangling, burning! We had little chance to retaliate, but we learned what it means to fight such an enemy. Conventional warfare isn't enough. It must be total, or nothing. We must destroy all of them, every last one, or there will be no tomorrow.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:616`

## paragon.3050.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们动手吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

Let's do this.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:617`

## paragon.3050.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

时间就是生命。我们必须加快这一进程。我们正面临着彻底毁灭的威胁。我们必须阻止这一切！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Time is of the essence. We must expedite the process. We are facing the threat of total destruction. We must prevent this!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:618`

## paragon.3050.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不能在这里浪费资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

We can't spend resources on this.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:619`

## paragon.3050.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3050.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H斯克兰德尖叫了起来，§!\n\n什么？！不……你犯了个十分严重的错误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

§HSkrand shrieks,§!\n\nWHAT?! No... You're making a terrible mistake.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:620`

## paragon.3051.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3051.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天灾已分析

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Crisis Analyzed

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:621`

## paragon.3051.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3051.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于斯克兰德对“威胁”作战的专长，及其提供的海量数据，我们现在面对这些涌现出来的天灾更加有备无患了。下一步就是将这些知识落到实处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Thanks to Skrand's expertise in warfare against the Menace, and the vast database he has provided, we are now much better equipped to deal with the emerging crisis. All that remains is to put this knowledge to good use.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:622`

## paragon.3052.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3052.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天灾作战方式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

Crisis Warfare

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:623`

## paragon.3052.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3052.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们获得的数据库中可查询到一些斯克兰德的帝国时代这些威胁的作战方式实例。看来，他们使用的策略确实类似于我们今天所面对的那些。他们的舰队不使用正常的物流方案，通常是自给自足的。\n\n更重要的是，干扰技术对他们不怎么起作用。破坏操作同样没有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

The databanks we've accessed provide us with some examples of how the Menace fought during the time of Skrand's empire. It appears that they used tactics similar to those we face today. They rarely use standard logistical schemes, and their fleets are usually self-sufficient.\n\nMore importantly, jamming technology is not as effective against such forces. Sabotage operations are likewise useless.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:624`

## paragon.3053.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3053.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天灾战斗模式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

Crisis Fighting Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:625`

## paragon.3053.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3053.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将我们与斯克兰德的数据进行对比得出了一些有趣的结论。似乎这些敌人相当地古板：它的大型舰队一次只聚焦在一个目标，而忽视其他所有的东西。我们应该可以识别他们的目标，并进行相应的防御。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

Comparing our own data to Skrand's, we have come to some interesting conclusions. It turns out that the enemy is quite inflexible: its largest fleets focus on one target at a time, to the exclusion of all else. It should be possible to identify such targets and defend accordingly.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:626`

## paragon.3060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

利爪号研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

Talon Study

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:628`

## paragon.3060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利爪号上的一群工程师帮传达了一条来自斯克兰德的指令。他们请求我们帮助维护利爪号的框架，并让我们的船坞做好修船的准备。如上所述，这些计划代价高昂。而且还有一个问题：考虑到这项工作的微妙性质，斯克兰德将不得不亲自监督该项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

A group of engineers from the Talon has delivered an order from Skrand himself. They requested help to maintain the Talon's frame and prepare our shipyards for potential repairs. As outlined, these plans would be a costly process. And there is one catch: given the delicate nature of the work, Skrand will have to oversee the project himself.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:629`

## paragon.3060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3060.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会帮忙的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

We will help.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:630`

## paragon.3060.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3060.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们腾不出资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

We can't spare resources.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:631`

## paragon.3061.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3061.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被遗忘的技术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

Forgotten Techniques

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:632`

## paragon.3061.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3061.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们的帮助下，利爪号的工程师成功地对我们的船坞进行了复古改装，以对利爪号的内部结构进行研究。他们利用我们的实验性的设计改进了他们的超光速引擎，使得即使在发生船体严重损坏的情况下，舰船仍然能够跃迁。虽然使用这种引擎会导致几乎瞬间到达目的地，但是它也会杀死船上的所有人，并且可能使舰船在数年内无法使用。另一方面，如果舰船在战斗中损毁，我们依然能可以尝试激活这个系统并修复残骸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

With our help, the Talon's engineers have retrofitted our shipyards to study the Talon's inner workings. Making use of our experimental designs they enhanced their FTL drives, which allow the ship to jump even after a critical hull breach. Although usage of this drive will result in almost instantaneous travel, it will also kill everyone on board and probably render the ship inoperable for years. On the other hand, if the ship is lost in battle, we can try to activate the system and recover the wreckage.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:633`

## paragon.3071.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3071.name&lang=en)

### 中文

利爪号再度启航！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

The Talon Flies Again!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:635`

## paragon.3071.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3071.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在利爪号工程师团队的密切支持和监督下，我们成功地重建了船体。利爪号再度启航！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

With the close support and supervision of the Talon's engineering team, we were able to rebuild the hull. The Talon takes flight again!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:636`

## paragon.3072.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3072.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失而复得的利爪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Lost Talon Recovered

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:637`

## paragon.3072.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3072.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多亏了额外的安全系统以及紧急协议，斯克兰德的旗舰在受到严重损坏后仍成功进行了紧急超光速跃迁。尽管出现在我们首都星球上方的残骸几乎无法被识别为一艘舰船，但船体依然可以作为重建的基础。这些残骸现在正在船坞等待修理。或者，我们可以对船体进行材料回收。考虑到它的状态，即便是我们认定这艘舰船的残骸无法修复的，斯克兰德也会理解这个决定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Thanks to additional failsafe systems and emergency protocols, Skrands' flagship managed to enter emergency FTL even after taking critical damage. Although what appeared in the skies above our capital world is barely recognizable as a ship, the hull can serve as a basis for rebuilding. The remains are now waiting at the docks for repairs. Alternatively, we could the hull for materials. Given its state, even Skrand would understand the decision if we deemed the wreck impossible to repair.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:638`

## paragon.3072.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3072.a&lang=en)

### 中文

修复利爪号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

Repair the Talon.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:639`

## paragon.3072.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3072.b&lang=en)

### 中文

拆了它，我们需要合金。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

Scrap it, we need the alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:640`

## paragon.3080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新希望

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

New Hope

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:642`

## paragon.3080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

近期在基因工程方面的进展有助于重新恢复舰队司令斯克兰德的种族。也许暂时还不具有相应的基因多样性来确保他们继续繁衍，但用兼容的基因填补空白应该可以完成这项工作。我们现在只需要足够的资金就可以开始行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

Recent developments in genetic engineering could enable us to repopulate Admiral Skrand's species. We may lack the genetic diversity to make them fully capable of reproduction, but filling in the gaps with compatible genes should get the job done. All that is required to get started is adequate funding.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:643`

## paragon.3080.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3080.a&lang=en)

### 中文

斯克兰德作出了很多贡献。他完全配得上这个。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

Skrand has served us well. He deserves this much.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:644`

## paragon.3080.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3080.b&lang=en)

我们没空做这些。

### English

We don't have time for this.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:645`

## paragon.3081.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3081.name&lang=en)

### 中文

“斯克兰德”们

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

Skrands

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:646`

## paragon.3081.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3081.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们复制斯克兰德DNA的工作某种意义上还是成功了。尽管他们的外观完全一致，但是克隆体的基因组成与斯克兰德的完全不同，它们构成了一个新物种。他们开始称自己为塔隆人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

Our efforts to replicate Skrand's DNA have been more or less successful. Although their appearance is identical, the genetic makeup of the clones is different enough from Skrand's that they constitute a new species. We are told they have taken to calling themselves the Talons.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:647`

## paragon.3081.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3081.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L塔隆人将会出现在我们的母星上。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

§LThe Talons will appear on our homeworld.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:648`

## paragon.3082.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3082.name&lang=en)

### 中文

担忧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

Concerns

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:649`

## paragon.3082.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3082.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

斯克兰德的鸟类DNA远比预想的要顽固，以至于有必要将其他鸟类的基因拼接起来。这个方法并不理想，但应该能让我们完成这项工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

Skrand's avian DNA is far more rigid than expected, so much so that it has been necessary to splice genes from other avian species. The approach is not ideal, but it should allow us to finish the job.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:650`

## paragon.3083.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3083.name&lang=en)

### 中文

并不相同

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

Not The Same

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:651`

## paragon.3083.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3083.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H斯克兰德的制服一尘不染，他的羽翼油光发亮。§!\n\n我已经看到了你们小小基因实验的成果。虽然这些克隆体还差强人意，但我相当感谢你们的努力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

§HSkrand's uniform is in perfect condition, his feathers well groomed.§!\n\nI saw the result of your little genetic experiment. These clones are far from perfect, but I am grateful for your attempt.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:652`

## paragon.3083.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3083.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对此表示遗憾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

We are sorry to hear that.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:653`

## paragon.3083.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3083.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

不必遗憾。这些克隆体让我们可以重温过去的生活方式。假以时日他们甚至还能孵出雏鸟。你们给予了我们希望。我们对此表示感谢。愿你的羽翼光洁生辉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

Do not be. These clones will provide us with a way to live as we did in the past. In time, it may even be possible to have nestlings. You have given us hope. We thank you for that. May your plumage shine.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:654`

## paragon.3090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

历史研究

### English

History Studies

## paragon.3090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了一份来自许多利爪号船员的请求。他们要求我们调配资源研究他们民族的过往。在利爪号徘徊于亚空间的时候发生了许多许多事情。他们希望能为子孙后代记录下一些东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

We received a request from several members of the Talon's crew. They have asked us to use our resources to research their people's past. Much occurred while the Talon lay in limbo. They have asked that it be recorded for posterity.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:660`

## paragon.3090.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3090.a&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

To the archives!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:661`

我们会通过的。

### English

## paragon.3091.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3091.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大迁徙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

The Migration

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:663`

## paragon.3091.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3091.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了关于斯克兰德和他的人民历史的第一份报告:\n\n在他们的时代，星系广阔而未知，星系外边缘被视为太过危险，无法探索。\n\n在星系中心周围，庞大的帝国在争夺权力。其中一个是鸟类文明。在其他人不敢涉足的黑暗之地，这些勇敢的探险家看到了机遇。大迁徙就这样开始了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

We have the first report on the history of Skrand and his people:\n\nIn their time the galaxy was vast and unknown, the outer rims considered too dangerous to explore.\n\nAround the galactic center, vast empires vied for power. One was an avian civilization. Where others saw darkness, these intrepid explorers glimpsed opportunity. So began the Great Migration.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:664`

## paragon.3092.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3092.name&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎之巢

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

Broken Nest

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:665`

## paragon.3092.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3092.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里附上另一份关于斯克兰德及其人民的历史的报告：\n\n大迁徙在很久以前就结束了。偏安于星系的一隅，斯克兰德的人民们享受着和平而繁荣的日子。但成平日久的他们，没有做好应对威胁的准备。\n\n攻击者不宣而战。没有一双翅膀来得及张开，尖牙立喙也失去了光芒。这些无面入侵者从太空而来，轰炸了鸟类的行星，留下了尸山血海。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

Here follows another report on the history of Skrand and his people:\n\nThe Migration was long over. Secure along the galaxy's outer rim, Skrand's kin enjoyed both peace and prosperity. But having traded vigilance for comfort, they were unprepared for the coming Menace.\n\nThe attackers came without warning. No wings were unfurled; neither talon nor beak flashed in combat. From space the faceless invaders bombed the avian worlds. In their wake they left only death.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:666`

## paragon.3093.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3093.name&lang=en)

### 中文

利爪一号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:590`

### English

First Talon

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:667`

## paragon.3093.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3093.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了下一份关于斯克兰德及其人民的历史的报告：\n\n斯卡拉维德的政府崩溃了，经济也在废墟中陷入停止。但人民的英勇丝毫未减。在远离前线的地方，一个项目正在酝酿之中。\n\n一艘史无前例的舰船将被建造出来。其规模之大足以使敌人胆战心惊。当利爪一号准备就绪时，它被划入了斯卡拉维德最后一位指挥官的指挥之下。年轻又未经考验的斯克兰德·夏普比克上了战场。很快，他的人民就会把他称为威胁克星，斯卡拉维德的第一利爪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

We've received another report on the history of Skrand and his people:\n\nSkravird's government collapsed. Its economy lay in ruins. But the valor of its people was undiminished. Far from the front lines, a project was hatched.\n\nA ship would be built unlike any that had come before. Its sheer scale would strike terror into enemy. When the First Talon was ready, it was placed under the command of the last of Skravird's commanders. Young and untested, Skrand Sharpbeak went to war. Soon his people would hail him as Menace Bane, First Claw of Skravird.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:668`

## paragon.3094.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3094.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最终飞航

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

Final Flight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:669`

## paragon.3094.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3094.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

以下是斯克兰德和他的人民历史的最后一份汇报：\n\n利爪一号，同时也是最后一号，穿过敌人的阵线。船员和指挥官的精明和勇敢带来了一场又一场的胜利。然而，它的航程是短暂的：即使是像利爪号这样的船，也不可能凭一己之力扭转战局。无奈之下，斯克兰德和他的军官们计划在斯克兰德的最后一次防御战中玉石俱焚。在一次包抄敌人的大胆行动中，斯克兰德命令利爪号进入一个未经测试的星门。他和利爪号便从此失去了音信。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:593`

### English

Thus reads the final report on the history of Skrand and his people:\n\nThe First Talon, first and last of its line, cut through the enemy ranks. Battle after battle was won by the cunning and valor of its crew and their commander. Alas, its flight was short lived: no ship, not even one such as the Talon, could turn the tide of war on its own. In desperation, Skrand and his officers planned to sacrifice themselves in the last defense of Skravird. In a daring bid to outflank the enemy, Skrand ordered the Talon into an untested Gateway. Neither he nor the Talon were ever heard from again.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:670`

## situation_paragon_skrand_sickness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_paragon_skrand_sickness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被称为“坏羽病”的致命疾病影响了斯克兰德的大部分船员。我们的预见到了结果的严峻。目前，我们只能通过提供适当的护理和处理症状来限制疾病的传播。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

The virulent disease known as Feather Rot has affected most of Skrand's crew. Our forecasts predict a grim outcome. For now, we can only limit the spread of the disease by providing proper care and dealing with the symptoms.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:680`

## approach_situation_paragon_skrand_sickness_nothing_tt

## situation_paragon_skrand_cure_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_paragon_skrand_cure_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就像其他任何新疾病，要应对坏羽病，研究总是第一位的。科研团队正在夜以继日地工作寻找治愈方法，我们的医疗人员则在竭力缓解症状，延缓疫情的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

As with any new disease, lab work is paramount in combating Feather Rot. Research teams are working tirelessly to find a cure, while our medical personnel are fighting to ease symptoms and limit the spread of the disease.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:693`

## situation_paragon_skrand_cure_regular

### English

## approach_situation_paragon_skrand_cure_regular_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapproach_situation_paragon_skrand_cure_regular_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们基于新型传染病和医疗保健的经验组建了一系列研究团队。他们的目标是找到治愈方法，同时也要优先其他任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

Research teams are assembled based on experience in novel diseases and health care. Their goal is to find a cure while also prioritizing other tasks.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:695`

## approach_situation_paragon_skrand_cure_generous_tt

### 中文

我们基于新型传染病和医疗保健的经验组建了一系列研究团队。他们的首要目标是找到治愈方法，而不是缓解症状或阻止疫情蔓延。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

Research teams are assembled based on experience in novel diseases and health care. Priority is placed on finding a cure rather than easing symptoms or limiting the spread of the disease.

## situation_paragon_skrand_integration_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_paragon_skrand_integration_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

指挥整合计划关注与缓解海军指挥层的内斗，特别是斯克兰德和我们的人之间的矛盾。寻找到紧张局面的根源可以改善我们指挥流程的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

The command integration project focuses on easing infighting within the admiralty, especially between Skrand and our own personnel. Addressing the root causes of the tension could improve the efficacy of our command processes.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:705`

## situation_paragon_skrand_crisis_desc

### 中文

我们被一股强大的力量所袭扰，这些东西试图摧毁我们所熟知的星系中的一切生命。斯克兰德提供了他的专业知识以及利爪号日志中相关的数据，但我们还需要一段时间才能完成全面的分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

We are beset by powerful forces that seek to destroy life in the galaxy as we know it. Skrand has offered his expertise as well as relevant data from the Talon's logs, but it will be some time before we can complete a comprehensive analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:715`

### English

## origin_legendary_leader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_legendary_leader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在暴力和苦难中，这个文明在一位领袖的领导下走向了统一。通过压制较弱的派系来巩固权力，他们现在成为了科学和技术进步的坚定倡导者。在这之后，是一段不稳定的和平的时期。但和平终究是一种短暂的状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

Amidst violence and hardship, this civilization was unified by a single leader. Consolidating power through the repression of weaker factions, they have now become a fierce advocate for science and technological advancement. In the aftermath, an uneasy peace has descended. But peace rarely lasts.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:756`

## origin_tooltip_legendary_leader_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_tooltip_legendary_leader_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—$SPECIES_TRAITS$['concept_perfected_genes']$NEW_LINE$—统治者特质：['concept_leader_trait_legendary_leader']

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

- $SPECIES_TRAITS$ ['concept_perfected_genes'] $NEW_LINE$- Ruler Traits: ['concept_leader_trait_legendary_leader']

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:757`

## START_SCREEN_LEGENDARY_LEADER

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_LEGENDARY_LEADER&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的文明总是冲突不断。帝国和王国一个又一个的崛起，又往往一个接一个在内乱的压力下崩溃消亡。\n\n当我们的星球又一次被战火所吞噬时，这种极端的文化再次达到了顶峰。就在一片混沌当中，一位领袖出现了。[owner.Ruler.GetTitle][owner.Ruler.GetName]用[owner.Ruler.GetHisHer]的无私奉献、人格魅力和权力制度成功地组织其了追随者。终于有那么一位能够稳定地联合起我们的人了。\n\n统一是一个漫长而痛苦的过程，但在[owner.Ruler.GetTitle]对星际文明未来的允诺下，挑起战火的势力一个接着一个投降并被纳入麾下。我们第一次拥有了长达上百年的和平，但在表面下，各种新仇旧怨也正在暗流涌动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

Our civilization has always been prone to conflict. Empires and kingdoms rose, but never lasted, always collapsing under the pressure of internal strife.\n\nThis culture of polarization climaxed when our world was consumed by war. Amidst chaos, a leader emerged. The [owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [owner.Ruler.GetName] managed to assemble a gathering of followers who were drawn to [owner.Ruler.GetHisHer] selflessness, charisma and authority. Finally someone was poised to unite us.\n\nThe Unification was a long and painful process, but one after another the warring factions eventually surrendered and fell in line, cowed by the [owner.Ruler.GetTitle]'s promises of a future amongst the stars. For the first time in centuries, we have peace, but under the surface, the scars of the old grievances run deep.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:758`

### 中文

### 中文

## paragon_origin_residential_monument_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_residential_monument_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座住宅纪念碑象征着我们敬爱的统治者的智慧。它既是一件艺术品，也是一个舒适的居住场所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

This residential monument demonstrates the wisdom of our beloved ruler. It is both a piece of art and a comfortable place to live.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:791`

海盗袭击

## paragon_origin_pirates_desc

### 中文

多股海盗实力互相勾结，开始袭扰我们的边境。该局势会严重危害我们的太空航路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

Various pirate groups have banded together to conduct smuggling operations on our borders. The situation has greatly endangered our space routes.

Released Prisoners

## paragon_origin_prisoners_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_prisoners_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改革派要求在全国范围内进行刑事赦免。虽然大多数人是作为政治异议者被关押的，但毫无疑问的是，一些危险分子也被释放了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

Reformists have demanded empire-wide criminal pardons. While most were being held as political dissidents, there is no doubt that some dangerous individuals were also released.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:795`

## paragon_origin_comms_down

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_comms_down&lang=en)

通讯阵列故障

### English

Faulty Comms Arrays

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:796`

## paragon_origin_comms_down_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_comms_down_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的标准通讯阵列有故障，无非继续为我们边境上遥远的殖民地提供稳定的连接。\n\n完成特殊项目以移除$paragon_origin_comms_down$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

Our standard communication arrays are faulty. They no longer provide proper connection to the faraway colonies on the edges of our borders.\n\nComplete Special Project to remove $paragon_origin_comms_down$.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:797`

## paragon_origin_worker_strike

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_worker_strike&lang=en)

### 中文

工人罢工

### English

Worker Strike

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:798`

## paragon_origin_worker_strike_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_worker_strike_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

工人负担过重、情绪低落，无法再完成额外的订单。我们公民所需要完成的巨额工作量显然正在酿成苦果。\n\n完成特殊项目以移除$paragon_origin_worker_strike$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

Workers are overburdened, unhappy, and unable to meet excessive quotas. The titanic effort required of our citizens is clearly taking its toll.\n\nComplete Special Project to remove $paragon_origin_worker_strike$.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:799`

## paragon_origin_military_dissidents

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_military_dissidents&lang=en)

### 中文

军界异见者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

Military Dissidents

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:800`

## paragon_origin_military_dissidents_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_military_dissidents_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传言称有一群低级军官对我们帝国目前的状态抱有异见。这群人正在招募更多的公民加入他们的行列。\n\n完成特殊项目以移除$paragon_origin_military_dissidents$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Rumors indicate that a group of low-ranking military officers are dissatisfied with our empire's current approach. The group is trying to recruit more citizens to join their cause.\n\nComplete Special Project to remove $paragon_origin_military_dissidents$.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:801`

## paragon_origin_nepotism

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_nepotism&lang=en)

### 中文

猖獗的裙带关系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

Rampant Nepotism

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:803`

## paragon_origin_nepotism_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_nepotism_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的帝国被猖獗的裙带关系所困扰，严重阻碍了正常运作。职位被朋友、家人和各种有关系的人填补，而不是交由德才兼备的人。\n\n完成特殊项目以移除$paragon_origin_nepotism$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Our empire is plagued by rampant nepotism, which hinders our operations. Positions are filled by friends, family members, and people with connections - not by those of merit.\n\nComplete Special Project to remove $paragon_origin_nepotism$.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:804`

## paragon_origin_forgotten_deal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_forgotten_deal&lang=en)

### 中文

宪政自由

### English

Constitutional Freedoms

## paragon_origin_forgotten_deal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_forgotten_deal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做了个交易来安抚我们的敌人。我们做出了让步，给予了自由，但我们必须确保他们不会滥用这一安排。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

We made a deal to placate our enemies. Concessions were made, and freedoms were granted, but we must ensure that they don't abuse this arrangement.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:806`

## paragon_origin_reformists_deal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_reformists_deal&lang=en)

### 中文

宪政自由

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

Constitutional Freedoms

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:807`

## paragon_origin_reformists_deal_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_reformists_deal_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做了个交易来安抚敌人。我们做出了让步，包括允许他们自由地推行自己的诸多议程等等。他们将得到特殊保护，并允许对一些立法机构施加影响。从积极的方面来看，我们的公民将从中受益，并为他们新获得的自由感到高兴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

We made a deal to placate our enemies. Concessions were made, including granting them the freedom to pursue many of their agendas. They will receive special protections and influence over some of our legislative bodies. On the positive side, our citizens will reap some of the benefits, and are happy with their newfound freedoms.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:808`

## paragon_origin_reformists

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_reformists&lang=en)

民主倾向

### English

Democratic Tendencies

有报道称，民主派正在迅速获得支持者。我们的安全部队目前正在观察他们的成长和行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

Reports state that the democratic faction is quickly gaining supporters. Our security forces are currently observing their growth and behavior.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:810`

## paragon_origin_broken_promise_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_broken_promise_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于允诺落空，整个帝国几乎每颗星球上的普通公民都在抗议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

## paragon_origin_saddening_grief

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_saddening_grief&lang=en)

### 中文

一个巨大的损失

### English

A Great Loss

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:815`

## paragon_origin_saddening_grief_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_saddening_grief_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

光耀者死了! 这个消息震撼了民众。突如其来的失去领袖让社会陷入了困境，因为人们提出了多项改革，政客们也在争夺权力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

The Luminary is dead! This news has rocked the populace. The sudden lack of leadership has left society reeling, as multiple reforms are proposed and politicians contend for power.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:816`

## paragon_origin_rebel_player

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_player&lang=en)

### 中文

革命精神

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

Revolutionary Spirit

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:817`

## paragon_origin_rebel_player_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_player_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是一个重新获得平等和团结意识的民族。我们比以往任何时候都更加强大，摆脱了暴君的统治，无论好坏，我们都将独立自主。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:722`

### English

We are a people renewed with a sense of equality and unity. Stronger than ever, and freed from our despot, we will rule on our own, for better or worse.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:818`

## paragon_origin_rebel_militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_militarist&lang=en)

### 中文

自由斗士

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

Freedom Fighters

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:819`

## paragon_origin_rebel_militarist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_militarist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们仰望星空，鼓舞他人，解放他人。我们焕然一新的海军已经准备好将平等带到星系最遥远的角落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

We look to the stars and beyond, to uplift and free others. Our renewed navy is ready to bring equality to the farthest corners of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:820`

## paragon_origin_rebel_pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

和谐社会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

Harmonized Society

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:821`

## paragon_origin_rebel_pacifist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_pacifist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

寻找内心的平静一直是我们社群的目标，而我们最终实现了。虽然我们为此付出了高昂的代价，但现在是时候收获回报了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

Finding inner peace has always been the goal of our community, and we finally managed to achieve it. Although we paid for it dearly, now it is time to reap the rewards.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:822`

## paragon_origin_rebel_xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

封闭社会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

Closed Society

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:823`

## paragon_origin_rebel_xenophobe_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_xenophobe_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近的动荡向我们表明，向外部势力开放可能会带来冲突和痛苦。我们需要在未来保护自己不受此类威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

The recent upheaval showed us that being open to external powers can bring strife and misery. We need to safeguard ourselves from such threats in the future.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:824`

## paragon_origin_rebel_xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

开放社会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

Open Society

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:825`

## paragon_origin_rebel_xenophile_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_xenophile_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近的动乱表明，封闭于外部力量会导致冲突和痛苦。我们需要开放自己，这样我们就不会在未来错过类似的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

The recent upheaval showed us that being closed to external forces can lead to strife and misery. We need to open ourselves up so we do not miss similar opportunities in the future.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:826`

## paragon_origin_rebel_materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

发展导向

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

Progress Oriented

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:827`

## paragon_origin_rebel_materialist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_materialist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在星系内战的战火中铸就的新社会知道他们工作的价值。我们一起努力定能实现我们所有的目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

The new society, forged in the fires of galactic civil war, knows the worth of their work. Together we strive to accomplish all of our goals.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:828`

## paragon_origin_rebel_spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

禁欲法则

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

Ascetic Approach

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:829`

## paragon_origin_rebel_spiritualist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_rebel_spiritualist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们经历了惨痛的教训。内战教会了我们克制和内心平静的重要性。必须坚决反对一切腐败和堕落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

We learned our lessons the hard way. Our civil war taught us restraint and the importance of inner peace. Decadence and corruption must be opposed at every turn.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:830`

## paragon_origin_fresh_start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_fresh_start&lang=en)

### 中文

统一帝国

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:735`

### English

Unified Empire

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:831`

## paragon_origin_fresh_start_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_fresh_start_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自大一统以来，我们的公民第一次真正站在一起。经过最近的改革，我们的帝国比以往任何时候都强大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

For the first time since the Unification, our citizens truly stand together. With the recent reforms, our empire is stronger than ever.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:832`

## paragon_origin_one_system_war_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_one_system_war_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过多年的内乱，人民终于团结起来了。而如今，当我们把目光转向星空时，一种新的冲突来源出现了。我们再次遭受战争的蹂躏，争权夺势迫在眉睫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

After long years of civil strife, the population was at last united. But now, as we turn our gaze to the stars, a new source of conflict emerges. Once more we are ravaged by war, and the struggle for supremacy is upon us.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:834`

## paragon_origin_united_through_war

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_united_through_war&lang=en)

### 中文

战火铸成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

Forged in Flames

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:835`

## paragon_origin_united_through_war_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_united_through_war_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

仅仅把我们的人民团结在太空殖民的共同事业中是不够的。虽然这个国家饱受内战的蹂躏，但效忠者们坚定地支持着统治者，面对威胁他们生活方式和我们敬爱的统治者的叛徒时，他们挺身而出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

It was not enough to unite our people in the common cause of space colonization. Though the nation has been ravaged by civil war, loyalists are steadfast in their support of the throne, standing strong in the face of traitors who threaten their way of life and our beloved ruler.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:836`

## council_in_tatter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acouncil_in_tatter&lang=en)

内阁不堪重负

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

Overburdened Council

## council_in_tatter_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acouncil_in_tatter_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

鉴于近年来对行政的需求保持这令人难以置信的增长态势，内阁难以适应其日益增长的职责压力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Given the tremendous bureaucratic strain in recent years to sustain incredible growth, the council has encountered difficulties adjusting to their growing responsibilities.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:838`

### English

## d_arcane_device_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_arcane_device_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这台古老的机器已经深深根植于我们行星上的政府避难所。作为我们敬爱的统治者的聪明才智的遗迹，其物质复制技术仍然是我们无法理解的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

This ancient machine is nestled deep within government vaults on our planet. A relic of the ingenuity of our beloved ruler, its matter replication technology remains incomprehensible to us.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:840`

## sustaining_apparatus

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asustaining_apparatus&lang=en)

### 中文

维持装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

Sustaining Apparatus

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:841`

## sustaining_apparatus_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asustaining_apparatus_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这台非凡的装置深藏在政府大楼里。作为我们敬爱的统治者的聪明才智的遗珍，它的内部工作原理对我们来说几乎是魔法。它能让我们维持统治者的生命。这种胶囊只适合一种特定的DNA序列。失去或迁移首都对依赖其生存的人来说就意味着死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

This extraordinary contraption is nestled deep within the government building. A relic of the ingenuity of our beloved ruler, its inner workings are almost magical to us. It allows us to sustain the life of our ruler. The capsule is tailored to only one specific DNA sequence. Losing or moving our capital will mean certain death for the one encased within it.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:842`

## leader_trait_legendary_leader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_legendary_leader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§G光耀者会随着时间的推移而不断增强力量。§!\n\n统一战争相当地残酷。最后，是[Root.GetName]将和平和意义带给了我们。光耀者是一座象征着我们整个物种所能达成成就的活着的丰碑，也激励着所有的人。[This.Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Desc]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

§GThe Luminary grows in power over time.§!\n\nThe Unification Wars were a brutal affair. In the end, [Root.GetName] brought peace and purpose to us all. The Luminary is the living symbol of what our species can achieve, and an inspiration to all.[This.Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Desc]

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:851`

### 中文

## paragon_origin_psionic_ascension

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_psionic_ascension&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n§P能量外溢：§!\n在一次灵能仪式之后，这位领袖成为了通往虚境的媒介。我们所有人的灵能被引导到[Root.GetHimHer]身上，使这位领袖成为一尊准神。然而，这种力量是有代价的，我们必须牺牲其他灵能个体来维持媒介渠道。\n十年间维护费：§R[owner.paragon_origin_ascension]§!£pop£ $POPS$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

\n\n§PPower Overflowing:§!\nAfter a psionic ritual, this leader became a conduit to the Shroud. The psionic energy of our entire people is channeled into [Root.GetHimHer], making this leader a quasi-divine being. However, such power comes at a cost, and we must sacrifice other psionic individuals to maintain the conduit.\nDecennial Upkeep: §R[owner.paragon_origin_ascension]§! £pop£ $POPS$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:859`

## paragon_origin_cybernetic_ascension

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_cybernetic_ascension&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n§P地核之心：§!\n从地核中形成，这一独特的义体植入体需要持续输入£sr_dark_matter£$sr_dark_matter$来维持其有效性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

\n\n§PCore Heart:§!\nFormed from a planetary core, this unique cybernetic implant requires a constant supply of £sr_dark_matter£ $sr_dark_matter$ to maintain its efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:860`

## paragon_origin_synthetic_ascension

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_synthetic_ascension&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n§P肉体永恒：§!\n通过创建一台独特的正电子脑并将其装入活性金属躯体，我们将$leader_trait_legendary_leader$复活了。为了维系[Root.GetHimHer]的存在，我们需要持续供应£sr_living_metal£$sr_living_metal$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

\n\n§PFlesh Eternal:§!\nBy creating a unique positronic brain and encasing it inside a Living Metal body, we brought the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ back to life. To sustain [Root.GetHimHer] we need a constant supply of £sr_living_metal£ $sr_living_metal$.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:861`

## paragon_origin_genetic_ascension

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_genetic_ascension&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n§P重铸基因：§!\n在无数次尝试将$leader_trait_legendary_leader$的基因拼接如其他天赋异禀的个体中，一位试验品成功地在手术中存活下来并呈现出了有益形状。然而，为了维持遗传性状的稳定性需要定期注入新的基因材料。\n十年度维护费：§R[owner.paragon_origin_ascension]§!£pop£ $POPS$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

\n\n§PRestored Genes:§!\nAfter several attempts at splicing the DNA of the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ into other talented individuals, a single patient managed to survive the operation and develop beneficial traits. However, new genetic material needs to be injected regularly to maintain genomic stability.\nDecennial Upkeep: §R[owner.paragon_origin_ascension]§! £pop£ $POPS$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:862`

## paragon.5004.name

### 中文

允诺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

The Promise

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:934`

## paragon.5004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!结束了我们的内斗，带来了大一统。[Owner.Ruler.GetHeSheCap]在一个爱国团结的帝国中上独掌大权，允诺带领我们去到繁星间，并创建一个伟大的星际帝国。\n\n今天，我们的人民已经失去了耐心，异见的声音正在拷问我们的领袖是否有能力胜任。内阁准备了一个三十年的计划来让他们息声，并带领我们的物种走向一个新的时代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! put an end to our internal strife and brought about the Unification. [Owner.Ruler.GetHeSheCap] came to power on a platform of patriotic unity and imperialism, promising to lead us into the stars and create a grand galactic empire.\n\nToday, our people grow impatient, and dissident voices question our leadership abilities. The council has prepared several 40-year plans that will put these doubts to rest, and propel us into the next age of our species.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:935`

## paragon_origin_promise_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_promise_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在大一统期间，§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!发誓总有一天我们的人民将会在其他的星球定居。对许多人而言，这一誓言成了一种先知式的预言。而其他人却很怀疑这能否真的实现。尽管最近超光速科技已经取得了进展，有一小撮人开始利用这一尚未兑现的诺言来破坏§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的统治。\n\n让这些异见者闭嘴的最好办法就是证明他们是错的。我们会启程去往星间，在新的星球定居，将我们的疆域拓展到越远越好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

During the Unification period, §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! swore that one day our people would settle on other planets. To many, that vow has become an almost prophetic promise. Others doubt it will ever become a reality. Recently, and despite advances in FTL technology, some have started using this unfulfilled promise to undermine the rule of §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!.\n\nThe surest way to silence these dissidents is to prove them wrong. We will take to the stars, settle new worlds and expand our borders as much as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:941`

## paragon_origin_promise_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_promise_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须履行承诺。[owner.GetName]子民们指望我们在未来40年内进入太空帝国的阶段。我们必须努力通过§G控制恒星系§!、§G殖民行星§!来扩张国家，如果遇到其他帝国则还需要§G签订防御条约§!。\n\n§G40年§!之后，我们每取得§G15点进度§!，我们的统治者§H$leader_trait_legendary_leader$特质§!就会得到进一步强化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:852`

### English

We must deliver on our promises. [owner.GetName] citizens are counting on us to grow into a spacefaring empire in the coming 40 years. We should strive to expand our nation by §Gcontrolling systems§!, §Gcolonizing planets§!, and - if we encounter other empires - §Gsigning defense pacts§!.\n\nAfter §G40 years§!, our Ruler's §H$leader_trait_legendary_leader$ Trait§! will improve for every §G15 points§! of progress we've made.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:955`

## council_agenda_origin_legendary_leader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acouncil_agenda_origin_legendary_leader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

40年计划是我们帝国制胜的关键。在光耀者的指导下我们劲往一处使，就会取得远比我们单独行动更伟大的成就。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

## council_agenda_origin_legendary_leader_effect

Adds Approaches to the §YUnifying Promise§! Situation.\nGives one random Luminary Insight effect.\nEthic-based Agendas gain a Luminary Insight effect for the duration of the §YUnifing Promise§! Situation.

### 中文

占领新星球

### English

New World Claimed

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:963`

## paragon.5005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新的殖民地是履行§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!允诺的第一步。\n\n殖民船被永久地转化成了新定居点的行政中心。我们正如火如荼地将其反应堆核心连接进电网，为殖民地发展提供能源。\n\n随着无数的殖民者从船上登陆，围绕着殖民船巨大的船体，各种简易搭盖的住所和棚户如雨后春笋般出现。以这些建筑作为基础，有朝一日此地将会成为[Root.GetName]上的第一座[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]城市。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

This new colony is the first step towards fulfilling the §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!'s promise.\n\nThe colony ship has been permanently converted into the administrative headquarters of the new settlement. Work has begun to connect its reactor core to the power grid to supply the growing colony with energy.\n\nAs colonists disembark in large numbers, makeshift dwellings and shelters have been erected around the former starship's massive hull. These will form the basis of what will one day be the first [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] city on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:964`

## paragon.5008.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5008.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚假允诺

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

False Promises

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:966`

## paragon.5008.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5008.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n在接下来的信息中，[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName]告诉[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]应当放下旧的疑虑，展望未来。[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetSheHeCap]指出了我们共同取得的伟大成就，并指出让我们的过去错误阻碍我们前进是极其不合理的。如果[Root.Owner.GetName]能够团结一致，就没有什么是我们无法克服的！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nIn the message that follows, [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName] tells the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] to cast aside old doubts and look to the future. [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetSheHeCap] points to the great things we have achieved together, and to the irrationality in allowing our past mistakes to hold us back from progress. If the [Root.Owner.GetName] stand together as one, there is nothing we cannot overcome!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:967`

## paragon.5009.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5009.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n在接下来的信息中，[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName]在[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]面前赞扬了某些特定个人的成就——包括[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetHerHis]自己的成就。[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetSheHeCap]接着列举了我们共同取得的伟大成功，从一次光荣的成功到下一次，构筑起一个更加美好的未来。\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:866`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nIn the message that follows, [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName] regales the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] with praise and stories on select individuals' accomplishments - including [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetHerHis] own. [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetSheHeCap] goes on to list what we have achieved together, one glorious success after the other, building up to a promise of an even greater future.\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:971`

## paragon.5010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]上新建的城市是一道靓丽的风景线。凭借奋斗与决心，殖民者成功地在新星球发展兴旺。\n\n[Root.GetName]上的[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]决定建造一座纪念§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的纪念碑以彰显忠诚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

The new cities built on [Root.GetName] are a sight to behold. Through grit and determination, the colonists have managed to thrive on a new world.\n\n[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] citizens on [Root.GetName] decided to show their loyalty by erecting a monument to honor §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:976`

### English

### English

## paragon.5011.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5011.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]庆典！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:877`

### English

The [Root.GetName] Celebrates!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:984`

## paragon.5011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[Root.Owner.GetName]每一块国有的屏幕被打断了播放安排，开始广播一段来自[Root.GetName]的官方公告。§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!站立在一座未揭幕的纪念碑前。§!\n\n今日，我们创造了历史，我们的物种过去从未达成过此般成就。在我的带领和你们辛勤的工作下，没有任何事物可以阻挡我们！[Root.GetName]上的殖民者想赞扬我，但今天，我想感谢你们每一位的辛勤劳动。[Root.Owner.GetName]万岁！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:878`

### English

§HEvery state-owned screen in the [Root.Owner.GetName] interrupts its scheduled stream to broadcast an official communication from [Root.GetName]. §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! stands in front of a veiled monument.§!\n\nToday, we have made history. Never before has our species realized a grander achievement. With my guidance, with your hard work, no obstacle shall stand in our way! The colonists of [Root.GetName] wished to honor me, and I thank them. Glory to the [Root.Owner.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:985`

## paragon.5011.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5011.a&lang=en)

### 中文

万岁！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:879`

### English

Glory!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:986`

## paragon.5011.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5011.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H随着这座§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的纪念碑揭幕，人群中爆发出了欢呼和掌声。§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!自豪地伫立着，眺望着地平线，以及我们那光辉的未来。这是我们第一次征服了一颗新的星球！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:880`

### English

§HThe crowd erupts in cheers as the monument of §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! is unveiled. §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§! stands proudly, looking towards the horizon and the glorious future that awaits us. For the first time, we have conquered a new world!§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:987`

## paragon.5012.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5012.name&lang=en)
