[Root.Owner.GetName]庆典！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:881`

### English

The [Root.Owner.GetName] Celebrates!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:989`

## paragon.5012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[Root.Owner.GetName]每一块国有的屏幕突然中断了正常的播放，然后开始广播一段来自[Root.GetName]的官方公告。画面中，§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!站立在一座未揭幕的纪念碑前。§!\n\n今日，我们创造了历史，我们的物种过去从未达成过此般成就。在我的带领和你们辛勤的工作下，没有任何事物可以阻挡我们！[Root.GetName]上的居民想以此赞扬我，但今天，我们应为在一起奋斗过感到光荣，让我们携手步入成功！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

§HEvery state-owned screen in the [Root.Owner.GetName] interrupts its scheduled stream to broadcast an official communication from [Root.GetName]. §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! stands in front of a veiled monument.§!\n\nToday, we have made history. Never before has our species realized a grander achievement. With my guidance, with your hard work, no obstacle shall stand in our way! The colonists of [Root.GetName] wanted to honor me, but today, all are honored equally. United we triumph!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:990`

## paragon.5012.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5012.a&lang=en)

### 中文

团结就是胜利！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

United!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:991`

## paragon.5012.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5012.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H随着这座以§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!被其他[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]英烈环绕着为主旨的纪念碑揭幕，人群中爆发出了欢呼和掌声。他们团结地矗立着，一个国家凝聚在了一个共同的目标上。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

§HThe crowd erupts in cheers as the monument of §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!, flanked by the heroic frames of other [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] is unveiled. They stand united, a nation focused on a common goal.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:992`

## paragon.5013.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5013.name&lang=en)

### 中文

纪念[Root.GetName]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

The [Root.GetName] is Commemorated!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:994`

## paragon.5013.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5013.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[Root.Owner.GetName]每一块国有的屏幕突然中断了正常的播放，然后开始广播一段来自[Root.GetName]的官方公告。画面中，§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!站立在一座未揭幕的纪念碑前。§!\n\n今日，我们创造了历史，我们的物种过去从未达成过此般成就。在我的带领和你们辛勤的工作下，没有任何事物可以阻挡我们！[Root.GetName]上的居民想以此赞扬我，但他们才是我们人民真正的英雄。我只不过是一介人民公仆，有了你们，我们才能迈入光明的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

§HEvery state-owned screen in the [Root.Owner.GetName] interrupts its scheduled stream to broadcast an official communication from [Root.GetName]. §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! stands in front of a veiled monument.§!\n\nToday, we have made history. Never before has our species realized a grander achievement. The people of [Root.GetName] wanted to honor me today, but they are the real heroes of our people. I am but a servant of our nation. Thanks to you, we take another step towards a brighter future!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:995`

## paragon.5013.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5013.a&lang=en)

### 中文

太谦虚了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

Such humility!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:996`

## paragon.5013.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5013.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是我拒绝让这些自豪的殖民者以我的名义建造一座纪念碑的原因。相反，这些资金将被用在城市建设上。繁荣在等待我们！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:888`

### English

This is why I refused to have these proud colonists build a monument in my name. Instead, the funds are to be used for city building. Prosperity awaits!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:997`

## paragon.5014.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5014.name&lang=en)

### 中文

过劳的内阁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

Overworked Council

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:999`

## paragon.5014.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5014.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

内阁已经不堪重负。\n\n尽管§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的能量似乎无穷无尽，但治理一个星际帝国的工作量已使内阁成员们超负荷工作。尽管如此，仍然有一些问题尚未解决，有必要将一部分担子分出去，让其他人处理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

The council is overwhelmed.\n\nWhile §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s energy seems unlimited, the councilors are overworked by the demands of a galactic empire. Issues have been left unresolved, and it will be necessary to pick up some of the slack.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1000`

## paragon.5014.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5014.a&lang=en)

我们会克服困难的。

### English

We will overcome this challenge.

### English

## paragon.5110.desc

### 中文

我们收到了许多关于海盗袭扰我们贸易路线的报道。内阁正在请求军事支援来应对这些威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:893`

### English

We have received multiple reports of pirates plaguing our trade routes. The council has asked for military support to deal with this menace.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1004`

通讯中断

### English

Communications Down

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1007`

## paragon.5120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一些殖民者和深空研究团队对我们的通讯系统颇有微词，他们已经有好几天收不到消息了。工程师建议在首都建造一座通讯中继器来增强信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

Some colonists and deep space research teams are criticizing our communication systems - there are days when they can't get any message through. Engineers suggest building a communication relay in our capital system to boost the signal.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1008`

## paragon.5120.a

### English

## paragon.5121.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5121.name&lang=en)

### 中文

信号增强器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

Signal Booster

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1011`

## paragon.5121.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5121.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

信号增强器已经激活。初步测试证明，它足以确保在我们的太空中进行稳定通信。\n\n现在看来，之前的干扰是有意为之的破坏行为。虽然破坏者的身份仍未知晓，但新系统应该能够保护我们免受任何进一步的干扰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

The signal booster has been activated. The first tests prove that it should be enough to ensure stable communication across our space.\n\nIt now appears that the previous disruptions were caused by intentional acts of sabotage. While the perpetrators remain unknown, the new system should protect us against any further disruption.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1012`

## paragon.5121.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5121.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们夺回了自己的声音。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

We have regained our voices.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1013`

## paragon.5130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工人罢工

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:901`

### English

Worker Strike

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1015`

## paragon.5130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

深空工人正在罢工，抗议严峻的工作环境。他们要求增加休息时间并提升工作安全管理。作为回应，我们提议给予他们数天全休日，并在首都星球举行一场盛大游行，重新点燃他们的民族自豪感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:902`

### English

Deep-space workers are striking in protest against harsh conditions. They demand more free time and increased safety regulations. In response, we have proposed a slate of work-free days and a grand parade on our capital world to reignite a sense of national pride.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1016`

## paragon.5130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5130.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就这么办！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:903`

### English

Get to it!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1017`

## paragon.5131.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5131.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大游行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

Grand Parade

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1019`

## paragon.5131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

游行进行得比我们的预想好得多！公民们为§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!欢呼喝彩的画面被拍下来，并在[Owner.GetName]范围内广为流传，以对恩慈的[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]表示尊敬。不过，在首都还是爆发过一些小规模的骚乱，但秩序很快就恢复了，没有伤亡发生。媒体也因安保单位的快速反应而对§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!大加赞扬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

The parade went better than expected! Images of citizens cheering for §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! were recorded and published across [Owner.GetName] to show support for the benevolent [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]. While a number of small riots were reported across the capital, order was swiftly restored. There were no casualties, and state media praised §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! for the swift response of our security forces.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1020`

## paragon.5131.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5131.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这些狗娘的异见者！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

These cursed dissidents!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1021`

## paragon.5140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

军界异见者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

Military Dissidents

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1023`

## paragon.5140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了一些关于基层军官诋毁§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!名声的报告。这样的行径绝不能逃避惩罚。那些相关者，最少最少，也应该被开除军籍。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

There have been reports of low-ranking military officers actively smearing the name of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!. This cannot go unpunished. Those responsible should, at the very least, be discharged from the service.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1024`

## paragon.5140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

发起调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

Open an investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1025`

## paragon.5141.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5141.name&lang=en)

### 中文

逮捕异见者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:910`

### English

Dissidents Arrested

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1027`

## paragon.5141.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5141.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些谋反的异见军官被逮捕了。庆幸的是，他们的人数很少，但这次事件表明，即使是我们的军队也可能被叛徒渗透。我们抓住了主要的煽动者，但我们不应该排除与非军事组织的联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:911`

### English

The dissident officers who were conspiring against us have been arrested. Thankfully, they were few in numbers, but this incident indicates that even our military can be infiltrated by traitors. We were able to catch the main instigators, but we shouldn't rule out possible ties to non-military groups.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1028`

## paragon.5141.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5141.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须保持警惕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:912`

### English

We must remain vigilant.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1029`

## paragon.5150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

猖獗的裙带关系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:913`

### English

Rampant Nepotism

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1031`

## paragon.5150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

内阁最近受到了严格审查。匿名投诉反映，数目惊人的重要职位被高官的亲朋好友占据。我们已答应调查此事，并改弦易辙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

The council has recently come under heavy scrutiny. Anonymous complaints highlight the staggering amount of important positions which have been filled by the friends and family of high-ranking officials. We have promised to look into the matter and to do better.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1032`

## paragon.5150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要清理队伍了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

We need to clean up our ranks.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1033`

## paragon.5151.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5151.name&lang=en)

### 中文

内阁清理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:916`

### English

Council Cleanup

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1035`

## paragon.5151.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5151.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

特别委员会对内阁高层进行了彻底的巡查。发现了许多资质不足的人，绝大多数人被解除职务。其中一小部分有前途的人才被送去参加培训。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

A special committee has scoured the higher echelons of the council. Many unqualified individuals were identified, and most have already been dismissed from their posts. A few of the more promising talents were sent to training sessions.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1036`

## paragon.5151.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5151.a&lang=en)

这下好多了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:918`

### English

Much better.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1037`

通讯中继器

Communication Relay

## PARAGON_ORIGIN_SPIRITUALIST_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_ORIGIN_SPIRITUALIST_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在首都恒星系建造一座通讯中继站以避免长距离通讯受到干扰。我们最好的工程师已经准备好开展工作了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:921`

### English

Building a relay in our capital system will prevent disruptions in long-range communications. Our best engineers are ready to get to work.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1041`

## PARAGON_ORIGIN_AUTHORITARIAN_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_ORIGIN_AUTHORITARIAN_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

大游行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:922`

### English

Grand Parade

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1042`

## PARAGON_ORIGIN_AUTHORITARIAN_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_ORIGIN_AUTHORITARIAN_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

准备一场盛大的游行，设立公休假日，这需要大量的准备工作。如果我们要重燃国家自豪感，一切都必须安排得尽善尽美。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:923`

### English

A grand parade and public holiday will require a great deal of preparation. Everything must be perfect if we are to rekindle the nation's pride.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1043`

## PARAGON_ORIGIN_PACIFIST_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_ORIGIN_PACIFIST_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

铲除异见军官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:924`

### English

Uprooting Dissident Officers

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1044`

## PARAGON_ORIGIN_PACIFIST_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_ORIGIN_PACIFIST_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

异见者正悄悄地在我们的军队里落地生根，他们诽谤§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!并试图谋反。我们必须采取快速而强有力的行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:925`

### English

Dissidents are taking root inside our military, slandering §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! and scheming against us. Swift and decisive action must be taken.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1045`

## PARAGON_ORIGIN_MATERIALIST_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_ORIGIN_MATERIALIST_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

制止裙带关系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:926`

### English

No Nepotism

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1046`

## PARAGON_ORIGIN_MATERIALIST_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APARAGON_ORIGIN_MATERIALIST_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

制止内阁中的裙带关系应该是我们的首要任务，因为它几乎阻碍了我们所做的一切。清理这些腐败可能需要一些时间，但我们的人民对我们抱有期待。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

Putting a stop to nepotism in the council should be our top priority, as it hinders almost everything we do. It may take some time to clean up all this corruption, but our people are counting on us.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1047`

## paragon.5020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

前途未卜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

What Lies Ahead

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1049`

## paragon.5020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管§Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle][owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!已经倍加努力，但仍有许多事务有待完成。整个帝国是由许多互相矛盾的派系组成，人们是在[owner.Ruler.GetTitle]不断的努力下才团结起来。\n\n尽管§Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle][owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!未曾想过要改革大一统后形成的政府，但一种新的、更直接的体制可以解决其中一些问题。另一方面，这样的决定可能会激起反对意见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:929`

### English

Despite §Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s work, there is still much to be done. The empire consists of conflicting factions, and the population is united only through the constant effort of the [owner.Ruler.GetTitle].\n\nAlthough §Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! did not wish to change the post-Unification government, a new, more direct approach could remedy some of these problems. On the other hand, such a decision may encounter significant opposition.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1050`

## paragon.5020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

宣布帝制！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:930`

### English

Proclaim Imperial rule!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1051`

## paragon.5020.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5020.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L只有授予[owner.Ruler.GetTitle]无限的权力，我们才有可能统治繁星。从现在起，[owner.Ruler.GetTitle]对内阁拥有绝对的权威。愿[owner.Ruler.GetHisHer]的统治长久而繁荣！§!\n这将强化§T[owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的统治，但可能在未来造成§R严重的后果§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:931`

### English

§LOnly by granting unlimited power to the [owner.Ruler.GetTitle], can we hope to rule the stars. From now on, the [owner.Ruler.GetTitle] will have absolute authority over the council. May [owner.Ruler.GetHisHer] rule be long and fruitful!§!\nThis will strengthen §T[owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s rule, but might have §Rdire consquences§! in the future.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1052`

## paragon.5020.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5020.b&lang=en)

### 中文

维持现状。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:932`

### English

Maintain the status quo.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1053`

## paragon.5020.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5020.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L我们的人民深信[owner.Ruler.GetTitle]将引领大家前往繁星，但从未同意创造一个世袭头衔或施行帝制。用这样的方式攫取权力是暴政。§!维持现状才能稳定国家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:933`

### English

§LOur people entrusted the [owner.Ruler.GetTitle] to guide them to the stars, but they never agreed to a royal title or imperial rule. Seizing power like this would be tyranny.§! Holding the status quo should stabilize the nation.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1054`

## paragon.5021.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5021.name&lang=en)

### 中文

登基仪式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:934`

### English

Imperial Proclamation

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1056`

## paragon.5021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

消息如野火般蔓延开。几位官员签署同意之后，一场盛大的登基仪式在全国范围内进行播报。大多数[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]都为§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!能够完全地掌握国家而感到欢欣鼓舞。\n\n在这一片欢腾的场景之下，掩藏着不可明说的问题和各类不确定情况。新的帝国将会是什么样的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:935`

### English

The news spread like wildfire. A few official signatures later, and a grand coronation ceremony was broadcast to the nation. The majority of [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are happy that §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! will now have full control of state.\n\nWhile the mood is festive, unspoken questions and uncertainties remain. What will this new empire look like?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1057`

## paragon.5021.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5021.a&lang=en)

### 中文

军方必须占主导地位。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:936`

### English

The military must play a central role.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1058`

## paragon.5021.b

## paragon.5022.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5022.name&lang=en)

### 中文

昭告天下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:944`

### English

Addressing the Nation

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1066`

## paragon.5022.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5022.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H现在是§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!昭告[Ruler.GetHerHis]臣民的时候了。国有媒体正在广播[Ruler.GetHerHis]发言。§!\n\n[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]同胞们！当我们处于危难年代时，你们寻求了我的指引。我向你们保证，你们对我的信任将会获得很好的回报。赋予我权力的同时也意味着伟大的责任，我发誓忠实地为这个国家和我的臣民服务。我作为皇帝的第一项法案是建立一个学府，在那里最优秀、最聪明的人将接受培训，建立一个我们都能为之自豪的未来。向繁星进军，向胜利进军！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:945`

### English

§HIt is time for §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! to address [Ruler.GetHerHis] loyal subjects. The state-owned media are broadcasting [Ruler.GetHerHis] speech.§!\n\nFellow [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! You have looked to me for guidance in our time of need, and I promise you that your trust will be well rewarded. The power bestowed upon me come with great responsibility, and I swear to faithfully serve this nation and you, my subjects. My first act as emperor is the establishment of a seat of learning where the best and brightest will be trained to build a future we can all be proud of. To the stars, and victory!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1067`

## paragon.5022.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5022.a&lang=en)

### 中文

向着繁星出发！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:946`

### English

To the stars!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1068`

## paragon.5023.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5023.name&lang=en)

### 中文

加入联邦

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:948`

### English

Federated

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1071`

## paragon.5023.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5023.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName]的讯息强调了今天是一个光荣的日子！这个联邦的建立确实是一件值得庆祝的事情。[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetHeSheCap]谈到了我们与盟国们构筑的纽带，阐明同[your_ally.GetName]的合使两国迈入全新的时代。一个充满伟大和繁荣的黄金时代！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:949`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName]'s message highlights what a glorious day this is! The founding of this Federation is truly a cause for celebration. [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetHeSheCap] speaks to bonds we forge with our allies, stating that cooperation with the [your_ally.GetName] will usher in a new age. A golden age of greatness and prosperity!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1072`

## paragon.5023.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5023.a&lang=en)

### 中文

联邦万岁！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:950`

### English

Long live the Federation!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1073`

## paragon.5025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

万王之王

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:951`

### English

King of Kings

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1075`

## paragon.5025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n宣言的重点放在了[your_ally.GetName]，该国承认了我们的优越性，服从我们的统治和指导！该国的明智和信任将得到应有的回报，我们希望其他国家也能做出正确的选择，向我国臣服。\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:952`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nThe announcement is focused on the [your_ally.GetName], who have recognized our superiority and submitted to our rule and guidance! Their wisdom and trust will be duly rewarded, and we hope that other nations will also make the right choice and submit.\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1076`

## paragon.5025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为统治而生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:953`

### English

We are born to rule.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1077`

## paragon.5027.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5027.name&lang=en)

### 中文

击溃敌人！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

Enemy Crushed!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1078`

## paragon.5027.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5027.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n前方传来签署和平条约的新闻，同时人们又听说我们的敌人被迫接受巨大的让步，因此欢欣鼓舞。我们见证了在光耀者的指引下获得的胜利！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:955`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nThe people rejoice as news arrives of a signed peace treaty and the vast concessions our foes were forced to accept. We stand witness to the triumpth of Luminary guidance!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1079`

## paragon.5027.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5027.a&lang=en)

### 中文

胜利！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:956`

### English

Victory!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1080`

## paragon.5029.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5029.name&lang=en)

### 中文

沉重后果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:957`

### English

Grim Outcome

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1082`

## paragon.5029.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5029.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n今天对于[Owner.GetName]来说是一个悲惨的日子。侵略者的残忍行径在新闻推送中得到了充分的展示，[Ruler.GetName]强调在这个黑暗时期团结一致是尤其必要的。§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!郑重承诺，确保实现可能的最好结果，同时此类事情不再发生。\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nToday is a grim day for the [Owner.GetName]. The brutality of the invaders is on full display in the news feed, as [Ruler.GetName] underlines the necessity of working together during these dark times. §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! makes a solemn vow to ensure the best possible outcome, and to never let this happen again.\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1083`

## paragon.5029.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5029.a&lang=en)

### 中文

绝不重蹈覆辙！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:959`

### English

Never again!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1084`

## paragon.5033.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5033.name&lang=en)

### 中文

俯首称臣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:960`

### English

Vassalage

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1086`

## paragon.5033.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5033.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName]保持沉默。新闻中显示[Owner.GetName]签署了附庸条约。尽管我们始终相信光耀者的统治，但是这一刻无疑是我们文明一个不可避免的挫折。\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:961`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName] is silent. The newsfeed shows the signing of [Owner.GetName]'s contract of vassalization. Despite our trust in the Luminary's rule, this moment signifies an undeniable setback for our civilization.\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1087`

## paragon.5033.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5033.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们只是在利用此人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:962`

### English

We are only using them.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1088`

## paragon.5038.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5038.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星系守护者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

Galactic Protectors

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1090`

## paragon.5038.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5038.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n今天是一个命运攸关的日子。[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName]自豪地宣布，[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetSheHe]带来了一个消息：星海共同体已经选举我们成为至高无上的星海监管人。他们承认只有我们才有能力确保这个星系的安全。我们被赋予了巨大的权力，我们的职责不仅仅是履行各国的期许，更是要将其超越。\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:964`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nToday is a fateful day. [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName] stands proudly as [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetSheHe] the news is delivered: the Galactic Community has elected us to supreme Custodianship. They have recognized that we alone are poised to ensure the safety of this galaxy. We have been granted great power, and it is our duty to not only meet, but surpass all expectations.\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1091`

## paragon.5038.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5038.a&lang=en)

### 中文

为了星系！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:965`

### English

For the Galaxy!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1092`

## paragon.5039.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5039.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最高荣誉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:966`

### English

Highest Honor

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1094`

## paragon.5039.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5039.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n今天是我们胜利的日子！星海参议院任命[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]为星海帝国的皇帝。为了表示敬意，[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName]发誓要把已知的星系及其人民带入一个新的繁荣时代——从现在开始，直到永远！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:967`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nToday is the day of our triumph! The Galactic Senate has appointed [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName] Emperor of the Galactic Imperium. In salute, [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetName] swears to usher the known galaxy and its peoples into a new age of prosperity - from now, and to eternity!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1095`

## paragon.5039.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5039.a&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国霸业千秋万代！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:968`

### English

Imperium Eternal!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1096`

## paragon.5050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

逐渐壮大的反对派

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:970`

### English

Mounting Opposition

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1099`

## paragon.5050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的意识形态反对者已经形成了一个强大的政治集团。他们呼吁他们的支持者聚集在一起，和平抗议他们认为由§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!所颁布的单边变革！\n\n他们认为，我们已经偏离了大一统后设立下的路线，并呼吁§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!废除某些改革。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

Our ideological opponents have formed a powerful political block. They have called upon their supporters to gather in peaceful protest against what they see as unilateral changes enacted by §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!\n\nThey argue that we have strayed from the path set after the Unification, and call upon §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! to repeal certain reforms.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1100`

## paragon.5050.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5050.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们怎敢怀疑[Ruler.GetHimHer]？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:972`

### English

How dare they question [Ruler.GetHimHer]?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1101`

异见崛起

### English

Dissidence on the Rise

## paragon_origin_forgotten_type

$paragon_origin_forgotten$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:974`

### English

$paragon_origin_forgotten$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1104`

## paragon_origin_forgotten_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_forgotten_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

抗议者聚集到政府大楼门口，要求§Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle][owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!听听他们的诉苦。这些人中间绝大多是人是年轻人，但是他们的领袖却是大统一战争中的老兵。忽略他们的需求可能是不明智的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:975`

### English

Protesters are gathering in front of governmental buildings demanding that §Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! hear out their grievances. Most of them are youngsters, but among their leaders are veterans of the Unification. Ignoring their demands may prove unwise.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1105`

## paragon_origin_forgotten_monthly_change_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_forgotten_monthly_change_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

抗议者们不是铁板一块，每天都有新的派系产生。我们正在努力找出他们的领袖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:976`

### English

The protests are not a coordinated effort, and new groups spring up almost daily. We are working hard to identify their leaders.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1106`

## situation_origin_forgotten_propaganda

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_origin_forgotten_propaganda&lang=en)

## paragon.5054.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5054.name&lang=en)

### 中文

指控

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

Accusations

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1117`

## paragon.5054.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5054.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一群自称“被遗忘者”的地下团体控诉§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的暴政。他们宣称我国政府从未真正代表人民的意愿，反而只是代表一小撮特定的群体利益。他们并不相信[Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]，并提供了曾经的不同政见者已经失踪的证据。他们这些反复发出的讯息开始在我们的公民群体中埋下怀疑的种子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:987`

### English

A secretive group calling themselves The Forgotten has accused §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! of tyranny. They claim that our government no longer represents the will of our people, only the interests of a select few. Their trust in [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName] has waned, and they have brought forward evidence of dissenters who have suddenly disappeared after criticizing our regime. Their repeated messages are starting to sow doubt in the minds of our citizens.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1118`

## paragon.5055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真相的代价

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:988`

### English

The Price of Truth

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1119`

## paragon.5055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究了所谓证据后，似乎§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!确实是向那些被遗忘者们撒谎了，至少也是隐瞒了真相。[Owner.Ruler.GetHeShe]与数个强大的利益派系签订了几份协议，在其中许诺了诸多[Owner.Ruler.GetHeShe]实际上做不到的事情。\n\n今天，被遗忘者代表那些被[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]所欺骗之人的声音，向我们呼吁将政府权力转移至议会及人民手中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:989`

### English

After reviewing the evidence, it appears that §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has indeed lied or at least concealed the truth to the council and the population at large. [Owner.Ruler.GetHeShe] has signed several contracts with various powerful stakeholders, promising many things that [Owner.Ruler.GetHeShe] could not realistically deliver upon.\n\nToday, the Forgotten have made themselves the voice of all those who have been tricked by the [Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] and are calling upon our government to transfer power back to the council and the people.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1120`

## paragon.5056.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5056.name&lang=en)

对人不对事

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:990`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1121`

## paragon.5056.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5056.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一些能够抹黑§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!秘密文件从一个匿名团体处流出。这一叛国行径为这场政治风波火上浇油。被遗忘者最有动机，但却表现得很无辜，因为我们完全找不到他们涉及此事的证据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:991`

### English

In an attempt to smear §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s reputation, secret documents have been leaked by an anonymous party. This treasonous act has poured oil onto the political fires that were already threatening to consume our government. The Forgotten, although prime suspects, appear innocent, as we could not find any proof of their involvement.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1122`

## paragon.5057.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5057.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

政治局势已经失控。煽动者正在煽风点火，而§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的拒绝让步引发了帝国范围内的暴力抗议浪潮。随着伤亡人数的增多，§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!别无选择，只能下令要求以一切必要手段镇压反对派。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:993`

### English

The political situation has escalated out of control. As agitators fan the flames of revolution, §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]'s§! refusal to back down has ignited an empire wide wave of violent protests. The casualties are mounting, and left with no other choice, the §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§! has ordered the opposition be crushed by any means necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1124`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1137`

## paragon.5060.desc.imprisioned

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5060.desc.imprisioned&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n社会正在螺旋坠入失控境地。猖獗的暴力、隐秘的煽动、无耻的谎言……这些问题困扰我们已久。我自豪地宣布，这些困难时期已经结束！那些试图破坏我们的人已被逮捕，我保证，他们将面临全面的法律制裁。不久之后，他们将为对你们造成的伤害而忏悔！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nSociety is spiralling out of control. Rampant violence, insidious agitation, shameless lies... These things have plagued us for too long. With great pride, I announce an end to these troubled times! Those who sought to undermine us have been arrested, and I promise, they will face the full force of justice. Before long, they will repent for the harm they caused you!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1138`

## paragon.5060.desc.negotiated

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5060.desc.negotiated&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n在冲突时期，我们很容易忘记为之奋斗的目标，把我们的政治对手视为彻头彻尾的敌人。我们经历了艰难的时刻，但今天我很自豪地告诉你们，我们已经达成了和平的结果。你们的声音已经被听到了。你们曾经信任过我，而我却让你们失望了。\n\n这样的情况到此为止！随着即将到来的宪法改革，我坚信我们已经回到了正确的道路上。出于你们对我能力的信任，我将谦卑地见证这些变革的落实。我发誓，我不会再让你们失望！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1008`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nIn times of conflict, it can be easy to lose sight of what we fight for, to view our political opponents as outright enemies. We have gone through trying times, but today I am proud to tell you that we have reached a peaceful outcome. Your voices have been heard. You had put your trust in me and I had failed you.\n\nNo more! With the upcoming constitutional reform, I firmly believe that we find ourselves back on the right path, and I am humbled by your trust in my ability to see these changes through. Upon my word, I shall not fail you again!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1139`

## paragon.5060.desc.destroyed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5060.desc.destroyed&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n今天是伟大的胜利之日，但这并非没有牺牲的代价。我们的敌人已经被消灭，但是他们的背叛使我的心处于巨大的伤痛之中。他们出于自私的目的，试图毁灭我们所有人，但最终，他们无法匹敌我们团结一致产生的力量。很快，他们将因自己的罪行受处决。我们取得了胜利！\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1009`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nToday marks a great victory, but it has not been bought without sacrifice. Our enemies have been destroyed, yet the sting of their betrayal makes my heart bleed. In their selfishness they sought to destroy us all, but in the end, they were no match for our collective strength. Soon they will be executed for their crimes. We have prevailed!\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1140`

## paragon.5060.a

### English

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1011`

### English

§LRecent events have shown that only the wise rule of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! can bring peace and prosperity to our society. We need to consolidate more power into the hands of our leader.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1142`

我们需要高效的政府！

### English

## paragon.5060.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5060.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L最近的事件表明，为了在未来克服这些困境，我们必须要有一个强大政府的领导。我们需要将更多权力移交给内阁。\n§!$paragon.leader.growth.negative$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1013`

### English

§LRecent events have shown that a strong government rule is needed to overcome such dilemmas in the future. We need to give more power to the council.\n§!$paragon.leader.growth.negative$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1144`

## paragon.5061.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5061.name&lang=en)

### 中文

噩耗传来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1015`

### English

Tragedy Strikes

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1147`

## paragon.5061.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5061.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Owner.GetHeirTitle][Owner.GetHeirName]§!去世了。我们尚未确定死因，但初步调查显示这是自然死亡。[Ruler.GetRegnalName]深受[Ruler.GetHisHer]子嗣之丧的打击。官方法令宣布了全国哀悼期，同时我们等待对继承人死因的调查结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1016`

### English

§Y[Owner.GetHeirTitle] [Owner.GetHeirName]§! is dead. We have yet to determine the cause of death, but preliminary reports suggest natural causes. [Ruler.GetRegnalName] was struck hard by the loss of [Ruler.GetHisHer] progeny. A period of national mourning has been decreed and we await the full results of the investigation into the cause of the heir's death.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1148`

## paragon.5062.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5062.name&lang=en)

### 中文

满目哀伤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1017`

### English

Blinding Grief

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1150`

## paragon.5062.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5062.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管所有的证据都表明这是一次自然死亡，§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!却拒绝接受这个事实。[Ruler.GetHisHer]的继承人之死对[Ruler.GetHimHer]产生了很大影响，并下令在我们的领土范围内展开搜捕行动。必须要找到那些为此负责的懦弱的刺杀者。安全部队已经开展行动，根除异见分子。\n\n这些逮捕行动所伴随的暴力程度已经震惊了许多公民。抗议活动再次爆发，谴责滥用权力并要求新一轮政府改革。一个行星的绝大多数人口起来反对[Ruler.GetTitle]的统治，这在我们的历史上属于首次。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1018`

### English

Despite every evidence pointing at a natural death, §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! refused to accept it. The death of [Ruler.GetHisHer] heir has greatly affected [Ruler.GetHimHer], and orders have been sent across our territories. Those responsible for this cowardly assassination must be found. Security forces are already conducting sweeps to root out dissenters.\n\nThe level of violence that has accompanied these arrests has shocked many of our citizens. Protests denouncing the abuses of power and demanding a new wave of government reforms have erupted once again. For the first time in our history, the majority of a planet's population has risen up against the rule of the [Ruler.GetTitle].

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1151`

### English

## paragon.5063.death.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5063.death.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!死后，改革派的支持率逐渐降低了。该团体的代表抛开意识形态上的分歧，特意参加了葬礼仪式。虽然今天被悲伤的气氛所笼罩，但也是一个令人团结一心的时刻——提醒[Root.Owner.GetName]所代表的意义。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1023`

### English

After §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]'s§! death, support for the Reformists dwindled. Putting aside their ideological differences, representatives from the group made a point of attending the funeral ceremony. Although today is marred by sorrow, it is also a moment of unity - a reminder of what the [Root.Owner.GetName] stands for.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1157`

## paragon.5063.norebel.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5063.norebel.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无论是我们对资源的巧妙利用还是引入了强有力的惩罚措施，叛乱的星球不再是一个问题。我们再次团结起来，民众专注于发展而不是内讧。§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!颁布法令，将今天定为一个特殊的纪念日。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1024`

### English

Whether due to our clever use of resources or the introduction of robust punishments, rebellious planets are no longer an issue. Once again we stand united, with the populace focused on growth rather than infighting. §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has decreed today will be a special commemorative holiday going forward.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1158`

## paragon_origin_reformists_struggle_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_origin_reformists_struggle_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改革派的煽动者打着自由的幌子试图摧毁我们的国家！随着暴力冲突在我们的领土上爆发，他们的谎言已经说服了许多人民转而反对我们。他们认为§Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!软弱、残忍、无能，但我们会向他们证明不是这样的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1029`

### English

Under the guise of freedom, the Reformists demagogues are trying to destroy the nation! As violent clashes erupt across our territories, their lies have convinced many of our people to turn on us. They think §Y[Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! weak, cruel and incompetent, but we will show them!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1164`

### 中文

对所有已知的改革派藏身处进行暴力打击，应该可以恢复国家的稳定。\n为了终结当下的局势，完成§Y$council_agenda_origin_delusional_tyrant_name$§!议程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1031`

### English

A brutal crackdown on all known Reformist hideouts ought to restore stability to the nation.\nTo put an end to the situation, finish the §Y$council_agenda_origin_delusional_tyrant_name$§! Agenda.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1166`

## approach_situation_paragon_origin_reformists_struggle_accept_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapproach_situation_paragon_origin_reformists_struggle_accept_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

对平民发动攻击是不可接受的。我们将以和平方式推进，呼吁停火并邀请改革派进行谈判。希望这样可以减少暴力行为的需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1033`

### English

Fighting against civilians is unacceptable. We will push forward peacefully, calling for a truce and inviting the Reformists to negotiate. Hopefully, this will limit the need for violence.

## decision_paragon_origin_subdue_reformists_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_paragon_origin_subdue_reformists_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

民主主义是必须要治疗的病痛。我们的安保部队将突袭所有改革派的藏身处。平民伤亡在所难免。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1044`

### English

Democracy is an ailment that needs to be remedied. Our security officers will raid Reformist hideouts and put an end to their schemes. Civilian casualties are to be expected.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1179`

## decision_paragon_origin_subdue_reformists_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_paragon_origin_subdue_reformists_tt&lang=en)

这将会移除§Y改革派诉求§!局势的§G4§!点进度。\n如果行星上没有平等主义的人口，有50%几率移除该行星的§Y民主倾向§!修正。\n这将会使§Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle][owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]的§!§Y光耀者特质§!§R劣化§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1045`

### English

This will remove §G4§! Progress from the §YReformists Demands§! Situation.\nThere is a 50% chance to remove §YDemocratic Tendencies§! modifier from the planet if no Egalitarian Pops are present.\nThis will §Rworsen§! §Y[owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]'s§! §YLuminary Trait§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1180`

## paragon.5064.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5064.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!发现我们的一位领袖正在反对政府！妥善的程序是直接处死这名叛徒，但是证据不是很充分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1052`

### English

§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has discovered that one of our leaders is plotting against the government! The proper course would be to execute the traitor, but evidence is scarce.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1188`

我们需要更多的证据。

### English

We need more evidence.

## paragon.5065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恐怖分子巢穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1055`

### English

Terrorist Nests

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1191`

## paragon.5065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!下令在我们的一颗行星上进行大范围的清洗行动。显然，那里存在一个改革派据点，但我们尚未掌握充分证据。我们确定要因为预感将忠诚公民的性命置于危险之中吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1056`

### English

§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! ordered a large scale cleansing on one of our planets. Apparently, there is a supposed Reformist holdout there, but no firm evidence has been produced. Will we really risk the lives of loyal citizens on a hunch?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1192`

## paragon.5070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现海盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1059`

### English

Pirates Spotted

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1195`

## paragon.5070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改革派的一个小团体加入了当地的海盗组织。虽然他们不是一个严重的威胁，但他们仍然可以扰乱我们在该星域的运营和贸易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1060`

### English

One of the alleged Reformist cells joined forces with a local group of pirates. Although they are not a serious threat, they can still disrupt our operations and trade in the sector.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1196`

## paragon.5071.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5071.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外部援助

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1061`

### English

Foreign Aid

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1197`

## paragon.5071.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5071.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[event_target:friendly_nation]的一位显赫公民希望在我们的内部斗争中帮助我们。他们不仅带来了美好愿景，同时还伴随着一批合金作为援助。似乎他们寻求改善我们之间的关系，但§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!怀疑这是为异己分子的运动建立供应线的诡计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1062`

### English

A prominent citizen from [event_target:friendly_nation] wishes to help us in our internal struggle. Their best wishes were accompanied by a shipment of alloys to aid us. It seems they seek to improve relations between us, but §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! suspects a ruse to establish supply lines for the dissident movements.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1198`

## paragon.5071.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5071.a&lang=en)

### 中文

发表致谢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1063`

### English

Send our thanks.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1199`

## paragon.5071.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5071.b&lang=en)

### English

This is a trick!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1200`

## paragon.5072.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5072.name&lang=en)

### 中文

窝点擒获

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1065`

### English

Cell Captured

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1201`

## paragon.5072.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5072.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的安全部门成功追踪并捣毁了一个改革派窝点。这次行动取得了极大的成功，人员损失降到最低，没有平民伤亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1066`

### English

Our security service managed to hunt down and dismantle one of the Reformists' cells. The operation was a clear success, with minimal loss of life and no civilian casualties.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1202`

## paragon.5073.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5073.name&lang=en)

### 中文

飞天窝点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1067`

### English

Cover Blown

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1203`

## paragon.5073.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5073.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的安全部门成功地追踪到了一个改革派窝点，但他们在整个街区都埋设了爆炸物。当我们的特工突入窝点的时候，他们引爆了炸药，杀死了许多人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1068`

### English

Our security service managed to track down a Reformist cell, but they had rigged the whole block with explosives. As our operatives breached the lair, they activated the charges, killing many.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1204`

## paragon.5074.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5074.name&lang=en)

### 中文

流血冲突

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1069`

### English

Firefight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1205`

## paragon.5074.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5074.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们和改革派发生了激烈的交火，有很多平民受伤。我们派出了应急部队来照料伤者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

Many civilians were hurt in a series of fierce firefights between our forces and the Reformists. We have sent emergency responders to take care of the wounded.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1206`

## paragon.5075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

保皇派集会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English

Loyalists Rally

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1207`

## paragon.5075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们忠诚的支持者举行了一场集会，声援§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!！我们在这场斗争中显然赢得了人民的支持！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1072`

### English

Our loyal supporters made a rally to back up §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!! We clearly have the support of the people in our fight!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1208`

## paragon.5090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

改革派集会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1073`

### English

Reformists Rally

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1209`

## paragon.5090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改革派的宣传部门在我们的某颗行星上发起了一场虚假信息运动。忠诚派势力正在保持高度警戒，意图控制局面，但抗议者的队伍继续壮大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1074`

### English

Reformist propagandists led a disinformation campaign on one of our planets. Loyalist forces are on their toes to keep the situation under control, but the protesters continue to grow in strength.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1210`

## paragon.5095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

改革派被俘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1075`

### English

Reformists Captured

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1211`

## paragon.5095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于内阁的努力工作，改革派领袖已被迅速逮捕。他们现在听由§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!发落。但另一方面，他们在民众中拥有许多支持者，所以明智的做法是通过谈判达成一个的双方都有利的结果——当然条件是我们说的算。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1076`

### English

Thanks to the Council's hard work, the Reformist leaders have been swiftly apprehended. They are now at the mercy of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!, but they have many supporters among the populace, and it is advisable to negotiate an outcome that suits both parties - albeit on our terms.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1212`

## paragon.5095.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5095.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们听听他们的条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1077`

### English

Let's hear their terms.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1213`

## paragon.5200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

准备谈判

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1078`

### English

Preparing to Negotiate

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1215`

## paragon.5200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过几个月的冲突，现在是时候让我们解决分歧，讨论如何结束这场荒谬的叛乱了。§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!已呼吁停火并与改革派进行谈判。所有敌对行动已经停止，每个人都在等待谈判结果的出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1079`

### English

After months of conflict, the time has come to resolve our differences and talk about how we can end this ridiculous revolt. §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has called for a ceasefire to negotiate with the Reformists. All hostilities have ceased, as everyone awaits the outcome of the negotiations.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1216`

## paragon.5201.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5201.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大演讲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1080`

### English

The Speech

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1217`

## paragon.5201.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5201.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]同胞！今天，我想让大家知道，我充满了自豪。我们求同存异，作为一个民族，向着未知一同进发，建造了新的星球，我们的聪明才智达到了新的高度。\n\n我要感谢诸位的付出……\n\n我还要……\n\n§H§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle][Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!突然一个踉跄，消失在了画面之外。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1081`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nFellow [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! Today, I want you to know I am filled with pride. As a people, we have come together and, despite differences, ventured into the unknown, built new worlds, and reached new heights of ingenuity.\n\nI want to acknowledge your sacrifice...\n\nI want...\n\n§H§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle] [Root.Owner.Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! suddenly staggers out of view.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1218`

## paragon.5201.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5201.a&lang=en)

发生什么了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1082`

### English

What is going on?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1219`

## paragon.5202.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5202.name&lang=en)

### 中文

陨落

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1083`

### English

The Fall

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1220`

## paragon.5202.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5202.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

新闻很快就传遍了全国上下：§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!正生命垂危。这场神秘的急病原因尚不明确，似乎也不会很快结束。\n\n至少目前，§Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!仍名义上在位。然而，如果想要显著延长我们领袖的寿命，可能必须依靠一些非传统方法了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1084`

### English

The news didn't take long to spread among the population; §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! is dying. While the cause of this mysterious illness remains unknown, it does not appear to be immediately terminal.\n\nFor now, §Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! can remain at their post. However, unorthodox methods will be necessary to extend our leader's life by any significant amount of time.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1221`

## paragon.5202.synth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5202.synth&lang=en)

### 中文

新闻很快就传遍了全国上下：§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的神经网络出故障了。这个神秘的故障让我们最聪明的人都摸不着头脑，似乎不会很快被排除。\n\n至少目前，§Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!仍名义上在位。然而，如果想要显著延长我们领袖的工作周期，可能必须依靠一些非传统方法了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1085`

### English

The news didn't take long to spread among the population; §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! neural net is failing. While the cause of this mysterious error has eluded our best minds, it is not immediately terminal.\n\nFor now, §Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! can remain at their post. However, unorthodox methods will be necessary to repair and extend our leader's functionality by any significant amount of time.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1222`

## paragon.5202.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5202.a&lang=en)

十字路口

### English

Crossroads

## paragon.5210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候让§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!再度执掌国家的大舵了。[Ruler.GetName]受赐福继承了$leader_trait_legendary_leader$的品质，仍然充满着庄严。然而，随着改革派势力的增强，一场全面内战逐渐逼近，[Ruler.GetTitle]发出了谈判的号召。\n\n[Ruler.GetName]报告道，谈判前期气氛十分紧张。来自[Owner.GetName]各处的代表在听取我们[Ruler.GetTitle]的官方声明——其中列出了所有我们可能接受的让步——之前，纷纷提出了自己的要求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1091`

### English

It is time for §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! to retake the helm of state. Blessed with the heritage of the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$, [Ruler.GetName] remains a magnetic figure. However, with the increasing power of the Reformists and a full-fledged civil war looming on the horizon, the [Ruler.GetTitle] issued a call to negotiations.\n\n[Ruler.GetName] reports that early negotiations have been tense. Representatives from all corners of [Owner.GetName] made their demands before listening to our [Ruler.GetTitle]'s official statement, which listed the concessions we might accept.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1229`

## paragon.5210.d.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5210.d.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!将被暗杀。\n§R国家将分裂并陷入内战。§!\n§L国家处于混乱之中。人们迫切地想点燃革命的火焰。我们已经听到了他们的号召。必须消灭所有暴政！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1098`

### English

§Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! will be assassinated.\n§RThe nation will fracture and a civil war will begin.§!\n§LThe nation is in disarray. People are desperate to light the flame of revolution. We have heard their call. All tyrants must be deposed!§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1236`

### English

## paragon.5211.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5211.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功挣脱了锁链！所有压迫者和暴君都已被推翻。我们可能是引发这场战争的火花，但我们所做的一切都是为了人民的利益。我们必将战胜一切！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1101`

### English

Our chains are broken! All oppressors and tyrants have been cast down. We may have been the spark that lit this war, but everything we do is for the good of the people. We shall overcome!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1240`

### English

协商结束

### English

## paragon.5220.desc.peace

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5220.desc.peace&lang=en)

### 中文

在谈判前夕，各方显然厌倦了战火，愿意促成协议。我们不知道这将产生什么样的长期后果，但现在我们团结起来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1134`

### English

On the eve of the negotiations, it became clear that the parties were sick of warring and would rather strike a deal. We cannot know what long-term consequences this will have, but for now we are united.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1275`

## paragon.5220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必将胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1135`

### English

We triumph.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1276`

## paragon.5220.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5220.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L我们要么通过武力，要么通过谈判来解决分歧。从现在开始，我们要真正团结起来。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1136`

### English

§LWe settle our differences either through might or through negotiation. From now on, we are truly united.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1277`

## paragon.5221.name

### English

## paragon.5220.desc.war

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5220.desc.war&lang=en)

### 中文

在谈判前夕，局势已经很明显，各方都无法达成一致。即使现在，叛乱实力也正在集结，战争爆发了。再一次我们的国家处于分裂的状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1138`

### English

On the eve of the negotiations, it became clear that the parties would never come to an agreement. Even now, rebel forces are massing, and war has been declared. Once more our nation stands divided.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1279`

## paragon.5222.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5222.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

号召谈判是一个捕获改革派领袖的完美陷阱。他们聚集在一个地方，收网有如探囊取物。我们不费吹灰之力就获得了有关他们藏身之处、下线和资产的信息。剩下的反对派也轻松地处理掉了，不需要任何让步，也没有发生代价高昂的内战。所有改革派都被§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的法令判处死刑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1142`

### English

The call to negotiations was a perfect trap for the Reformist leaders. Gathered in one place, they were easily apprehended. We wasted no time extracting information about their hideouts, followers, and assets. The remaining opposition was swiftly dealt with, without concessions or a costly civil war. Every Reformist has been sentenced to death by decree of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1283`

## paragon.5222.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5222.a&lang=en)

我们现在不能回头了。只能继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1143`

### English

We can't turn back now. Proceed.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1284`

## paragon.5223.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5223.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着内乱的余烬逐渐地消失，一个新的社会正从灰烬中崛起，准备将§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的智慧火花带到星空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1145`

### English

As the embers of civil strife fade away, a new society is rising from the ashes, ready to carry the spark of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s wisdom to the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1286`
