## paragon.5226.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5226.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1147`

### English

Shrouded Planet

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1288`

## paragon.5226.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5226.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在第一阶段完成后，涡旋状的灵能能量充满了行星，席卷了整个地表。虽然现在无法在那里生存，但最强的灵能使汇聚一堂，守护着举行下一阶段仪式的寺庙。现在，需要做的只剩下将这些灵能力量从我们志愿者身上传到入§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的体内。这样将会大幅度提升我们§Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!的灵能能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1148`

### English

At the completion of the first phase, swirling psionic energies engulfed the planet and ravaged its surface. Although it is now impossible to survive there, our strongest psionics gathered to shield a temple at which the next phase of the ritual will take place. Now, all that remains is to direct the psionic force from our volunteers into §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!. Doing so should greatly bolster the psionic capabilities of our §Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1289`

## paragon.5227.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5227.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能仪式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1149`

### English

Psionic Ritual

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1290`

## paragon.5227.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5227.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

初始献祭：§R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension]公民§!\n每十年献祭需要：§R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension]公民§!\n所选灵能能力：§G[Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\n我们最强大的灵能者正在驯服消耗整颗行星所产生的狂野能量。他们现在将这些力量汇聚到神殿中心，那里有等待的被献祭者。很快仪式就要开始了，这些勇敢志愿者的灵能能量将被提取并注入§Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!体内。被献祭者越多，灵能能量就越强——但我们必须小心不要超过§Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!的控制范围，否则可能会面临一场非常可观的灾难。\n\n一但仪式结束，我们需要持续供应一系列志愿者，以避免§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的身体被能量摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1150`

### English

Initial sacrifice: §R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension] Citizens§!\nDecennial sacrifices needed: §R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension] Citizens§!\nChosen psychic powers: §G[Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus] [Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1] [Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2] [Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3] [Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\nOur strongest telepaths are taming the wild energies consuming the planet. They will now funnel these toward the center of the temple, where the sacrificial candidates stand waiting. Soon the ceremony will begin, and the psionic powers of these brave volunteers will be extracted and directed into the §Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!. The more people consumed, the stronger the psionic energy - but we must be careful not to go beyond what the §Y[Ruler.GetTitle]'s§! can control, or we risk a spectacular catastrophe.\n\nOnce the ritual is complete, we will need to supply a steady stream of volunteers to prevent the power from destroying the §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! body.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1291`

## paragon.5227.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5227.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L高密度的能量流经§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的躯体，但它们很难被一副血肉之躯所保留，而是溢流到了周遭的志愿者身上，在他们被虚境之火吞噬之前提升了他们的灵能力量。§!\n\n我们的灵能团队将使用灵能信标与§Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§!相连并驾驭这一力量，为所有的舰船增加§G$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_HP_ADD$§!和§G$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_REGEN_ADD_PERC$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1154`

### English

§LConcentrated energies flow through the veins of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!, but they are hardly contained by the physical body. Instead, they lash out at nearby volunteers, enhancing their power before they are consumed in pyrokinetic flames.§!\n\nOur psionic crews will use Psychic Beacons to connect with §Y[Ruler.GetTitle]§! and harness this power, adding §G$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_HP_ADD$§! and §G$MOD_SHIP_SHIELD_REGEN_ADD_PERC$§! to all ships.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1295`

## paragon.5227.d.tt

### 中文

§L高密度的能量流经§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的躯体，让[Ruler.GetHerHis]血肉之躯的基础特征发生了改变和扭曲。每当[Ruler.GetHerHisCap]锐利的目光落在仪式的随从身上，很明显，其脑海中的所思所想立刻就不再只属于自己了。§!\n\n§Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!能够学着驾驭该能力，为整个帝国提升§G$MOD_PLANET_CRIME_MULT$降低§!和§G加密§!。

Whenever [Ruler.GetHerHisCap] piercing gaze lands on a ritual attendant, it's clear that their thoughts are no longer private.§!\n\n§Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! could learn to harness this power, adding §G$MOD_PLANET_CRIME_MULT$ Reduction§! and §GEncryption§! for the empire.

To our volunteers, [Ruler.GetHerHis] voice is as agonizing as it is compelling.§!\n\n§Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! could learn to harness this power, adding §G$tile_resource_unity_add$§! and §G$MOD_POP_GOVERNMENT_ETHIC_ATTRACTION$§! to the empire.

能量溢流

## paragon.5230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这次考验取得了巨大的成功。得益于§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!与生俱来的韧性以及我们非凡的灵能潜能，我们成功将其复活！但我们还需要几年时间才能把帝国交还给这位合法的统治者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1164`

### English

The ordeal proved to be a great success. Thanks to their innate resilience and our exceptional psionic potential, §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has been returned to life! But it will take a few years before we can hand the nation back to its rightful ruler.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1305`

## paragon.5236.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5236.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行星之心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1165`

### English

Planetary Heart

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1306`

## paragon.5236.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5236.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们取出了行星的核心，处理后包裹在了一个框架中，该框架利用暗物质产生了相对其尺寸而言巨大的能量。现在，我们可以制造出特殊的义体来增强§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!，也许还可以修复它的身体和思想，而只需要提供更多的§Y暗物质§!保持设备正常运行。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1166`

### English

We extracted the planetary core, processed it, and encased it inside a frame that uses Dark Matter to generate an enormous amount of power, given its compact size. Now, we can produce exceptional cybernetics to enhance §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! and perhaps fix its body and mind for a meager cost of supplying further §YDark Matter§! to keep the device operational. 

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1307`

## paragon.5237.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5237.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$leader_trait_legendary_leader$之心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1167`

### English

Heart of the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1308`

## paragon.5237.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5237.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

升级维护费：§R每月[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension]暗物质§!\n所选义体增强：§G[ROOT.Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\n这座工厂正是为了此目的而修建。很快这里就能制造出我国有史以来最先进的义体，为我们敬爱的§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!提供动力。我们决定安装的升级越多，我们每个月就需要补充越多的暗物质来保持心脏的运作。但我们应该注意不要过度使用，否则身体可能无法承受这些升级的副作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1168`

### English

Upgrades upkeep: §R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension] Dark Matter monthly§!\nChosen cybernetic enhancements: §G[ROOT.Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\nA factory has been built explicitly for this purpose. Soon it will begin producing the most advanced cybernetics ever seen, all to power our beloved §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!. The more upgrades we install, the more Dark Matter we will need to supplement each month to keep the heart operational. But we should be careful not to overdo it, or the body may perish under the incredible strain.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1309`

## paragon.5237.a

## paragon.5240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的操作成功了！所有增强义体的新能源都高效稳定地运转中，使§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!能够再一次出现在在忠诚的人民面前。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1182`

### English

Our operation was successful! The new power source for all the cybernetic enhancements has proven highly efficient and stable, enabling §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! to walk again among the people.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1323`

## paragon.5246.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5246.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地建造了一个合适的框架以及艺术品级的正电子脑来容纳§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的意识。现在，还需要完成的就是扫描其大脑，并完成新的上传和重编程工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1184`

### English

We managed to build a proper frame as well as a state-of-the-art positronic brain to host the consciousness of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!. Now, what's left is to scan its brain and proceed with the new upload and reprogramming.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1325`

## paragon.5247.name

意识编程

### English

## paragon.5247.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5247.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

升级维护费：§R每月[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension]活体金属§!\n已选程序升级：§G[ROOT.Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\n工厂里几乎没有活生生的操作人员，只有几个有知觉的平台维护着这台精巧的机器。§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的意识已被上传到网络中，随时准备接受必要的升级。我们必须小心，重编程不能过多，否则会极大提升所需的计算能力，以至于我们所创建的最先进的单元也无法承受。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1186`

### English

Upgrades upkeep: §R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension] Living Metal monthly§!\nChosen program upgrades: §G[ROOT.Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\nThe factory is practically devoid of living workers, but a few sentient platforms maintain the delicate machinery. §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s consciousness is currently stored on a network, ready to receive the necessary upgrades. We must be careful not to overdo the reprogramming, or the required computing power will become too much, even for our most advanced units.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1327`

§L通过引入全帝国共享的学习网络，我们让其他领袖也可以从§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的广博知识中受益。§!\n\n这会增强其他领袖的学习能力，但也会提升§G$MOD_LEADER_SPECIES_EXP_GAIN$§!和§G领袖维护费§!。

§L恰当的战斗计算需要消耗巨额的计算能力，但为§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!安装了正电子脑后，我军舰队可以时刻做好战斗准备§!\n\n这将增强我们的全体舰队，为所有舰船提升§G$MOD_SHIP_FIRE_RATE_MULT$§!和§G$MOD_SHIP_WEAPON_DAMAGE$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1192`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5247.d.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L为§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!安装一个特制的回收算法能够使其站在可持续生产的角度思考，进而减少潜在的浪费。§!\n\n这将减少我们对区划及建筑的资源投入，降低§G$mod_planet_districts_upkeep_mult$§!和§G$mod_planet_buildings_upkeep_mult$§!。

## paragon.5256.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5256.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学团队已经确定了几位携带可行基因编码的候选人，可以用于强化§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!。一旦疗程结束，§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!将不再需要担心衰老造成的失态。未来我们应该还可以对基因进行进一步修饰。当然，升级统治者的DNA序列需要新鲜的基因材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1202`

### English

Our science team has identified several candidates with the viable genetic code to enhance §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!. Once the procedure is complete, §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! will no longer be burdened by the indignity of aging. Further modifications should be possible in the future. Of course, fresh genetic material will be needed to upgrade the ruler's DNA sequence.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1343`

## paragon.5257.name

### 中文

基因操控

### English

Gene Manipulation

## paragon.5257.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5257.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

初始牺牲：§R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension]基因兼容个体§!\n每十年需要牺牲：§R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension]公民§!\n已选基因能力：§G[Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\n特殊的基因重组罐已经准备好，我们最好的科学家正在监管这项手术。\n\n匹配的领袖将会被溶解并取出基因物质。然后，他们的特定DNA会被用于注入§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!。我们可以使用更多匹配的个体来增强这一过程，产生可控的变异，使得§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!更快地重返青春，但我们必须小心不要做过头。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1204`

### English

Initial sacrifice: §R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension] Gene Compatible Individuals§!\nDecennial sacrifices needed: §R[ROOT.paragon_origin_ascension] Citizens§!\nChosen genetic powers: §G[Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Focus][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait1][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait2][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait3][Paragon_Origin_Ascension_Trait4]§!\n\nSpecial gene-resequencing tanks have been prepared and our best minds are overseeing the operation.\n\nCompatible leaders will be dissolved to retrieve their genetic material. Then, certain sequences of their DNA will be infused with §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!. We can use more compatible individuals to enhance this process, producing controlled mutations to allow §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! to rejuvenate even faster, but we must be careful not to overdo it.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1345`

### 中文

§L我们可以逆转衰老过程。给§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!提供更年轻的大脑将增强此人的认知能力。拥有经验的智慧和年轻的敏捷，一切皆有可能。§!\n\n获得随机§G统治者特质§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1206`

### English

§LWe can reverse the aging process. A younger brain for §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! will enhance their cognitive capabilities. With the wisdom of experience and the agility of youth, anything is possible.§!\n\nGrants random §GRuler Trait§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1347`

## paragon.5257.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5257.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§L就大脑的认知中心而言，通常来说就是越大越好。我们可以进一步刺激§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的大脑生长。§!\n\n通过提高集中注意力和理性思考能力，§Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!可以帮助我们的科学家在任务中研究未知，提升我们的§G$MOD_LEADER_SPECIES_EXP_GAIN$§!和§G$MOD_COUNTRY_ALL_TECH_RESEARCH_SPEED$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1208`

### English

§LWhen it comes to the cognitive center of the brain, bigger is usually better. We can stimulate further growth in §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!.§!\n\nWith increased powers of concentration and reasoning, §Y[Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! can help our scientists on their quest to study the unknown, increasing our §G$MOD_LEADER_SPECIES_EXP_GAIN$§! and §G$MOD_COUNTRY_ALL_TECH_RESEARCH_SPEED$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1349`

### 中文

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的实验已经被证明是卓有成效的，一个完美版§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!被塑造出来了。现在，随着[Ruler.GetTitle]回到了[Ruler.GetHerHis]忠实追随者面前，我们可以重新开始星海征途了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1218`

### English

Our experiment has proven fruitful, and the perfect form of §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has been achieved. Now, with our [Ruler.GetTitle] back among [Ruler.GetHerHis] loyal people, the stars are ours to claim!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1359`

### 中文

通过一定的牺牲，我们成功地让§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!领导国家的能力提升到了无可估量的程度。问题是：对一位好的领袖而言什么品质是最重要的？新的增长点应该被导向到何处？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1220`

### English

With our sacrifices, we managed to push §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]'s§! capabilities to lead a nation beyond measure. The question is: what qualities are the most important for a good leader? Where should this new growth be channeled?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1362`

## paragon.5270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\n经历了濒死的边缘，我们的光耀者发表了一篇充满希望和感激的演讲。在感谢公民和科学家使这项非凡壮举成为可能后，[Ruler.GetName]谈到了未来：[Ruler.GetSheHe]比以往更加强大，准备重新掌舵我们辉煌的国家。团结一致，我们战胜了叛徒和叛乱分子，也将一起为每颗星球上的每个公民带来荣耀和繁荣！荣耀归于[Owner.GetName]！荣耀归于[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！\n\n同时也向光耀者致敬。\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1236`

### English

$paragon.beginning.speech$\n\nReturned from the brink of death, our Luminary's speech is a hopeful and grateful one. After giving thanks to citizens and scientists for enabling this extraordinary feat, [Ruler.GetName] speaks of the future: that [Ruler.GetSheHe] is stronger than ever, ready to retake the helm of our glorious nation. Together, we prevailed over traitors and rebels. And together, we will bring glory and prosperity to every citizen, on every world! Glory to [Owner.GetName]! Glory to [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]!\n\nAnd glory to the Luminary.\n\n$paragon.ending.speech$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1379`

## paragon.5305.name

### 中文

环境改造洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1241`

### English

Terraform Insight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1385`

## paragon.5305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些年来，得益于天赋异禀的科学家们，我们创造了无与伦比的繁荣和稳定。为了寻找新的途径殖民并发展星球，他们谋划了一些目前被认为是不可能做到的事情——基于目前在异种生物学领域的理解：他们将我们本土的动植物植入到外星环境，并通过大规模的地质改造来改变这颗星球，使其变得宜居。\n\n尽管我们现在不太可能复制这一成功，毕竟这颗被改造的星球有一定特殊性，但这表明，随着时间的推移，我们可以逐渐改进这一方法，使其能够通用于所有星球。\n\n到了那个时候，我们就可以换一个领域拨款促进其发展了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1242`

### English

Recent years have boasted unparalleled prosperity and stability, owing to our most talented scientists. Seeking new worlds to grow and exploit, they managed something that was - given our current understanding of xenobiology - believed impossible: they managed to implant our native flora and fauna into an alien planet, changing it to suit our population, through the use of enormous earthworks.\n\nAlthough replicating this success now is unlikely, as the changed planet was rather unique, it has shown that in time, we could enhance and adapt this process to suit other worlds.\n\nUntil then, we can allocate funds to grow the empire in other areas.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1386`

### English

## paragon.5310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的海军是国家防卫的堡垒。§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!宣布了将建造一艘崭新的船只，由国家出资，以增强我们的舰队。这艘旗舰将引领我们进入辉煌的时代，粉碎任何阻挡我们前进的敌人！很快，这艘船就会下水，届时我们将举行欢庆活动。\n\n现在，我们可以调配资金，在其他领域推动帝国的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1244`

### English

Our navy is the bastion of defense. The §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§! has announced a brand new state-funded ship to bolster our fleet. This flagship will lead us into a glorious era, crushing anyone who stands in our way! Soon the vessel will be launched, and we will celebrate.\n\nNow, we can allocate funds to grow the empire in other areas.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1388`

### English

## paragon.5315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

科技突破

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1246`

### English

Scientific Breakthrough

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1390`

## paragon.5315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学是创造更美好、更光明未来的关键。在得到新的激励之后，我们的科学家正在创造比以往任何时候都要多的突破。这些进展已被§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!看在眼里，他向一些杰出的科学家颁发了功勋勋章。\n\n我们现在可以将扩张的努力集中在不同的领域上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1247`

### English

Science is the key to a better and brighter future. Freshly incentivized, our scientists are making more breakthroughs than ever. The progress has been acknowledged by §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§! with medals of merit awarded to select scientists.\n\nWe can now afford to focus our expansion efforts on different disciplines.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1391`

## paragon.5320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

言传身教

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1248`

### English

Lead by Example

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1392`

## paragon.5320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!是所有人的榜样，引领我们穿越星空。在这样一个有强有力的领袖领导的时代，我们的官员们为能够为国家服务而感到自豪，并且追求卓越。\n\n我们现在可以将扩张的努力集中在不同的领域上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1249`

### English

The §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§! is a role model for all, guiding us among the stars. With a strong leader at the helm, our officials are proud to serve, and strive for excellence.\n\nWe can now afford to focus our expansion efforts on different disciplines.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1393`

## paragon.5325.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5325.name&lang=en)

### 中文

实验性空间站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1250`

### English

Experimental Starbase

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1394`

## paragon.5325.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5325.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有关新建设项目的报告对公众信心产生了深刻的影响。完成了生产成本论证之后，我们期待着这一令人难以置信的工程壮举的实现。\n\n我们现在可以将扩张的努力集中在不同的领域上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1251`

### English

Reports on the new construction project are having a profound effect on public morale. With the production costs behind us, we look forward to the realization of this incredible feat of engineering.\n\nWe can now afford to focus our expansion efforts on different disciplines.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1395`

## paragon.5330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

活体金属

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1252`

### English

Living Metal

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1396`

## paragon.5330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们通过技术和科学的进步获得了许多发现，但我们仍有很多东西要学习。我们的勘探队发现了一种非同寻常的物质，同时具有液态和金属特性。此外，这种物质似乎还是有生命的。我们还不知道如何理解或利用这种材料，但它引起了广泛关注。\n\n在我们更加深入地了解这种物质之前，我们应该在不同的学科方向集中精力进一步有所拓展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1253`

### English

We have discovered much through technological and scientific advancement, but we still have a lot to learn. Our exploration team found an extraordinary substance which exhibits liquid and metallic properties. Furthermore, this substance appears to be alive. We do not yet know how to understand or utilize this material, but it is most intriguing.\n\nUntil we know more, we would do well to focus our expansion efforts on different disciplines.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1397`

## paragon.5335.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5335.name&lang=en)

### 中文

力量之源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1254`

### English

Power Source

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1398`

## paragon.5335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们的技术进步已经走得很远，但宇宙仍然隐藏着许多秘密。我们的勘探队发现了一种非常稠密的物质，被认为是某种形式的暗物质。目前，我们还无法确定这种新材料的潜在用途。\n\n在我们找到方法之前，我们应该在不同的学科方向集中精力进一步有所拓展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1255`

### English

Our technological advancements have come far, but the universe holds many secrets. Our exploration team has discovered an extraordinary, dense substance. It is believed to be some form of Dark Matter. For now, we are unable to discern a potential use for this new material.\n\nUntil we find a way, we would do well to focus our expansion efforts on different disciplines.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1399`

## paragon.5340.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5340.name&lang=en)

### 中文

杰出统治

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1256`

### English

Exceptional Rule

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1400`

## paragon.5340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

第一艘航天飞机的周年纪念日让人们在共同的目标下团结起来。受到§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!演讲的启发和鼓舞，[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]全体人民正在共同努力，使他们的新星系首都成为冰冷太空中的一座繁荣灯塔。\n\n现在，我们可以将我们的努力聚焦其他方面了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1257`

### English

The anniversary of the first space shuttle has united the population in common purpose. Inspired and invigorated by §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!' speech, the [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] population are working together to make their new galactic capital a beacon of prosperity in the cold void of space.\n\nNow, we can focus our efforts elsewhere.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1401`

### English

Capital World Ascension Tier increased by §G+1§!

## paragon.5345.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5345.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发明天才

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1259`

### English

Inventor Genius

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1403`

## paragon.5345.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5345.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有关一种新型机器的消息正在传播，这种机器能够从回收的材料中提炼大量的合金。这个设备非常高效，但是它的发明者在向我们的§Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!展示之后不久去世了。其他工程师认为不可能对其进行复制，出于对民众利益的考量，我们将机器放入一个受保护的设施中。\n\n我们现在可以调配资金，在其他领域进行发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1260`

### English

News has been spreading about an innovative machine which is capable of refining huge quantities of alloys from recycled materials. The device is highly efficient, but its creator died shortly after presenting it to our §Y[Root.Owner.Ruler.GetTitle]§!. Deemed impossible to replicate by other engineers, we put the machine into a guarded facility for protection, and for the benefit of our people.\n\nWe can allocate funds to develop in other areas.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1404`

## paragon.5360.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5360.name&lang=en)

### 中文

勘探洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1261`

### English

Prospecting Insight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1405`

## paragon.5360.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5360.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的勘探者再次证明了自己的价值。他们报告称发现了一个隐蔽的地下矿藏，里面有几种不同寻常的资源。一方面我们可以继续开展工作，确认这些新资源的性质和潜在储量；另一方面，我们可以换一个领域拨款促进其发展了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1262`

### English

Our prospectors proved themselves useful once again. They reported finding a hidden underground deposit of several unusual resources. While we work to ascertain the properties and potential for these new assets, we can continue to expand in other areas.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1406`

## paragon.5370.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5370.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星环基地洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1263`

### English

Orbital Rings Insight

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1407`

## paragon.5370.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5370.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在深空殖民和运输领域进行的广泛研究产生了许多科研成果。我们最聪明的科学家们与最厉害的工程师们密切合作。他们发展出了一个新的概念：轨道环；这是一个能够支持工业或民用设施的巨大环形空间结构，覆盖整个行星。尽管现在我们可能缺乏建造这样一个星环基地所需的工业能力，但前方的道路是清晰的。同时，我们可以将扩张的努力集中在其他地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1264`

### English

Extensive research in the field of deep space colonization and transportation proved fruitful. Our brightest scientists have worked closely with the best engineers. They have developed a new concept for an orbital ring; a space structure encompassing the planet with a giant ring capable of supporting industrial or civilian facilities. For now, we may lack the industrial capability to build such an Orbital Ring, but the path ahead is clear. Meanwhile, we can afford to focus expansion efforts elsewhere.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1408`

## paragon.5900.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5900.name&lang=en)

### 中文

启明之光消逝！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1265`

### English

Luminary Gone!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1409`

## paragon.5900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

没有谁可以永远活着。最终，我们在死亡面前都是平等的。今天，$leader_trait_legendary_leader$最后一次将我们所有人团结在一起。我们从未举行过如此盛大的葬礼：激情昂扬的演讲，高尚的誓言，无尽的送行队伍。在我们领土全境，人们因$leader_trait_legendary_leader$为我们所做的一切表示感激。没有统治者是完美的，但我们的统治者不知疲倦地工作，为我们所有人建设一个更美好的未来。\n\n虽然我们希望减轻这样一个事件可能引发的余波，但我们的母星还是陷入了动荡之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1266`

### English

No one lives forever. In the end, we are all equals in death. Today, the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ unites us all one last in time. Never before have we held such a grand funeral: powerful speeches, noble vows, endless processions. All across our territories, the people are expressing their thanks for everything the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ did for us. No ruler is perfect, but ours worked tirelessly to build a better future for us all.\n\nAlthough we hoped to mitigate the potential backlash of such an event, our homeworld has fallen into turmoil.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1410`

## paragon.5900.desc.50years

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5900.desc.50years&lang=en)

### 中文

没有人可以永生。最终，我们都在死亡面前平等。今天，$leader_trait_legendary_leader$最后一次将我们聚集到一起。我们从未举行过如此盛大的葬礼：激情昂扬的演讲，高贵的誓言，无尽的送行队伍。在我们境内，人们表达了对$leader_trait_legendary_leader$为我们所做的一切的感激之情。没有统治者是完美的，但我们的领袖不知疲倦地工作，为我们所有人建立更美好的未来。\n\n尽管我们做了应对危机的准备，$leader_trait_legendary_leader$的去世仍然让我们陷入挣扎，因悲伤而痛苦。克服这一损失需要时间，但这也会让我们变得更加坚强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1267`

### English

No one lives forever. In the end, we are all equals in death. Today, the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ unites us all one last in time. Never before have we held such a grand funeral: powerful speeches, noble vows, endless processions. All across our territories, the people are expressing their thanks for everything the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ did for us. No ruler is perfect, but ours worked tirelessly to build a better future for us all.\n\nDespite preparations made to deal with crises, the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$'s demise has us struggling, saddened by grief. It will take time to overcome this loss, but from it we will grow stronger.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1411`

## paragon.5900.desc.100years

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5900.desc.100years&lang=en)

### 中文

没有人可以永生。最终，我们都在死亡面前平等。今天，$leader_trait_legendary_leader$最后一次将我们聚集到一起。我们从未举行过如此盛大的葬礼：激情昂扬的演讲，高贵的誓言，无尽的送行队伍。在我们境内，人们表达了对$leader_trait_legendary_leader$为我们所做的一切的感激之情。没有统治者是完美的，但我们的领袖不知疲倦地工作，为我们所有人建立更美好的未来。\n\n由于我们已经做好应对这样的危机的准备，即使在$leader_trait_legendary_leader$去世之后，我们也将稳步前行。克服这一损失需要一些时间，但从中我们也会变得更加坚强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1268`

### English

No one lives forever. In the end, we are all equals in death. Today, the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ unites us all one last in time. Never before have we held such a grand funeral: powerful speeches, noble vows, endless processions. All across our territories, the people are expressing their thanks for everything the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ did for us. No ruler is perfect, but ours worked tirelessly to build a better future for us all.\n\nOwing to preparations made to deal with such a crisis, we will land on our feet even after the $leader_trait_legendary_leader$'s demise. It will take some time to overcome this loss, but from it we will grow stronger.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1412`

## paragon.5900.a

### English

## paragon.6000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的健康正在快速流失，为此我们最好的医疗专家提出了一种非常规的方案。我们会直接在内阁会议厅中建造一座精心设计的装置。让这台机器接管[Ruler.GetHerHis]的大部分生理流程，我们就能让[Ruler.GetHimHer]无限期地存活下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1274`

### English

With §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]'s§! health quickly failing, our best medical minds have devised an unorthodox solution. An elaborate apparatus will be built directly inside of the council chambers. By granting this machine control over many of [Ruler.GetHerHis] natural processes, we can keep [Ruler.GetHimHer] alive indefinitely.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1419`

## paragon.6000.desc.ap_mind_over_matter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6000.desc.ap_mind_over_matter&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.6000.desc$\n\n我们的[GetPsychicNounPlural]也提出了一个方案。通过引导我们人民大众的[GetSorrow]可以操纵虚境的无上能量[GetRejuvenate]我们逝去的领袖。只需要付出些许代价，§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!就能无限期地活下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1275`

### English

$paragon.6000.desc$\n\nOur [GetPsychicNounPlural] have also devised a plan. By harnessing the collective [GetSorrow] of our people, the myriad energies of the Shroud could be manipulated  to [GetRejuvenate] our fallen leader. For a price, §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! can be kept alive indefinitely.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1420`

## paragon.6000.desc.ap_the_flesh_is_weak

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6000.desc.ap_the_flesh_is_weak&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.6000.desc$\n\n我们的义体控制论专家提出了一个绝妙的方案……只需要用最先进的植入体，我们可以将统治者体内每一个衰竭的生物组织都替换掉，§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!就能万寿无疆了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1276`

### English

$paragon.6000.desc$\n\nOur cyberneticists have devised a brilliant plan... Using only the most cutting-edge enhancements, we can replace each faulty biological piece of the ruler's body. Once complete, §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! will rule for eternity.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1421`

## paragon.6000.desc.ap_synthetic_evolution

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6000.desc.ap_synthetic_evolution&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的机器人专家提出了一个修复§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的方案：尽管花费颇高，但我们可以将其意识转移入一个新的自修复正电子神经网络中。能够与所有人建立联系，使我们能够让统治者的能力更上一层楼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1277`

### English

Our roboticists have a suggestion to repair §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!: while expensive, we can move their mind into a new self-correcting positronic network. Being able to network with the entire population allows us to improve their already considerable skills.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1422`

## paragon.6000.desc.ap_engineered_evolution

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6000.desc.ap_engineered_evolution&lang=en)

### 中文

\"$paragon.6000.desc$\n\n再或者，我们的基因专家提出了另一个方案。尽管花费颇高，但可以用无限的克隆体来承载§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!，使[Ruler.GetHimHer]最终实现永生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1278`

### English

$paragon.6000.desc$\n\nAlternatively, our geneticists have devised an alternative. Unlimited (though expensive) clone bodies could be made to house §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!, effectively making [Ruler.GetHimHer] immortal.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1423`

## paragon.6005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国生机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1295`

### English

The Living Empire

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1440`

## paragon.6005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

整个流畅冗长而艰巨。对§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!而言也相比无比痛苦。但结果不言而喻：安置进那台装置后，统治者的怪病消失得无影无踪。\n\n虽然对他的痊愈心存感激，但[Ruler.GetHimHer]身边的人注意到，§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的行为举止和情绪都发生了微妙的变化。\n\n现在，我们的领袖必须待在宫殿里，虽然如果首都被我们的敌人占领，我们仍准备有一个转移装置的计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1296`

### English

The procedure was long and arduous. For §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! it must have been excruciating. But the results speak for themselves: installed in the apparatus, no trace remains of the ruler's mysterious illness.\n\nWhile grateful to be cured, those close to [Ruler.GetHimHer] have noted §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s mannerisms and mood have subtly changed.\n\nFor now, our leader must remain in the palace, though a plan exists to move the apparatus should the capital be overrun by our enemies.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1441`

## paragon.6005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

恭迎归位，[Ruler.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1297`

### English

Welcome back, [Ruler.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1442`

## paragon.6010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

升级完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1298`

### English

Upgrades Complete

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1443`

## paragon.6010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工作成功了！§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!被救回来了。\n\n也许因为[Ruler.GetHisHer]的很大一部分躯体被这个流程影响，[Ruler.GetHisHer]对义体科技的看法发生了改变。过去这项技术仅仅是一种工具，但[Ruler.GetSheHe]现在将其视为通向完美的终极道路。\n\n我们的领袖回来了。但会不会有什么地方不同还不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1299`

### English

Our work was a success! §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! is saved.\n\nPerhaps because such a significant portion of [Ruler.GetHisHer] body was affected by the procedure, [Ruler.GetHisHer] views on cybernetics have shifted. Where before the technology was merely a tool, [Ruler.GetSheHe] now views it as the ultimate path to perfection.\n\nOur leader is returned to us. But how changed remains an open question.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1444`

## paragon.6010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[Ruler.GetName]的无尽的升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1300`

### English

Endless upgrades for [Ruler.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1445`

## paragon.6015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

注入灵能

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1301`

### English

Psionic Infusion

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1446`

## paragon.6015.desc_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6015.desc_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们大功告成了。借助§H虚境§!的力量，§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!得以幸存。前所未有的强大灵能在其身上流淌而过，[Ruler.GetHeShe]获得了永生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1302`

### English

Our work was a success. By invoking the power of the §HShroud§!, §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has been saved. With unprecedented levels of psionic energies coursing through them, [Ruler.GetHeShe] has achieved immortality.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1447`

## paragon.6015.desc_2.organic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6015.desc_2.organic&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，这场磨难让[Ruler.GetHimHer]微妙地起了变化……[Ruler.GetHimHer]身边的人窃窃私语着，似乎看到了其体内有两个截然不同的灵魂。似乎虚境的能量以比最初预期的更严峻、更具侵蚀性的代价维持着光耀者的生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1303`

### English

However, the ordeal has left [Ruler.GetHimHer] subtly changed... Those close to [Ruler.GetHimHer] whisper there are two distinct personalities at work within. It appears the Shroud's energies are keeping the Luminary alive at a steeper, more corrosive cost than initially expected.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1448`

## paragon.6015.desc_2.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6015.desc_2.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，这场磨难让[Ruler.GetHimHer]的参数变化超出我们的预期。对[Ruler.GetHimHer]的诊断显示有互相冲突的逻辑运算函数同时在子系统中运作。似乎虚境的能量救了光耀者一命，但比起先的预期对其系统更具有侵蚀性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1304`

### English

However, the ordeal has left [Ruler.GetHimHer] altered beyond our expected parameters. Diagnostics of [Ruler.GetHimHer] show conflicting logistical processor functions at work within their subsystems. It appears the Shroud's energies are keeping the Luminary active, but with a more corrosive cost on their systems than initially predicted.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1449`

## paragon.6015.desc_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6015.desc_3&lang=en)

### 中文

但这一切都可以等一等。目前，更紧迫的问题是保持泽珞的持续供应；如果供应中断，我们的领袖就会灭亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1305`

### English

But all this can wait. For now, the more pressing concern is to maintain a constant supply of Zro; should we ever run out, our leader will perish.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1450`

## paragon.6015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再次唤醒，[Ruler.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1306`

### English

Reawaken, [Ruler.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1451`

## paragon.6020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

合成拓展

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1307`

### English

Synthetic Expansion

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1452`

## paragon.6020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成功了！§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!的意识被转移到了一个新的合成框架中。量子处理器使[Ruler.GetHimHer]可以即时与任一个合成人个体连接。\n\n这一强大的超意识开始改变我们领袖的观点。现在[Ruler.GetSheHe]专注于宏观处理模式，旨在提高我们文明的整体效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1308`

### English

Success! §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!'s consciousness has been transferred to a synthetic frame. Quantum processing allows [Ruler.GetHimHer] instantaneous networking with every synthetic unit.\n\nThis powerful meta-awareness has begun to change our leader's point of view. Now, [Ruler.GetSheHe] are focused on macro processing patterns, aiming to improve the overall efficiency of our civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1453`

## paragon.6020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[Ruler.GetName]已全面上线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1309`

### English

[Ruler.GetName] is fully online.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1454`

## paragon.6025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

更完美之躯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1310`

### English

A More Perfect Body

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1455`

## paragon.6025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

克隆流程和手术已经顺利完成。我们的领袖被救活了。\n\n然而，许多人表达了对[Ruler.GetHisHer]态度不可预料变化的担忧。§Y[Ruler.GetTitle][Ruler.GetRegnalName]§!迷恋于新的躯体，[Ruler.GetHisHer]产生了对我们人民下一个进化阶段的执念。[Ruler.GetSheHe]动用了相当多的资源来完善大众的基因图谱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1311`

### English

The cloning process and operation were a complete success. Our leader is saved.\n\nHowever, many have expressed concern about some unforeseen changes in [Ruler.GetHisHer] attitude. Enamored with [Ruler.GetHisHer] new form, §Y[Ruler.GetTitle] [Ruler.GetRegnalName]§! has grown obsessed with the next stage of our people's evolution. [Ruler.GetSheHe] has diverted considerable resources to perfect the genetic profile of the masses.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1456`

## paragon.6025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[Ruler.GetName]现在已经是一个完美的基因原体了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1312`

### English

[Ruler.GetName] is now a perfect genetic specimen.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1457`

## paragon.6035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新时代

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1319`

### English

A New Age

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1465`

## paragon.6035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在手术康复不久后，一场盛大的庆祝活动在[capital_scope.GetName]举行。$leader_trait_legendary_leader$发表了一场演讲，感谢公民和科学家，而[Ruler.GetName]则谈到了他们为§Y[Owner.GetName]§!规划的美好、繁荣的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1320`

### English

Not long after recovering from the procedure, a grand celebration was held on [capital_scope.GetName]. $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ gave a speech thanking citizens and scientists, and [Ruler.GetName] spoke of the bright and prosperous future they have planned for the §Y[Owner.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1466`

## paragon.6035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

生命无止尽的$leader_trait_legendary_leader$应该领导我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1321`

### English

The timeless $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ shall lead us.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1467`

## paragon.6035.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6035.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会为了人民对政府进行改革。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1322`

### English

We will reform the government for the people.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1468`

## paragon.6035.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.6035.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

政府会进行改革。$leader_trait_legendary_leader$将继续支持和引导我们，但现在人民有机会选择自己的领袖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1323`

### English

The government will be reformed. The $leader_trait_legendary_leader$ will continue to support and guide us, but now the people will have the chance to choose their leader.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1469`

## leader_trait_the_apparatus_luminary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_apparatus_luminary&lang=en)

### 中文

装置徒刑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1325`

### English

Apparatus Bound

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1472`

## leader_trait_the_apparatus_luminary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_apparatus_luminary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不朽，但代价是什么呢？这位领袖被囚禁在了内阁会议厅里一台笨重的生命维持装置上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1326`

### English

Immortality, but at what cost? This leader is bound to a cumbersome life-sustaining apparatus in the council chambers.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1473`

## leader_trait_the_cybernetic_luminary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_cybernetic_luminary&lang=en)

### 中文

无尽升级

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1327`

### English

Endless Upgrades

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1474`

## leader_trait_the_cybernetic_luminary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_cybernetic_luminary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

永生是可以实现的。由于几次高级升级，这位领袖可以直接与每个高级义体官员同步。然而，这确实限制了他们独立学习的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1328`

### English

Immortality is achievable. Due to several advanced upgrades, this leader can sync directly with every high-level cybernetic administrator. However, this does limit their ability to learn independently.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1475`

## leader_trait_the_psionic_luminary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_psionic_luminary&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境借道者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1329`

### English

The Passenger

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1476`

## leader_trait_the_psionic_luminary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_psionic_luminary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不朽的代价非常高昂……虚境实体在这位领袖的意识中留下了后门。这位实体一脚已经踏入了我们的现实世界，而这位领袖这需要持续的泽珞供应以维生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1330`

### English

The price of immortality is high... The Shroud Entity left a doorway in the mind of this leader. The Entity has gained a foothold in our realm, and the leader must be supplied with a constant supply of Zro.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1477`

## leader_trait_the_synth_luminary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_synth_luminary&lang=en)

### 中文

合成觉醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1331`

### English

Synthetic Awareness

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1478`

## leader_trait_the_synth_luminary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_synth_luminary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖的合成躯体就是机器人专家精湛技艺的浓缩。量子处理器使其可以直接连接到我们人民的超连续体中。尽管能提升效率，但那些被调整的人来说效果并不好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1332`

### English

This leader's synthetic body is the epitome of the roboticist's art. Quantum processing grants a direct connection with the meta-continuousness of the people. While boosting efficiency, the effects do not bode well for those altered.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1479`

## leader_trait_the_genetic_luminary

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_genetic_luminary&lang=en)

### 中文

基因完美

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1333`

### English

Genetic Perfection

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1480`

## leader_trait_the_genetic_luminary_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_the_genetic_luminary_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无尽的克隆使这位领袖获得了永生。只要现在的皮囊损坏或老化，就将有十几个新的皮囊取而代之。这导致了对基因完美的痴迷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:1334`

### English

An endless series of clones has granted this leader immortality. Should the current sleeve become damaged or obsolete, a dozen more are ready to take its place. This has led to an obsession with genetic perfection.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_3_l_english.yml:1481`
